### [1] [RAMS: Resource-Adaptive and Detection-Conditioned Model Switching for Embedded Edge Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14716)
*Kushal Khemani,Evan Leri,George Xu,Amit Hod*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RAMS 是一个面向嵌入式硬件的轻量级运行时控制器，通过监控设备压力并动态切换三个常驻 YOLOv8 模型层级（NANO/SMALL/MEDIUM），在无模型重载延迟的情况下平衡推理延迟与检测质量。其核心创新包括两种检测条件切换策略（防止近期检测到弱势道路使用者后激进降级）以及无需真实标注的 VRU-Weighted Accuracy Score 离线评估指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式边缘设备上的目标检测面临推理延迟与检测质量之间的权衡，尤其在资源压力动态变化时。现有方法在模型切换时通常存在模型重载延迟，且缺乏有效的运行时自适应机制来根据检测内容（如弱势道路使用者）智能决策，同时缺少无需标注数据的离线策略评估手段。

Method: RAMS 采用如下方法：(1) 在设备上同时驻留 YOLOv8 三个层级（NANO 320px、SMALL 416px、MEDIUM 640px），实现零延迟模型切换；(2) 运行时控制器监控设备压力，从空闲行为中校准切换阈值；(3) 定义五种切换策略，包括两种检测条件策略——当近期窗口内检测到 VRU 时锁定或倾向更高精度层级；(4) 提出 VRU-Weighted Accuracy Score (SWAS) 作为无需真实标注的离线策略比较指标，并引入 oracle-bounded 变体以分离检测器循环偏差与真正的层级保留收益。

Result: 在 Raspberry Pi 5、x86 笔记本和 Jetson Orin（ONNX/TensorRT）上的实验表明：(1) 同一控制器方程在 37 倍延迟跨度上通用运行；(2) Jetson Orin TensorRT 重载下，safety2 策略实现 3.41ms 平均延迟，比固定 MEDIUM 推理快 5.6 倍，同时通过近似 NANO 运行配合 VRU 正窗口期选择性 SMALL/MEDIUM 锁定，保留其 74% 的代理精度；(3) 检测条件切换在重载下相比纯阈值策略，oracle 评分下 SWAS 提升 25.4%，检测器派生评分下提升 47.3%；(4) KITTI 实时评估显示各层级 VRU 召回率分别为 24.2%、41.2%、59.0%，表明反应式覆盖从根本上受限于基线检测器召回能力。

Conclusion: RAMS 证明了在嵌入式硬件上通过轻量级运行时控制器实现动态模型层级切换的可行性与有效性。检测条件切换策略能显著提升 VRU 相关的检测质量保持，但系统性能最终受限于各层级检测器的基线召回率。SWAS 指标为无需标注的策略评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Edge object detection on embedded hardware requires balancing inference latency and detection quality under changing resource pressure. We present RAMS, a lightweight runtime controller that monitors device pressure, calibrates switching thresholds from idle behavior, and dynamically selects among three resident YOLOv8 tiers (NANO/SMALL/MEDIUM at 320/416/640 px) without model-reload latency. RAMS defines five switching policies, including two detection-conditioned variants that prevent aggressive downgrades after recent vulnerable-road-user (VRU) detections. We further introduce the VRU-Weighted Accuracy Score (SWAS), a scalar metric for offline policy comparison without ground-truth annotations, together with an oracle-bounded variant that separates detector circularity from genuine tier-retention benefit. Across Raspberry Pi 5, x86 laptops, and Jetson Orin ONNX/TensorRT deployments, the same controller equations operate over a 37x latency range. On Jetson Orin TensorRT under heavy load, the safety2 policy achieves 3.41 ms mean latency, 5.6x faster than fixed-MEDIUM inference, while retaining 74% of its proxy accuracy through near-NANO operation with selective SMALL and MEDIUM locks during VRU-positive windows. Detection-conditioned switching improves SWAS by 25.4% under oracle scoring and 47.3% under detector-derived scoring relative to threshold-only policies under heavy load. Live KITTI evaluation reports per-tier VRU recall of 24.2%, 41.2%, and 59.0%, showing that reactive overrides are fundamentally limited by baseline detector recall.

</details>


### [2] [AI for Maritime Security: Comparative Evaluation of CNN and Vision Transformer Architectures for Maritime Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14720)
*Ismet Gocer,Zakirul Bhuiayn,Shakeel Ahmad,Raza Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究利用深度学习和视觉Transformer对海事图像进行船舶检测，在6,468张多天气图像上评估六种模型，ViT达到100%准确率且视频处理最快，验证了AI视觉在海上安防中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升海事安全，利用先进AI与计算机视觉技术在不同实时环境下实现海面船舶的智能检测，弥补传统监控手段在多变天气条件下的不足。

Method: 构建包含6,468张多天气（多云、雾天、雨天、晴天）海事图像数据集；评估六种深度学习架构：一个基础CNN、四个迁移学习模型（Xception、VGG16、MobileNetV2、EfficientNetV2L）及一个Vision Transformer（ViT）；通过准确率、I类/II类错误、模型大小和视频处理时间进行多维度对比。

Result: 模型性能因计算约束和部署条件而异。轻量级架构（MobileNetV2等）适合资源受限设备；ViT综合表现最优，达到100%准确率、最低错误率及最快视频处理时间。

Conclusion: AI驱动的计算机视觉系统在海事监控、边境防护与自主导航领域具有巨大潜力。ViT在该任务上全面领先，但实际部署需根据计算资源在轻量化与高性能模型间权衡。

Abstract: This study aims to enhance maritime security by using advanced Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Computer Vision (CV) techniques. For this purpose, it was designed and assessed intelligent object detection systems that can detect the presence of ships on the sea surface under different real-time environments. To achieve this goal, a maritime image dataset with 6,468 images was used, covering different weather conditions like cloudy, foggy, rainy, and sunny environments. Six deep learning architectures were evaluated, including a base Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) model, four transfer learning models (Xception, VGG16, MobileNetV2, and EfficientNetV2L), and a Vision Transformer (ViT) model. The models were compared using multiple performance indicators, including accuracy, Type I and Type II errors, model size, and video processing time. The results show that model performance varies depending on computational constraints and deployment conditions. While lightweight architectures are suitable for resource-limited devices, the ViT achieved the best overall performance, reaching 100% accuracy with the lowest error rates and the fastest video processing time. The findings highlight the potential of AI-driven computer vision systems for maritime surveillance, border protection, and autonomous navigation.

</details>


### [3] [Disagreement-Based Cross-Model Routing for Implicit Video Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14723)
*Durga Sandeep Saluru*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于模型间分歧的跨模型路由策略，用于隐式视频问答。当主模型（Gemini 3.1 Pro）三次采样结果不一致时，将问题路由至第二模型（Claude Opus 4.8），在ImplicitQA基准上取得显著提升，尤其体现在需要跨镜头推理的类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在ImplicitQA隐式视频问答基准上，前沿视频LLM已接近性能天花板，且传统自洽性策略（同模型多次采样多数投票）因模型在难题上的错误具有相关性而失效。需要一种无需标注、无需训练的纯推理阶段方法来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出基于分歧的跨模型路由（Disagreement-based Cross-Model Routing）：(1) 对主模型（Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview，原生视频模型）在temperature=0下三次采样，利用其视频处理管线固有的样本间方差；(2) 识别约20%三次采样结果不一致的问题子集；(3) 仅将该不一致子集路由至第二模型（Claude Opus 4.8），后者采用均匀采样帧结合自适应思考（adaptive thinking）进行推理。

Result: 在1001题验证集上，AvgAcc较主模型最佳单次采样提升+1.43。分类别增益集中在：Motion & Trajectory (+5.49)、Inferred Counting (+3.45)、Vertical Spatial Reasoning (+1.82)——均为最依赖跨镜头参照解析的类别。在CVPR 2026 ImplicitQA挑战赛172题留出测试集上，达到82.03 AvgAcc / 79.71 MacroAvgAcc，较主模型最佳单样本提升+1.81。

Conclusion: 模型间分歧可作为有效的路由信号：当单一模型对难题的错误系统性相关时，跨模型路由能引入互补推理能力。该方法无需训练、无需标签，纯推理阶段即可实现，且增益集中于最需要跨镜头时空推理的类别。

Abstract: We study multiple-choice video question answering on the ImplicitQA benchmark, where the correct answer is never explicitly shown but must be inferred from off-screen events, line-of-sight cues, causal structure, and cross-shot spatial layout. On this benchmark a single frontier video LLM already operates near its accuracy ceiling, and we observe that conventional self-consistency strategies -- majority voting across repeated samples of the same model -- can hurt rather than help, because the model's errors on hard questions are correlated. We propose disagreement-based cross-model routing, a pure inference-time procedure that requires no labels and no training. We triple-sample a native-video model (Gemini 3.1 Pro Preview) at temperature zero, exploit the genuine sample-to-sample variance of its video-processing pipeline to identify the roughly 20% subset of questions where the three samples disagree, and route only that subset to a second model from a different family (Claude Opus 4.8) that consumes uniformly sampled frames with adaptive thinking. On the 1001-question validation set with public ground truth -- our main evaluation -- the method improves AvgAcc by +1.43 over the best single sample of the primary model, with per-category gains concentrated on Motion & Trajectory (+5.49), Inferred Counting (+3.45), and Vertical Spatial Reasoning (+1.82) -- the categories most dependent on cross-shot reference resolution. The same pipeline applied to the held-out 172-question CVPR 2026 ImplicitQA challenge test set achieves 82.03 AvgAcc / 79.71 MacroAvgAcc (+1.81 over the best single sample of the primary model), confirming the validation result on an independent split.

</details>


### [4] [VigilFormer: Deformable Attention for Video Anomaly Detection with Causal Risk Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14724)
*Xinze Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VigilFormer 是一种统一的视频异常检测框架，结合可变形时空注意力与因果时间建模，在 UCF-Crime、ShanghaiTech 和 CUHK Avenue 上分别取得 87.83%、97.21% 和 89.74% 的 AUC，同时达到 41.5 FPS 的推理速度，在精度和速度上均优于现有弱监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监控视频异常检测方法难以同时兼顾检测精度与实时吞吐量——要么侧重更强的特征提取器，要么侧重更高效的架构，鲜有两者兼备的方案。此外，密集注意力机制计算代价高昂，静态帧中存在大量冗余计算，且缺乏帧级标签下的弱监督学习仍具挑战性。

Method: 提出 VigilFormer 统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 可变形时空编码器 (DSTE)，在帧间稀疏地关注信息丰富的位置，避免密集注意力的二次代价，同时捕获不规则运动模式；(2) 因果异常分类器 (CAC)，在片段级特征上施加膨胀因果卷积，并优化对比多示例学习目标，在无帧级标签条件下分离异常与正常表征；(3) 自适应置信度调度器 (ACS)，在推理时动态跳过低信息帧，减少静态场景下的冗余计算。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上评估：UCF-Crime AUC 87.83%，ShanghaiTech AUC 97.21%，CUHK Avenue AUC 89.74%，单 GPU 推理速度达 41.5 FPS，在精度和速度两个维度上均超越近期弱监督方法。

Conclusion: VigilFormer 通过将高效的可变形注意力、因果时间建模与自适应推理调度相结合，成功实现了监控视频异常检测中精度与实时性的统一，为实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection in surveillance settings must balance detection accuracy against real-time throughput, a tension that existing methods address either through stronger feature extractors or more efficient architectures, but rarely both. We present VigilFormer, a unified framework that combines deformable spatio-temporal attention with causal temporal modeling to detect anomalies in untrimmed surveillance video. The proposed Deformable Spatio-Temporal Encoder (DSTE) attends to a sparse set of informative locations across frames, avoiding the quadratic cost of dense attention while retaining the ability to capture irregular motion patterns. A Causal Anomaly Classifier (CAC) applies dilated causal convolutions over snippet-level features and optimizes a contrastive multiple-instance learning objective that separates anomalous and normal representations without frame-level labels. To meet deployment constraints, an Adaptive Confidence Scheduler (ACS) dynamically skips low-information frames at inference time, reducing redundant computation in static scenes. Evaluated on UCF-Crime, ShanghaiTech, and CUHK Avenue, VigilFormer achieves AUC scores of 87.83%, 97.21%, and 89.74% respectively, at 41.5 FPS on a single GPU, outperforming recent weakly-supervised methods in both accuracy and speed.

</details>


### [5] [Interpolation between Convolution and Attention via K-Nearest Neighbors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14725)
*Mingi Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出卷积最近邻（ConvNN）框架，证明卷积和自注意力可以统一在 k-近邻聚合框架下——两者分别通过空间邻近度和特征相似度选择邻居，处于连续谱系的两端，而非本质不同的运算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前计算机视觉中CNN和Transformer被视作根本不同的架构范式：前者依赖空间局部卷积，后者依赖全局自注意力。作者旨在打破这一二元对立，揭示两者在数学上的内在统一性，从而为系统性地探索局部与全局聚合之间的中间形态提供理论基础。

Method: 提出ConvNN统一框架，将卷积和自注意力形式化为邻居选择+加权聚合两个步骤的特例。具体地：(1) 将邻居选择限制在归一化空间坐标上可精确恢复标准卷积和深度可分离卷积；(2) 将空间邻近度替换为缩放点积相似度则精确恢复自注意力及其稀疏变体（如KVT-attention）；(3) 框架支持通过可配置的相似度函数、邻居选择策略、位置编码和聚合核，作为卷积和注意力层的即插即用替代方案。

Result: ConvNN在数学上严格证明了卷积与自注意力是同一框架下的特殊情形，二者存在于从局部（空间邻近）到全局（特征相似）的连续谱上，而非类别上不同的计算原语。

Conclusion: 卷积和自注意力并非本质上对立，而是可以通过k-近邻聚合框架统一理解。ConvNN框架不仅提供了这一理论洞察，还具备实际工程价值——作为即插即用的层替代方案，支持在局部和全局聚合之间进行连续谱的系统探索。

Abstract: The shift from Convolutional Neural Networks to Transformers has reshaped computer vision, yet these two architectural families are typically viewed as fundamentally distinct. Convolutional Neural Networks are defined by spatially local convolution operations, while Transformers rely on global self-attention. We argue that convolution and self-attention, despite their apparent differences, can be unified within a single k-nearest neighbor aggregation framework. The critical insight is that both operations are special cases of neighbor selection and weighted aggregation. Convolution selects neighbors by spatial proximity while self-attention selects by feature similarity, revealing that they lie on a continuous spectrum rather than representing categorically different computations.
  We introduce Convolutional Nearest Neighbors (ConvNN), a unified framework that formalizes this connection. ConvNN exactly recovers standard and depthwise convolution by restricting neighbor selection to normalized spatial coordinates, and exactly recovers self-attention and its sparse variants, including KVT-attention, by replacing spatial proximity with scaled dot-product similarity. Beyond these special cases, ConvNN serves as a drop-in replacement for both convolution and attention layers, enabling systematic exploration of the intermediate spectrum between local and global aggregation through configurable similarity functions, neighbor selection strategies, positional encodings, and aggregation kernels.

</details>


### [6] [FairGen: Preference-Aligned Diffusion for Demographically Equitable Medical Image Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14727)
*Zhimin Li,Ruichen Zhang,Zhen Tan,Howard J Aizenstein,Jingtong Hu,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FairGen是一个公平性感知的扩散框架，用于合成人口统计学平衡的医学图像，在保持病理相关特征的同时提升数据多样性，在皮肤科、放射学和神经影像三项任务中分别实现95.9%、80.0%和35.2%的公平性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据存在严重的人口统计学不平衡——医疗资源可及性差异和疾病患病率差异导致某些人口亚群数据稀缺，且同一疾病在不同人口群体中可能呈现不同的表型特征。传统AI模型在此类不平衡数据上训练会放大诊断偏差，加剧医疗不平等。因此需要一种能够生成人口统计学平衡的医学图像、同时不牺牲病理特征保真度的数据增强方法。

Method: 提出FairGen框架，核心是将医生对齐的偏好（physician-aligned preferences）嵌入到扩散模型的生成过程中。具体而言，FairGen在条件扩散生成过程中引入公平性约束，通过引导生成过程覆盖稀少的人口亚群，同时利用偏好优化机制确保合成图像保留疾病相关的视觉特征，从而在提升亚群覆盖的同时不损害病理信息的完整性。

Result: 在三个基准任务上评估：皮肤镜图像公平性提升95.9%，胸部X光片提升80.0%，脑部MRI提升35.2%。合成图像在下游分类任务中保持与原临床数据训练模型相当的诊断准确率。经临床医生专家评审和独立外部队列验证，证实这些收益超越了标准保真度指标，且不限于原始分布内数据集。

Conclusion: FairGen证明了通过公平性感知的扩散生成可以有效缓解医学影像中的种群不平衡问题，在不牺牲诊断准确性的前提下大幅提升模型公平性，为减少AI诊断系统中的医疗差距提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Medical imaging is central to modern diagnostics, and artificial intelligence (AI) systems are increasingly used to support image-based analysis by improving efficiency, accuracy, and access to care. However, inequities in healthcare access and differential disease prevalence create severe demographic imbalances in clinical image data. Such imbalances are compounded by the fact that diseases can manifest with distinct features across demographic groups, rendering certain phenotypic presentations naturally rare. AI models trained on such imbalanced data risk perpetuating diagnostic bias and widening healthcare disparities. Here we introduce FairGen, a fairness-aware diffusion framework that synthesizes demographically balanced medical images while preserving pathology-relevant visual features. By embedding physician-aligned preferences into the generation process, FairGen improves subgroup coverage during synthesis and downstream classification. Applied to dermatology, radiology, and neuroimaging benchmark tasks, FairGen achieves fairness improvements of 95.9% for skin images, 80.0% for chest radiography, and 35.2% for brain MRI, while maintaining competitive diagnostic accuracy relative to models trained on original clinical data. Clinician-facing expert review and external validation on independent cohorts further support that these gains extend beyond standard fidelity metrics and are not confined to the original in-distribution datasets.

</details>


### [7] [FUSE: Quantifying Uncertainty in Vision-Language Models by Bayesian Fusing Epistemic and Aleatoric Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14728)
*Harry Zhang,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FUSE 是一个面向视觉语言模型（VLM）的概率框架，通过贝叶斯融合机制将两种不确定性——源自输入数据视觉-语言歧义的**偶然型嵌入级不确定性**（aleatoric）和源自VLM语义响应多样性的**认知型模型级不确定性**（epistemic）——分析性地组合为标量度量，用于可靠预测模型输出正确性，在不确定性校准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人等关键应用中，量化VLM输出的不确定性至关重要，但现有方法未能同时捕获数据侧和模型侧的互补不确定性来源。

Method: 提出 FUSE 概率框架：(1) 从输入数据的视觉-语言歧义中提取偶然型嵌入级不确定性；(2) 从VLM的语义响应多样性中估计认知型模型级不确定性；(3) 通过贝叶斯融合机制将两者分析性组合为单一标量不确定性度量。

Result: FUSE 在不确定性校准任务上超越基线方法，达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: FUSE 能够有效融合偶然型和认知型两种互补不确定性来源，其输出的标量不确定性度量可以可靠预测VLM的输出正确性，适用于下游实际应用。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are playing an increasingly important role across multiple domains. In many applications, such as robotics, it is crucial to quantify the uncertainty in the output of these models. } We develop FUSE, a probabilistic framework for capturing two complementary sources of uncertainty in vision-language modeling: (i) aleatoric embedding-level uncertainty derived from input data vision-language ambiguity, and (ii) epistemic model-level uncertainty estimated from the semantic response diversity of VLMs. Our approach formulates a Bayesian fusion mechanism that analytically combines these uncertainty sources to produce a scalar measure of uncertainty. This measure can be used to reliably predict the model's output correctness for downstream applications. We demonstrate that our method outperforms baselines and achieves SOTA uncertainty calibration.

</details>


### [8] [Hierarchical GRU with Input-Conditioned Slot Queries for Ball Action Anticipation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14730)
*Parthsarthi Rawat*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种分层模型，通过共享局部Transformer、GRU时序聚合和Transformer解码器，结合频率重加权匈牙利匹配与高斯软目标，在SoccerNet球动作预测基准上取得17.91% mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球视频中球动作预测具有类别不平衡和时序定位困难的问题，现有方法对稀有动作类预测能力不足，且时间边界监督不够精细。

Method: 分层架构：共享局部Transformer编码每5秒子窗口的clip特征 → GRU跨子窗口聚合时序上下文 → Transformer解码器以K个输入条件事件槽解码，三解耦头分别预测目标性(objectness)、动作类别和时序偏移。训练策略：频率重加权匈牙利匹配，系统性地偏向稀有动作类别；采用高斯软目标代替硬边界进行时间bin监督。

Result: 在SoccerNet Ball Action Anticipation测试服务器上达到17.91% mAP。

Conclusion: 分层时序建模与针对类别不平衡的匹配策略可有效提升球动作预测性能，但mAP仍有较大提升空间，未来可进一步优化。

Abstract: We present a hierarchical model for ball action anticipation in football broadcast video. Given a 30-second observation window, the system predicts actions occurring in the subsequent 5-second window across 10 classes. A shared local Transformer encodes clip-level features within each 5-second sub-window; a GRU then aggregates temporal context across all sub-windows; finally, a Transformer decoder with K input-conditioned event slots decodes the anticipation target via three decoupled heads (objectness, class, temporal offset). We introduce frequency-reweighted Hungarian matching that systematically favours rare action classes, and Gaussian soft targets for temporal bin supervision. On the SoccerNet Ball Action Anticipation benchmark, our method achieves 17.91% mAP on the test server.

</details>


### [9] [BBR-Net: Boundary-Balanced Replay for Continual Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14731)
*Zahid Ullah,Sieun Choi,Jihie Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出边界平衡回放网络（BBR-Net），通过边界感知优先级和类别平衡选择回放样本，证明在域偏移下保留解剖结构是持续医学图像分割的核心因素，且回放有效性取决于存储结构信息的质量而非仅靠记忆容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于回放的持续学习方法在医学图像分割中主要保留外观信息，缺乏对解剖结构的显式建模。该研究旨在探究结构一致性是否主导持续心脏超声分割中的知识保留。

Method: 提出BBR-Net，采用边界感知优先级和类别平衡策略选择回放样本以保留解剖结构信息丰富的区域。在CAMUS和CardiacNet两个数据集上，按前向（CAMUS→CardiacNet）和反向（CardiacNet→CAMUS）任务顺序评估。进一步通过受控结构扰动实验，逐步破坏源任务边界同时固定数据集、架构和训练协议，以孤立结构质量的影响。

Result: 前向设置下，BBR-Net源任务性能接近离线联合训练，灾难性遗忘显著减少，目标任务适应保持竞争力。消融实验证实边界感知优先与类别感知采样结合可改善保留-适应平衡。反向设置下，结构感知回放失效，因初始表征来自噪声大且结构不一致的数据。结构扰动分析表明，随着结构可靠性下降，遗忘持续增加，回放有效性受存储结构信息质量而非记忆容量的主导。

Conclusion: 在域偏移下保留解剖结构是持续医学图像分割的中心因素，回放机制应考量结构可靠性以支持鲁棒的知识保留。

Abstract: Continual learning for medical image segmentation remains challenging under domain shift because replay-based methods often preserve appearance information without explicitly modeling anatomical structure. This study investigates whether structural consistency governs knowledge retention in continual cardiac ultrasound segmentation. We propose the Boundary-Balanced Replay Network (BBR-Net), which selects replay samples using boundary-aware priority and class balance to preserve anatomically informative regions. The method is evaluated on CAMUS and CardiacNet under forward (CAMUS to CardiacNet) and reverse (CardiacNet to CAMUS) task orders. In the forward setting, BBR-Net retains source-task performance close to an offline joint-training reference, while markedly reducing catastrophic forgetting and preserving competitive target-task adaptation. Ablation results show that boundary-aware prioritization contributes to retention and improves the balance between source-task preservation and target-task adaptation when combined with class-aware sampling. In contrast, the reverse setting reveals that structure-aware replay fails when initial representations are learned from noisy and structurally inconsistent data. To isolate this effect, we conduct a controlled structural perturbation analysis by progressively corrupting source-task boundaries while keeping the dataset, architecture, and training protocol fixed. Forgetting increases consistently as structural reliability decreases, suggesting that replay effectiveness is strongly influenced by the quality of stored structural information, rather than by memory capacity alone. These findings indicate that preserving anatomical structure under domain shift is a central factor in continual medical image segmentation, and that replay mechanisms should account for structural reliability to support robust knowledge retention.

</details>


### [10] [Steady-Forcing: Balancing Spatial Persistence and Motion Continuity in Long-Horizon Nature Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14732)
*Matiur Rahman Minar,Seunghun Oh,GangHyeon Jeong,Unsang Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出Steady-Forcing框架，通过V-Sink视觉锚点、EMA-Sink运动记忆、块相对时间编码、定期缓存清理及教师蒸馏等机制，解决了固定相机下长时自然场景视频生成中“背景漂移”与“运动停滞”之间的稳定性-运动性权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在长时生成中存在稳定性-运动性权衡：保持空间稳定性的机制会抑制运动（如水、火、烟雾停滞），而维持运动则导致场景布局漂移。在固定相机自然场景视频生成中，这两种失效模式可以更清晰地分离，但现有方法缺乏针对性解决方案，且通用评测基准（如VBench）对此类伪影惩罚不足。

Method: 作者提出Steady-Forcing框架，包含：(1) V-Sink：持久视觉锚点，保持背景一致性；(2) EMA-Sink：指数移动平均运动记忆，维持流体运动的视觉合理性；(3) 块相对时间编码；(4) 定期缓存清理机制；(5) 基于Wan2.1-14B教师模型的知识蒸馏，该教师模型在任务特定配置下引入了运动奖励先验。

Result: 在七个基线方法上的评估表明，Steady-Forcing在长时背景一致性和成像质量方面均有提升。盲测用户研究显示感知稳定性和运动连续性更强。此外，基准评测发现VBench等通用指标对固定相机伪影惩罚不足，甚至将漂移导致的光流误奖励为"动态程度"，且未直接惩罚纹理硬化或流动停滞。

Conclusion: Steady-Forcing有效缓解了固定相机自然场景长时生成中的稳定性-运动性权衡。论文同时指出当前通用视频生成基准对静态相机场景的评估存在缺陷，呼吁构建针对固定相机自然流动评估的专用任务基准。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models enable streaming generation but often degrade over long rollouts: static scene layouts drift, while mechanisms that improve spatial stability tend to suppress motion, causing natural flows such as water, fire, or smoke to stagnate. We study this stability-motion trade-off in fixed-camera long-horizon nature video generation, where the two failure modes can be more clearly separated than in moving-camera settings. We propose Steady-Forcing, a memory and training framework combining a persistent visual anchor (V-Sink), an exponential moving-average motion memory (EMA-Sink), block-relative temporal encoding, periodic cache purification, and distillation from a Wan2.1-14B teacher with motion-rewarded priors under task-focused configurations. Together, these components are designed to preserve background identity while sustaining visually plausible fluid dynamics over multi-minute autoregressive rollouts. Evaluations across seven baselines show that Steady-Forcing improves long horizon background consistency and imaging quality, while a blind user study indicates stronger perceived stability and motion continuity. The benchmark evaluation further suggest that generic VBench aggregate scores under-penalize fixed-camera artifacts as well as rewarding drift-induced optical flow as Dynamic Degree while not directly penalizing texture hardening or flow stagnation - motivating future task-specific benchmarks for static-camera nature-flow evaluation. Project page: https://minar09.github.io/steadyforcing/

</details>


### [11] [UtVAA: Ultra-tiny Vision Transformer with Affix Attention for Mobile Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14735)
*Romiyal George,Sathiyamohan Nishankar,Selvarajah Thuseethan,Roshan G. Ragel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UtVAA是一种超微型视觉Transformer架构，通过Affix Attention模块和Dilated Bottleneck模块在极低参数量（最低204.67K）和计算量（53.95M FLOPs）下实现竞争性的图像分类精度，适合移动端和边缘设备部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision Transformers虽表征能力强，但其二次复杂度自注意力和庞大参数量限制了在资源受限的移动端和边缘设备上的部署，亟需在保持判别性能的前提下将ViT压缩至超微型规模。

Method: 提出UtVAA架构，核心包括：(1) Affix Attention模块——融合深度可分离逐点局部特征提取、线性自注意力、坐标注意力（coordinate attention）用于空间依赖建模，以及轻量三元融合策略整合局部与全局表征；(2) Dilated Bottleneck模块——利用膨胀深度可分离卷积扩大感受野，同时通过残差连接保持低FLOPs和训练稳定性。架构按Tiny/Medium/Large三档缩放。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、PlantVillage-Tomato和SLIF-Tomato四个数据集上，最小模型以204.67K参数和53.95M FLOPs取得了竞争性精度，证明在百万参数以下的体量内仍能保持良好判别性能。

Conclusion: 基于Transformer的视觉模型可被重新设计为超微型架构而不显著损失判别性能，UtVAA验证了这一可行性，为移动端和边缘端的视觉识别部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) have demonstrated strong representation capability in image classification. However, their quadratic self-attention complexity and large parameter counts limit deployment on resource-constrained mobile and edge devices. This paper introduces UtVAA, an ultra-tiny Vision Transformer architecture designed for efficient visual recognition under strict computational budgets. It incorporates a novel Affix Attention block that combines depthwise-pointwise local feature extraction, linear self-attention, coordinate attention for spatial dependency modelling, and a lightweight ternary fusion strategy to integrate local and global representations. In addition, Dilated Bottleneck blocks expand the receptive field using dilated depthwise separable convolutions while maintaining low FLOPs and stable optimisation through residual connections. UtVAA is implemented in scalable Tiny, Medium, and Large variants, with the smallest model containing 204.67K parameters and 53.95M FLOPs. Experimental results on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, PlantVillage-Tomato and SLIF-Tomato datasets show that UtVAA achieves competitive accuracy within a sub-million-parameter regime. Overall, the results demonstrate that transformer-based vision models can be redesigned into ultra-tiny architectures without significant loss in discriminative performance, making UtVAA suitable for mobile and edge deployment. Code is available at https://github.com/romiyal/UtVAA

</details>


### [12] [GridVQA-X: A Framework for Evaluating Multimodal Explainability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14740)
*Sujay Belsare,Sudarshan Nikhil,Sushant Kumar,Ponnurangam Kumaraguru,Chirag Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GridVQA-X，首个专为评估跨模态可解释性而设计的诊断框架。通过合成逻辑生成具有数学保证的解释，并训练成对模型（纯推理模型与捷径依赖模型）作为测试平台，揭示了现有MxAI方法无法区分真正的跨模态推理与浅层跨模态捷径，暴露了当前可解释性评估中的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉-语言模型的快速发展，其预测的可解释性需求日益迫切，但多模态可解释性研究严重滞后。现有评估协议缺乏真值标注，无法区分真正的跨模态推理（如空间组合）与浅层跨模态捷径（如词袋属性匹配），导致无法判断MxAI方法是忠实捕获了模态间的协同交互，还是仅对简单特征检测器产生的推理幻觉。

Method: 作者设计了GridVQA-X诊断框架，利用封闭世界的合成逻辑生成独特且具有数学保证的解释真值。在该受控环境中，训练架构相同但行为不同的成对模型：M_pure学习鲁棒的空间-关系推理，M_spur在结构上被迫依赖跨模态捷径。这种行为分歧构成了严格的测试基准——忠实的解释器必须为每个模型报告不同的推理路径。

Result: 实验发现，当前广泛使用的MxAI方法均无法区分依赖真正空间-关系推理的模型和利用跨模态捷径的模型，无法捕捉真实的跨模态协同效应，并错误表征了多模态模型的实际决策方式。

Conclusion: 现有MxAI方法在捕捉真实跨模态协同方面存在严重不足，无法忠实反映多模态模型的决策过程。GridVQA-X为跨模态可解释性研究提供了一个亟需的严格诊断基准，揭示了该领域亟需开发能够真正捕捉模态间交互推理的新方法。

Abstract: With the increasing development of Vision-Language Models, it becomes imperative that their predictions are readily explainable to relevant stakeholders. However, the field of explainability has not kept pace with the multimodal surge. While recent Multimodal Explainable AI (MxAI) methods generate explanations to attribute the interaction between different modalities, current evaluation protocols lack the ground truth required to distinguish between true cross-modal reasoning (e.g., spatial composition) and shallow cross-modal shortcuts (e.g., Bag-of-Words attribute matching). It remains unknown whether MxAI methods faithfully capture synergistic interactions or merely hallucinate reasoning on models acting as simple feature detectors. In this paper, we introduce GridVQA-X, the first diagnostic framework specifically designed to evaluate cross-modal explainability. Unlike natural datasets, GridVQA-X leverages a closed-world synthesis logic to generate unique, mathematically guaranteed explanations. We utilize this controlled environment to train paired ground-truth models on identical architectures: $M_{\text{pure}}$, which learns robust spatial-relational reasoning and $M_{\text{spur}}$, which is structurally forced to rely on cross-modal shortcuts. This behavioral divergence creates a rigorous testbed: a faithful explainer must report distinct reasoning pathways for each model. Our findings reveal that widely used methods fail to distinguish between models relying on genuine spatial-relational reasoning and those exploiting cross-modal shortcuts, highlighting a critical gap in capturing true cross-modal synergy and misrepresenting how multimodal models actually make decisions.

</details>


### [13] [HorusEye: Language as Dynamic Attention for Emergency Visual Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14741)
*Armel Yara*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍HorusEye，一个将语言作为动态注意力的紧急视觉分析系统。研究通过五阶段流程，在RefCOCO-Degraded数据集（15,244张图像）上评估多个视觉语言模型在视觉定位、语言反馈恢复、健康VQA和幻觉分析中的表现。核心发现包括Gemini在热成像条件下通过迭代语言反馈提升47.3%，而Qwen2-VL反而下降5.1%；发现了"热成像悖论"；BLIP-2在退化条件下幻觉加剧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急场景（如火灾、烟雾、热成像）中视觉条件严重退化，现有视觉语言模型（VLMs）在这些条件下的表现尚不明确。研究旨在系统评估VLMs在退化视觉环境中的鲁棒性，并探索语言反馈作为动态注意力机制是否可以弥补视觉退化带来的性能损失。

Method: 研究分为五个阶段：(1)构建RefCOCO-Degraded数据集，包含3,811张基础图像的四种条件（清晰、雾、烟、热成像），共15,244张图像；(2)在视觉定位任务上评估Gemini、Qwen2-VL、BLIP-2、LLaVA、Kosmos-2等多个VLMs；(3)引入迭代语言反馈机制，评估模型利用语言线索恢复性能的能力；(4)进行健康VQA任务评估；(5)开展幻觉分析，比较不同模型在退化条件下的幻觉倾向。同时探索了裁剪策略在不同成像条件下的效果差异。

Result: (1)语言反馈效果具有模型依赖性：Gemini在热成像条件下通过迭代语言反馈提升47.3%，而Qwen2-VL在相同协议下性能下降5.1%；(2)发现"热成像悖论"：在RGB图像上有效的裁剪策略在热成像中灾难性失效；(3)BLIP-2在退化条件下幻觉现象独特加剧，不适合紧急场景部署。

Conclusion: 语言反馈作为动态注意力机制在退化视觉环境中的有效性高度依赖具体模型架构；模型在退化条件下的行为差异显著，提示紧急部署前需要进行针对性的鲁棒性评估；BLIP-2的异常幻觉行为使其明确不适合紧急响应场景。

Abstract: We introduce HorusEye, Language as Dynamic Attention for Emergency Visual Analysis. Our investigation followed five stages. The first one is benchmarking RefCOCO-Degraded, a dataset of 15,244 images (3,811 base images x 4 conditions: Clean, Fog, Smoke and Thermal) with systematic visual degradation. Through four research questions, we evaluate multiple VLMs (Gemini, Qwen2-VL, BLIP-2, LLaVA, Kosmos-2) across visual grounding the second stage, language feedback recovery the third one, health VQA tasks the fourth, and hallucination analysis the final stage. Our key finding is that language feedback effectiveness is model-dependent: Gemini achieves +47.3% improvement in thermal conditions through iterative language feedback, while Qwen2-VL shows -5.1% degradation under the same protocol. We also identify the 'Thermal Paradox' where cropping strategies that improve RGB performance catastrophically fail in thermal imagery. Furthermore, BLIP-2 uniquely hallucinates more under degradation, making it unsuitable for emergency deployment

</details>


### [14] [Style-CCL: Content-Preserving Style Transfer via Curriculum Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14746)
*Shiwen Zhang,Haoyuan Wang,Xianghao Zang,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Style-CCL，一个多阶段课程持续学习框架，用于训练 Style-Content Diffusion Transformer (SC-DiT)，解决内容保留风格迁移中语义风格主导、纹理风格学习困难及灾难性遗忘等问题，在风格相似度、内容一致性和美学质量三个指标上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) 的内容保留风格迁移方法采用单阶段训练范式，在混合风格类别上训练时，语义风格（易）会主导学习过程，阻碍纹理风格（难）的习得，同时损害内容保留能力。因此需要一种新的训练策略来解耦并平衡不同难度风格的学习。

Method: 提出 Style-CCL 多阶段课程持续学习框架：(1) 将训练数据从语义风格（简单）到纹理风格（困难）、从干净数据到合成数据进行课程排序；(2) 基于 SC-DiT 架构，采用双分支设计，通过独立的 ROPE 嵌入和因果掩码解耦风格与内容；(3) 在阶段间引入 Random Memory Rehearsal 机制，回放先前阶段的样本以防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在风格相似度（style similarity）、内容一致性（content consistency）和美学质量（aesthetic quality）三项核心指标上均达到 state-of-the-art 性能，验证了多阶段课程持续学习相比单阶段训练范式的显著优势。

Conclusion: Style-CCL 通过课程学习和持续学习的结合，有效解决了 DiT 风格迁移中语义-纹理风格学习不平衡和灾难性遗忘问题，显著提升了内容保留风格迁移的整体质量。该框架为 DiT 在风格迁移中的训练策略提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Content-Preserving Style transfer, given content and style references, remains challenging for Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) due to entangled content and style features. With a reverse triplet synthesis pipeline to build a million-scale training set and a dual-branch Style-Content DiT (SC-DiT) that decouples style and content via separate ROPE embeddings and causal masking, we observe that such a one-stage training paradigm on mixed style categories causes semantic styles to dominate, hindering texture style learning, and harming content preservation. To address these issues, we propose Style-CCL, a Multi-Stage Curriculum Continual Learning framework that trains SC-DiT from semantic (easy) to texture (hard) styles, and from clean to synthetic data, with Random Memory Rehearsal across stages to avoid catastrophic forgetting. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our Style-CCL achieves state-of-the-art performance in three core metrics: style similarity, content consistency, and aesthetic quality.

</details>


### [15] [MMLongEmbed: Benchmarking Multimodal Embedding Models in Long-Context Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14747)
*Haitian Wang,Ruoxi Sun,Quantong Qiu,Juntao Li,Junhui Li,Hua Chen,Jinxiong Chang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MMLongEmbed基准，系统评估多模态嵌入模型在长上下文场景下的表现，发现现有模型依赖浅层特征匹配，难以捕捉深层语义结构依赖，且性能随上下文长度和信息位置系统性退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态嵌入模型的理论上下文窗口虽已大幅扩展，但缺乏对长上下文场景下实际理解与表征能力的系统评估，这成为实际部署的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建MMLongEmbed基准，包含四个检索任务，覆盖文本、文档、视频三种模态及多种上下文长度范围，对主流模型进行广泛评测，分析性能随上下文长度和关键信息位置的变化规律，以及跨模态的冗余信息鲁棒性差异。

Result: 现有模型严重依赖浅层特征匹配，难以捕获深层语义和结构依赖；性能随上下文长度增加和信息位置变化呈系统性退化；不同模型对跨模态冗余信息的鲁棒性存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 更大的理论上下文窗口并不等同于有效的长上下文多模态理解，当前MEM架构在深层语义建模方面存在根本性不足，MMLongEmbed为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Recent advancements have significantly expanded the theoretical context windows of Multimodal Embedding Models (MEMs). However, larger context windows do not necessarily translate into effective comprehension and representation of long-context multimodal inputs, which remains a critical bottleneck for real-world deployment. To address the lack of systematic evaluation in this setting, we introduce MMLongEmbed, the first comprehensive benchmark for evaluating MEMs in long-context scenarios. MMLongEmbed comprises four retrieval tasks spanning multiple context-length ranges, covering text, document, and video modalities. Through extensive evaluation of state-of-the-art models, we find that current architectures rely heavily on superficial feature matching and struggle to capture deep semantic and structural dependencies. We further observe that performance degradation varies systematically with context length and key information placement. Moreover, models exhibit substantially different robustness to redundant contextual information across modalities. For reproducibility, the benchmark and code are publicly available.

</details>


### [16] [Is My Vision-Language Data in Your AI? Membership Inference Test (MINT) Demo 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14748)
*Daniel DeAlcala,Gonzalo Mancera,Julian Fierrez,Aythami Morales,Ruben Tolosana,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍MINT Demo 2框架，一种通过成员推断测试来检测特定数据是否被用于模型训练的技术，在面部识别和LLM上达到90%准确率，并提供支持图像和文本模态审计的网络平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升机器学习训练过程的透明度，为新兴AI法规的合规性提供实用工具。

Method: 提出MINT（Membership Inference Test）理论框架，根据对审计模型已知信息的多寡设计多种架构变体（MINT、aMINT、gMINT），并在面部识别模型、4个SOTA大语言模型及大规模公开图像与文本数据库上进行实验验证，最终构建综合性网络平台。

Result: 在检测训练数据方面达到最高90%的准确率，平台成功整合多模态审计能力。

Conclusion: MINT Demo 2有效实现了对机器学习模型训练数据的成员推断，为促进AI透明度和合规审计提供了理论与实用并重的解决方案。

Abstract: We present the Membership Inference Test (MINT) Demo 2, a framework designed to improve transparency in machine learning training processes. MINT is a technique for experimentally determining whether specific data were used during machine learning model training. We establish the theoretical framework and propose multiple architectures for MINT depending on the amount of information known about the models that are being audited. Experimental results using a popular face recognition model, 4 state-of-the-art LLMs, and multiple, diverse, and large-scale public image and text databases achieve promising accuracy levels in the detection of training data of up to 90%. Building on these results, we introduce a comprehensive web platform1 that expands these capabilities to image and text modalities. The platform integrates a diverse technological stack, including MINT, aMINT, and gMINT, allowing users to audit a wide range of models. This demonstrator aims to promote AI transparency and provides a practical tool to foster compliance with emerging AI regulations.

</details>


### [17] [Automated 3D Kinematic Monitoring for Circadian Activity and Anomaly Detection in Juvenile Fish](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14749)
*Chih-Wei Huang,Chang-Wen Huang,Chung-Ping Chiang,Tsung-Wei Pan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对精准水产养殖中高分辨率行为表型瓶颈，提出一种融合深度学习目标检测与双目立体视觉的3D行为表型框架，实现对高密度幼年罗非鱼的实时监测，首次精确量化自由游动幼鱼的真实3D游泳速度和加速度，并建立了昼夜运动基线作为生理应激预警指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精准水产养殖面临"表型瓶颈"：传统方法无法量化鱼类的瞬时三维（3D）物理运动强度，2D方法存在透视畸变，难以获得真实游泳速度。因此需要一种高通量、非接触的3D行为表型方法来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出高通量3D行为表型框架，核心为深度学习目标检测与双目立体视觉的集成系统。具体包括：(1) 深度学习模型实现高密度环境下的幼年罗非鱼自动检测；(2) 双目立体视觉重建3D绝对空间坐标；(3) 自动化非接触体长估计；(4) 从3D轨迹中精确量化速度和加速度。

Result: 该框架成功实现了：(1) 高密度养殖环境下幼年罗非鱼的实时3D轨迹重建；(2) 首次估算自由游动幼鱼的真实物理游泳速度（消除2D透视畸变后）；(3) 建立了昼夜运动基线模式，可用于生理应激早期预警和鱼类活力客观评估。

Conclusion: 所提出的3D行为表型框架有效突破了传统2D方法的局限，能够精确量化鱼类的真实3D运动状态，为精准水产养殖提供了客观的鱼类活力指标和应激预警工具，具有显著的应用前景。

Abstract: Precision aquaculture faces a "phenotyping bottleneck" in tracking high-resolution behavioral traits, as conventional methods cannot quantify instantaneous three-dimensional (3D) physical exertion. To address this, we present a high-throughput 3D behavioral phenotyping framework integrating deep learning object detection with binocular stereo vision for real-time monitoring of juvenile tilapia in high-density environments. The system automates non-contact body length estimation and reconstructs 3D swimming trajectories from absolute spatial coordinates. By eliminating 2D perspective distortions, this approach precisely quantifies 3D velocity and acceleration, marking the first estimation of true physical swimming speeds in free-roaming juveniles. Results show the framework successfully establishes circadian locomotor baselines, serving as an early warning system for physiological stress and providing an objective metric for fish vitality.

</details>


### [18] [X-Tokenizer: A Multimodal Action Tokenizer for Vision-Language-Action Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14752)
*Xirui Kang,Yanpei Shi,Lucy Liang,Roy Gan,Dongxiu Liu,Pushi Zhang,Danpeng Chen,Xiaoyi Qin,Yinan Zheng,Jinliang Zheng,Hao Wang,Xianyuan Zhan,Hang Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: X-Tokenizer 提出将动作分词从单纯的压缩重构提升为语义接口学习，通过语义残差量化（SRQ）的非对称结构和多模态对比对齐，为 VLA 模型提供兼具语义监督与运动保真度的共享动作表示，在真实机器人和仿真任务上均显著超越 FAST。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型中的动作分词器主要面向重构设计，虽能保留运动几何，但仅为骨干网络提供弱语义监督。作者认为动作分词不应仅是压缩工具，而应成为连接多模态推理与可执行控制的语义接口，从而更有效地桥接视觉-语言理解与连续机器人控制。

Method: 提出 X-Tokenizer，一种轻量级的编码器-语义残差量化（SRQ）-解码器架构。核心创新 SRQ 在残差向量量化上施加非对称结构：第一层通过掩码动作建模（MAM）训练，形成捕获粗粒度运动意图的离散动作语言；更深层保持面向重构的残差编码以保留细粒度细节。此外，X-Tokenizer 通过与预训练基础模型表示空间的对比对齐以及下一帧视觉-语言特征预测进行预训练，使动作 token 与多模态语义对齐。在 240 万条轨迹（20 亿动作帧）上预训练后，冻结的 X-Tokenizer 作为表示塑形监督信号插入混合离散-连续 VLA。

Result: X-Tokenizer 在真实世界综合评测和 RoboTwin 2.0 仿真中均取得领先结果，在多模态 grounding 上超越 FAST 13.5%，在长程任务上超越 FAST 8.25%。

Conclusion: 动作分词器不仅是压缩工具，更是 VLA 预训练中的语义接口。X-Tokenizer 通过 SRQ 非对称结构将高层语义与运动细节解耦，证明了这一范式在提升多模态对齐和长程任务能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models must bridge pretrained vision-language reasoning and precise continuous robot control. Existing action tokenizers discretize actions primarily for reconstruction, producing codes that preserve motion geometry but provide only weak semantic supervision to the backbone. We therefore formulate action tokenization not as mere compression, but as semantic interface learning between multimodal reasoning and executable control. To this end, we introduce X-Tokenizer, a lightweight encoder-Semantic Residual Quantization (SRQ)-decoder architecture that provides a shared action interface across diverse robotic arm embodiments. Its key component, SRQ, imposes an asymmetric structure on residual vector quantization: the first level is trained with Masked Action Modeling (MAM) to form a discrete action language that captures coarse motion intent, while deeper levels remain reconstruction-oriented residuals that preserve fine-grained details. To further align action tokens with multimodal semantics, X-Tokenizer is pretrained with contrastive alignment to the representation space of a pretrained foundation model and with next-frame vision-language feature prediction. Pretrained on 2.4M trajectories (2.0B action frames), a single frozen X-Tokenizer plugs into a mixed discrete-continuous VLA as a representation-shaping supervision signal. X-Tokenizer achieves top real-world aggregate and strong RoboTwin 2.0 simulation results. Outperforming FAST in multimodal grounding (+13.5%) and long-horizon tasks (+8.25), it shows that action tokenizers serve as semantic interfaces for VLA pretraining beyond mere action compression.

</details>

### [19] [PhoneHarness: Harnessing Phone-Use Agents through Mixed GUI, CLI, and Tool Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14832)
*Chenxin Li,Zhengyao Fang,Zhengyang Tang,Pengyuan Lyu,Xingran Zhou,Xin Lai,Fei Tang,Liang Wu,Yiduo Guo,Weinong Wang,Junyi Li,Yi Zhang,Yang Ding,Huawen Shen,Sunqi Fan,Shangpin Peng,Zheng Ruan,Anran Zhang,Benyou Wang,Chengquan Zhang,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PhoneHarness 是一个面向手机智能体的混合动作基准与执行框架，它将 GUI 操作、CLI 命令和主机端工具调用统一为可验证的工作流，配套的 PhoneHarness Bench 通过可观察副作用来评估任务完成情况，取得了 75.0% 的通过率，比最强非 PhoneHarness 设置高出 12.9 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前手机智能体研究大多将智能体视为 GUI 控制器，仅评估界面操作能力，但真实的手机使用任务需要智能体在应用 GUI、设备端命令和结构化工具之间做出决策，并留下可验证的执行证据。现有基准无法有效衡量这种混合动作能力。

Method: 提出 PhoneHarness，一个混合动作基准与执行框架：(1) 在设备端运行智能体循环，统一管理 GUI 操作、CLI 命令和主机端工具调用三种动作类型；(2) 采用确定性动作路由机制，结合有边界的 GUI 委托；(3) 生成可审计的执行轨迹；(4) 配套基准 PhoneHarness Bench，以任务是否产生可观察副作用（而非仅输出合理最终答案）作为评估标准。

Result: 在标注评测集上，PhoneHarness 达到 75.0% 的通过率，比最强非 PhoneHarness 基线设置高出 12.9 个百分点。

Conclusion: 可靠的手机自动化不仅依赖于视觉 GUI 控制，还取决于动作面路由和可验证执行。PhoneHarness 框架与基准相互依赖：框架使混合手机工作流可执行，基准则衡量智能体能否可靠、安全地使用该框架。

Abstract: Phone agents are increasingly expected to complete real mobile workflows rather than merely predict the next screen action. However, much of the current mobile-agent literature still evaluates agents primarily as GUI controllers that observe a screen, emit taps and swipes, and are scored by target app state. Real phone-use tasks are broader: they require deciding when to use app GUIs, device-side commands, or structured tools, while leaving evidence that the intended side effect actually occurred. We introduce PhoneHarness, a mixed-action benchmark and execution harness for studying phone-use agents on verifiable mobile workflows. PhoneHarness runs a device-side agent loop over GUI, CLI, and host-side tool actions, combining deterministic action routing with bounded GUI delegation and auditable execution traces. Its benchmark, PhoneHarness Bench, evaluates whether agents complete tasks with observable side effects, not only whether they produce plausible final answers. On the annotated evaluation split, PhoneHarness reaches a 75.0% pass rate, outperforming the strongest non-PhoneHarness settings by 12.9 percentage points. PhoneHarness and PhoneHarness Bench therefore play distinct but mutually dependent roles: the harness makes mixed phone workflows executable, while the benchmark measures whether agents can use that harness reliably and safely. Our findings suggest that reliable phone automation depends on action-surface routing and verifiable execution, not only visual GUI control.

</details>


### [20] [Evaluating the Robustness of Proof Autoformalization in Lean 4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14867)
*Zhengtao Gui,Sheng Yang,Zhouxing Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首次系统评估证明自动形式化模型的鲁棒性，发现现有模型对全局扰动（释义改写）敏感且大多无法忠实反映局部扰动（数值、符号、步骤更改）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有证明自动形式化的评估仅关注精心整理的理想化非形式证明，忽视了真实场景中非形式证明可能偏离理想形式的情况。因此需要评估模型在非理想输入下的鲁棒性——即是否仍能保持形式化的正确性与忠实性。

Method: 定义两类扰动：全局扰动（以不同风格释义改写非形式证明，期望形式化结果保持一致性）；局部扰动（更改数值、符号或证明步骤，可能反事实，期望形式化结果忠实反映更改）。基于 miniF2F 和 MATH-500 构建含两类扰动的基准，自动度量全局扰动下形式化结果的稳定性与局部扰动下输出的忠实性，并用该基准评估 7 个最新模型。

Result: 7 个最新模型均对全局扰动敏感，且大多数无法在局部扰动下保持忠实，说明现有证明自动形式化模型鲁棒性不足。

Conclusion: 证明自动形式化模型的鲁棒性是一个被忽视但关键的问题；当前模型在非理想输入下表现脆弱，未来研究需着力提升模型对输入变化的稳定性与忠实性。

Abstract: Proof autoformalization aims to translate a mathematical informal proof written in natural language into a formal proof in a formal language such as Lean~4. Several works have developed LLM-based models for proof autoformalization. However, existing evaluations have typically focused on translating well-formed informal proofs from curated datasets. We argue that a robust proof autoformalizer must remain faithful even for informal proofs that diverge from these idealized ones, and we present the first study on the robustness of proof autoformalization models. We formulate two categories of perturbations and evaluate robustness under each: a global perturbation paraphrases the informal proof in a different style, under which the formalization should remain consistent; a local perturbation alters a value, symbol, or proof step, possibly in a counterfactual way, and a robust formalization should faithfully reflect the perturbation rather than reverting to the original one or inferring a different one on its own. We build a benchmark with both perturbations on miniF2F and MATH-500, and automatically measure how stable a proof autoformalization's correctness is under global perturbations and how faithfully its output reflects local perturbations. We evaluate seven recent models, all of which are sensitive to global perturbations and mostly fail to remain faithful under local perturbations. Code and data are available via https://github.com/ucr-rai/robust-proof-autoformalization.

</details>


### [21] [Context Compression Is Not One Thing: Readable Symbolic Re-expression vs. Coherent Summary at Matched Budget](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14875)
*Sisong Bei,Mikhail L. Arbuzov,Ziwei Dong,Dmitri Kalaev,Alexey Shvets*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Telegraph English，一种将检索段落重写为结构化实体-关系声明的可读符号格式，在匹配token预算下，多跳QA任务上显著优于其他压缩基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型上下文窗口有限，现有压缩方法（截断、删除、摘要）在匹配token预算下往往会丢失多跳推理所需的关键实体-关系证据。需要一种能更密集保留推理证据的压缩方式。

Method: 设计Telegraph English格式，将检索到的段落重写为"实体-关系-实体"三元组形式的结构化符号语句；在MuSiQue、TwoWiki和HotpotQA三个多跳QA数据集上，与字符级删除、截断、随机子采样三种匹配预算压缩基线以及一致性散文摘要进行对比实验；预先注册了"深度-交互假设"（推理深度越深，Telegraph English优势越大）进行检验。

Result: Telegraph English在所有数据集上均优于三种匹配预算基线，F1提升13-20个百分点；在最难数据集上优于同编码器生成的一致性散文摘要；深度-交互假设被证伪——优势并未随推理深度增加而增长。

Conclusion: 可读的符号化重表达在匹配token预算下比自然语言或一致性摘要更密集地保留了实体内容，是多跳QA有效的上下文压缩策略。优势主要来自实体密度提升，而非对深层推理的特殊助益。

Abstract: We study context compression for multi-hop question answering with small language models. We propose Telegraph English, a readable symbolic format that rewrites retrieved passages into structured entity-relation statements, preserving reasoning evidence at lower token cost. In controlled experiments on MuSiQue, TwoWiki, and HotpotQA, Telegraph English outperforms three matched-budget compression baselines (character-level deletion, truncation, and random sub-sampling) on every dataset, with gains of 13 to 20 F1 percentage point. It also outperforms a coherent prose summary produced by the same encoder on the hardest dataset. A pre-registered depth-interaction hypothesis is null: the advantage does not grow with reasoning depth within datasets. We interpret these results as evidence that readable symbolic re-expression preserves entity content more densely than either natural language or coherent summarization at matched token budget.

</details>


### [22] [Simplifying the Modeling of Arbitrary Conditionals in Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14943)
*Yinhan Lu,Eric Elmoznino,Léo Gagnon,Sarthak Mittal,Tejas Kasetty,Guillaume Lajoie*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AC-GPT通过在标准因果Transformer中引入简单修改，使其能在单次前向传播中评估和采样任意条件分布（包括过去、未来和混合上下文），同时保持标准左到右的自回归性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准因果Transformer只能高效处理左到右的条件概率，无法直接处理任意条件分布（如给定前后文预测中间文本）。现有解决方案往往牺牲了标准自回归建模的质量。因此需要一种既能保持标准因果建模优势，又能灵活处理任意条件分布的方法。

Method: 提出AC-GPT，对标准因果Transformer进行简单修改，在保持左到右排序和下一token预测目标不变的前提下，通过单次前向传播即可评估和采样任意条件分布。该方法兼容现有LLM的微调。

Result: 实验结果表明，AC-GPT在建模任意条件分布方面优于基线方法，且不损害标准左到右生成性能。

Conclusion: AC-GPT成功实现了对任意条件分布的高效建模，同时保持了因果Transformer在自然语言上的强性能和高效训练优势，且可应用于现有LLM的微调。

Abstract: Causal Transformers model sequences through an autoregressive factorization of the joint distribution, which enables efficient left-to-right decoding and conditional likelihood computation. However, they cannot tractably sample from or evaluate arbitrary conditionals -- e.g., a block of text conditioned on past and future tokens. Recent work aims to solve this problem through novel architectures, but they often lead to sub-optimal modeling of such conditionals and degraded generations. We propose Arbitrary Conditionals GPT (AC-GPT) which introduces a simple modification to standard causal Transformers to enable evaluating and sampling from arbitrary conditionals -- including past, future, and mixed contexts -- within a single forward pass. Unlike prior approaches, our method preserves the standard left-to-right ordering and next-token prediction objective essential for both strong performance and efficient training on natural language. Crucially, this compatibility allows existing LLMs to be fine-tuned for arbitrary conditioning. Our empirical results indicate that our method outperforms baselines on modeling arbitrary conditionals, without degrading standard left-to-right performance.

</details>


### [23] [CoRA: Confidence-Rationale Alignment for Reliable Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14961)
*Juming Xiong,Weixin Liu,Kevin Guo,Congning Ni,Junchao Zhu,Chongyu Qu,Chao Yan,Katherine Brown,Avinash Baidya,Xiang Gao,Bradley Malin,Zhijun Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM中置信度与推理依据的对齐问题，提出基于GRPO的强化学习框架，通过联合奖励答案正确性、置信概率和基于评分量规的推理论证质量（评估依据性、连贯性、任务匹配度及与所选答案的关联），在三个数据集上将对齐误差降低最高达26.51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管思维链推理可提升LLM性能，但模型的高置信度答案可能具有误导性——其生成的推理论证看似合理却可能不完整或缺乏支撑。现有研究仅关注答案正确性与置信度的校准，忽视了置信度与推理过程质量之间的对齐问题。因此，作者旨在研究"置信度-推理依据对齐"：模型对答案的置信度是否由其生成的推理过程所正当化。

Method: 提出基于GRPO（Group Relative Policy Optimization）的强化学习框架，联合优化三个奖励信号：(1) 答案正确性奖励；(2) 承诺答案概率（即置信度）奖励，鼓励模型在推理充分时给出高置信度；(3) 基于评分量规的推理支撑度奖励，由LLM评判器依据四个维度评估——依据性（grounding）、连贯性（coherence）、任务匹配度（task match）以及与所选答案的关联性。评判器在评估时不接触正确答案，确保评估的独立性。在MedQA、MathQA和OpenBookQA三个数据集上使用三种开源LLM进行实验。

Result: 与未微调检查点、SFT和仅使用正确性奖励的GRPO相比，所提方法将置信度-推理依据对齐误差降低了最高26.51%，同时保持了有竞争力的准确率，并通常改善了置信度校准。

Conclusion: 可靠的思想链推理不仅需要高置信度的答案，更需要能够实质性支撑答案的推理过程。置信度与推理依据的对齐是构建可信LLM推理系统的关键维度。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning can improve LLM performance, but high answer confidence may be misleading when the accompanying CoT rationale is plausible yet incomplete or poorly supported. We study confidence--rationale alignment: whether a model's confidence in its committed answer is justified by its generated rationale. We introduce a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework that jointly rewards answer correctness, committed-answer probability, and rubric-based rationale support, where the rubric assesses grounding, coherence, task match, and connection to the selected answer without revealing the gold answer to the judge. Across MedQA, MathQA, and OpenBookQA using three open-weight LLMs, our method reduces the confidence--rationale alignment error by up to 26.51% compared with untuned checkpoints, SFT, and correctness-only GRPO, while maintaining competitive accuracy and often improving calibration. These results show that reliable CoT reasoning requires not only confident answers, but rationales that substantively support them.

</details>

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Nemotron 3 Ultra 是一个 550B 总参数 / 55B 激活参数的混合专家（MoE）混合 Mamba-Attention 语言模型，经 20T tokens 预训练，支持 1M 上下文，在保持顶尖精度的同时实现高达约 6 倍的推理吞吐量提升，适合长时间自主代理任务，并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在推理吞吐量和长上下文处理能力之间存在权衡，难以高效支撑长时间运行的自主代理任务。本文旨在通过混合架构与高效训练策略，在保持顶尖准确性的前提下大幅提升推理效率。

Method: 采用 Mixture-of-Experts 与 Mamba-Attention 混合架构（总参数 550B，激活参数 55B），关键技术包括：LatentMoE、多令牌预测（MTP）、NVFP4 预训练、多环境 RLVR、多教师在线策略蒸馏（MOPD）及推理预算控制。预训练规模为 20 万亿 tokens，上下文长度扩展至 1M tokens，后训练阶段结合 SFT、RL 与 MOPD。

Result: 在保持与当前最先进公开 LLM 同等准确度的前提下，推理吞吐量提升最高约 6 倍，支持 1M token 上下文长度。

Conclusion: Nemotron 3 Ultra 凭借顶尖精度、高推理吞吐量和超长上下文能力，成为长时间自主代理任务的理想选择。模型权重（基座、后训练及量化版本）、训练数据与配方已在 HuggingFace 全面开源。

Abstract: We introduce Nemotron 3 Ultra, a 550 billion total and 55 billion active parameter Mixture-of-Experts Hybrid Mamba-Attention language model. We pre-trained Nemotron 3 Ultra on 20 trillion text tokens, then extended the context length to 1M tokens, and post-trained using Supervised Fine Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Multi-teacher On-Policy Distillation (MOPD). Nemotron 3 Ultra is our most capable model yet, employing multiple key technologies - LatentMoE, Multi Token Prediction (MTP), NVFP4 pre-training, multi-environment RLVR, MOPD, and reasoning budget control. Nemotron 3 Ultra achieves up to ~6x higher inference throughput as compared to state-of-the-art publicly available LLMs while attaining on-par accuracy. The state-of-the-art accuracy, high inference throughput, and 1M token context length make Nemotron 3 Ultra ideal for long-running autonomous agentic tasks. We open-source the base, post-trained, and quantized checkpoints, along with the training data and recipe on HuggingFace.

</details>


### [25] [Are Online Skill and Memory Modules Always Worth Their Tokens? A Budget-Constrained Study of Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15017)
*Sina Hajimiri,Masih Aminbeidokhti,Jose Dolz,Ismail Ben Ayed,Issam H. Laradji,Spandana Gella,Nicolas Gontier*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了在线网页智能体的增强模块（记忆、工作流、技能）的价值，发现在固定推理预算下，一个使用相同预算进行额外推理步骤的普通基线模型，其表现可匹敌甚至超越这些增强方法，表明增强模块的表面收益在预算匹配后往往消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前在线网页智能体普遍通过记忆、工作流或技能等模块对基础模型进行增强，但这些模块在每次任务中都会消耗额外的推理token，而文献中很少将这一开销与模型推理成本一并报告。作者认为，在固定总推理预算的约束下，增强方法的真正收益值得重新审视。

Method: 作者在三个WebArena领域（Reddit、GitLab、电商）和三个模型（Gemini 3 Flash、GPT-5.4-mini、Qwen 3.6-27B）上，将AWM、ASI、ReasoningBank三种增强方法与一个token匹配的普通基线进行对比。该基线不使用任何增强模块，但享有与增强方法相同的总推理预算，用于执行额外的模型推理步骤。此外，还在WorkArena-L1上以Qwen 3.6-27B验证了结论在知识工作类任务中的可推广性。研究还分析了运行间方差对评估结果的影响。

Result: 在聚合成功率上，token匹配的普通基线在所有三个WebArena领域和三个模型上均达到或超越三种增强方法，且通常使用更少的总token。在WorkArena-L1上也观察到类似趋势。增强方法仅在特定领域表现出有限的局部优势。此外，运行间方差对结果有实质性影响。

Conclusion: 在线增强模块带来的表面增益往往源自定义者享有的额外推理预算，而非增强机制本身。在固定预算下，直接让基础模型多做几步推理通常效果相当或更好。建议将运行间方差作为在线网页智能体评估的核心标准之一，并呼吁社区在报告增强方法时同时披露token开销。

Abstract: Online web agents often augment a base actor with memory, workflow, or skill modules. These modules can improve performance, but they also consume test-time tokens, a cost rarely reported alongside the actor's inference cost. We study online augmentation, where this overhead is paid on every task, and re-evaluate its benefits under a fixed total inference budget. We compare AWM, ASI, and ReasoningBank with a token-matched vanilla baseline that uses the same budget for additional actor steps. Across three WebArena domains and three models, Gemini 3 Flash, GPT-5.4-mini, and Qwen 3.6-27B, the vanilla baseline matches or surpasses all three augmentation methods in aggregate success rate while often using fewer total tokens. We observe a similar trend on WorkArena-L1 with Qwen 3.6-27B, indicating that the effect extends to enterprise knowledge-work tasks. Our results suggest that skills and workflow memory can be useful in specific domains, but their apparent gains often vanish against a budget-matched actor. We further show that run-to-run variance materially affects outcomes and should be reported as a core evaluation criterion for online web agents.

</details>


### [26] [Deep Temporal Modeling and Ensemble Fusion for Multimodal Emotion Recognition from Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15026)
*Desta Haileselassie Hagos,Saurav Keshari Aryal,Patrick Ymele-Leki,Anietie Andy,Legand L. Burge*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文在WESAD数据集上系统评估了LSTM、TCN和Transformer三种深度学习模型在多模态生理情感识别中的表现，通过消融实验和传感器/模型融合策略，发现Transformer在多模态设置下最优，TCN在仅手腕信号下最优，而集成方法取得了最高准确率98.91%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生理压力与情绪识别对健康监测和情感计算至关重要，但现有研究缺乏对不同深度学习架构在多模态可穿戴传感器数据上的系统比较，以及传感器融合与模型集成策略的深入分析。

Method: 使用WESAD数据集（包含手腕和胸部传感器信号），对比LSTM、TCN、Transformer三种模型；进行消融实验（仅手腕/仅胸部）；实施后期融合集成策略（组合三种架构的多模态预测）和早期传感器级融合（拼接手腕与胸部信号后输入模型）。

Result: Transformer在多模态输入下准确率最高；TCN在仅手腕配置下表现最佳；集成方法获得最高整体准确率98.91±0.13%和宏观F1分数98.56±0.17%。

Conclusion: 传感器融合与基于集成的模型融合对构建鲁棒的生理情绪识别系统十分有效，多模态结合多架构集成可显著提升识别性能。

Abstract: Physiological stress and emotion recognition are important for health monitoring and affective computing. In this work, we present a comprehensive evaluation of deep learning models such as Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), Temporal Convolutional Networks (TCN), and Transformer on the WESAD dataset for multimodal affect recognition using wrist and chest sensor signals. We perform ablation studies to assess the individual contributions of each modality by training models on wrist-only and chest-only inputs. In addition, we implement a late-fusion ensemble strategy that combines predictions from all three architectures trained on multimodal input. We also employ early fusion at the sensor level by concatenating wrist and chest signals before feeding them into each model. Our results show that Transformer models consistently achieve the highest accuracy in multimodal settings, while TCN models perform best in the wrist-only configuration. The ensemble method yields the highest overall accuracy (98.91 +/- 0.13%) and macro-F1 score (98.56 +/- 0.17%). These findings demonstrate the effectiveness of sensor fusion and ensemble-based fusion in developing robust systems for physiological emotion recognition.

</details>


### [27] [ReportQA: QA-Based Radiology Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15037)
*Yiming Shi,Shaoshuai Yang,Xi Chen,Haolin Li,Hengyu Zhang,Che Jiang,Kaiwen Wang,Xun Zhu,Dong Xie,Fei Wang,Dejing Dou,Miao Li,Ji Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ReportQA，一种面向放射学报告生成的临床相关且灵活的评价框架。该框架通过构建知识树、利用LLM提取结构化信息并生成QA对，引入QAScore指标，相比现有指标与放射科医生判断更具一致性，并揭示了当前报告推断范式的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告评价指标存在局限：自然语言生成指标缺乏临床相关性；临床效能（CE）指标虽关注重要医学发现，但仅覆盖有限实体集，且依赖人工标注难以扩展。放射学报告在临床实践中本质上是信息传递媒介，临床医生据此执行下游诊断任务。基于此洞察，需要一个临床相关、灵活且可扩展的评价框架。

Method: 1) 收集覆盖多模态、多解剖区域的影像数据集；2) 在放射科医生指导下构建临床实体与属性的知识树，利用大语言模型（LLM）从原始报告中提取结构化信息；3) 基于预定义模板生成QA对，并通过自过滤和基于报告的过滤进行质量控制；4) 评估时将报告作为上下文，LLM作为评判模型回答QA对，基于QA准确率计算QAScore指标。

Result: QAScore与放射科医生判断的对齐度优于现有指标。在多个SOTA视觉语言模型上的实验表明：当前基于报告的推断范式难以学习细粒度临床表征，并表现出强烈的负面先验偏差；而问题驱动的推断是更有效的替代方案。

Conclusion: ReportQA及QAScore为放射学报告生成系统提供了临床相关、灵活且可扩展的细粒度定量分析框架。问题驱动的推断范式优于传统的报告驱动推断。为促进可复现性和扩展性，作者开源了知识树、结构化报告、QA对及全套pipeline代码。

Abstract: Radiology report evaluation is essential for advancing automated report generation. Natural language generation metrics have limited clinical relevance. Clinical efficacy (CE) metrics evaluate important medical findings, but focus mainly on presence and cover only a limited set of entities. Due to heavy reliance on manual annotations, it is difficult for CE metrics to extend clinical entities or attributes. In clinical practice, radiology reports serve as a medium for information transfer. Clinicians use them to perform downstream diagnostic tasks without directly inspecting images. Based on this insight, we propose ReportQA, a clinical-related and flexible radiology report evaluation framework, supporting detailed quantitative analysis of radiology report generation systems. We first collect datasets covering multiple imaging modalities and anatomical regions. We then construct knowledge trees of clinical entities and attributes with radiologist guidance, and use large language models (LLMs) to extract structured information from raw reports. Next, we generate QA pairs from predefined templates and apply quality control through self-filtering and report-based filtering. During evaluation, the report is treated as context, and an LLM acts as a judge model to answer the QA pairs. Based on the resulting QA accuracy, we introduce QAScore metric. Compared with existing metrics, QAScore shows better alignment with radiologist judgments. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art vision-language models reveal that current report-based inference paradigms struggle to learn fine-grained clinical representations and exhibit strong negative prior biases. In contrast, question-driven inference provides a more effective alternative. For reproducibility and extensibility, we release the knowledge trees, structured reports, and QA pairs, along with the pipeline code for QA construction and evaluation.

</details>


### [28] [Equity with Efficiency: An Empirical Study of Tokenizers for Multilingual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15044)
*Kieron Seven Jun Wei Lee,Muhammad Reza Qorib,Andrew Ivan Soegeng,Hwee Tou Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次对东南亚11种语言的多语言LLM分词器进行了系统比较，评估了压缩效率、跨语言公平性及下游任务表现。结果表明，Parity-aware BPE在效率-公平权衡上达到帕累托最优，形态驱动字节编码在语义推理上表现最佳，而Byte Latent Transformer在低资源场景下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流多语言LLM使用的字节级BPE分词器在结构上偏袒高资源语言和拉丁字母，导致东南亚等代表性不足的语言面临推理成本膨胀和跨语言能力差距扩大的问题。亟需系统评估各类公平分词器在实际任务中的表现。

Method: 构建涵盖11种东南亚语言的统一基准测试，从分词器层面的压缩效率和跨语言公平性两个维度进行分析，并通过在相同训练数据上训练1.5B参数的语言模型来评估下游任务表现，比较Parity-aware BPE、Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding和Byte Latent Transformer三种分词策略。

Result: Parity-aware BPE处于效率-公平权衡的帕累托前沿，以竞争性成本实现强压缩公平性；Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding通过更丰富的形态表征获得最佳语义推理性能，但计算开销更高；Byte Latent Transformer在下游任务中表现不佳，可能因其架构假设与有限低资源训练数据的约束不匹配。

Conclusion: 跨语言公平性与分词效率并非根本对立。研究为设计公平的多语言模型提供了实践指导：可根据具体需求在Parity-aware BPE（效率优先）和形态驱动编码（语义性能优先）之间做出选择。

Abstract: Multilingual large language models (LLMs) depend on subword tokenization to bridge discrete text and continuous neural representation. State-of-the-art multilingual LLMs often use Byte-level Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) tokenizers that structurally favor high-resource languages and Latin scripts. For speakers of underrepresented languages, particularly those across Southeast Asia, this bias inflates inference costs and widens cross-lingual capability gaps. We present the first systematic comparison of equitable tokenizers on a unified benchmark spanning 11 Southeast Asian languages. Beyond tokenizer-level analysis of compression efficiency and cross-lingual equity, we assess downstream task performance through controlled 1.5B-parameter language model training using the same training data. Our results show that Parity-aware BPE lies on the Pareto frontier of the efficiency-equity trade-off, achieving strong compression parity at competitive cost. Morphology-Driven Byte Encoding delivers the best semantic reasoning performance through morphologically richer representations, albeit at a higher computational expense. Byte Latent Transformer underperforms on downstream tasks, possibly because its architectural assumptions misalign with the constraints of limited low-resource training data. Together, our findings demonstrate that cross-lingual fairness and tokenization efficiency are not fundamentally at odds, and offer practical guidance for designing equitable multilingual models.

</details>


### [29] [A Practical Evaluation Method for Long-Form Simultaneous Speech-to-Speech Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15059)
*Yulin Xue,Siqi Ouyang,Lei Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向长格式连续语音的SimulS2ST实用评估方法，利用ASR、强制对齐和句子嵌入对齐技术恢复目标语音的时间戳并与源句匹配，从而计算句子级延迟（YAAL）和质量（xCOMET）指标并聚合为系统级分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SimulS2ST评估主要针对短语音或预分段语音，无法适用于长格式连续输入；同时，先前方法难以复现且其假设在端到端系统中不成立，缺乏实用的长格式SimulS2ST评估方案。

Method: 给定源语音、预分段的源转录文本和参考翻译：①对生成的目标语音运行ASR获取文本；②通过强制对齐恢复token级别的时间戳；③使用基于句子嵌入的对齐器将目标文本与对应源句进行匹配；④在句子级别计算延迟（YAAL）和质量（xCOMET）指标；⑤最终聚合为系统级得分。

Result: 在代表性SimulS2ST系统上的实验表明该方法在实践中有效，并揭示了当前系统在长语音上存在显著的延迟累积问题。

Conclusion: 该评估方法有效填补了长格式SimulS2ST评估的空白，为未来系统优化提供了可靠的评测手段，同时暴露了延迟累积这一亟待解决的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Simultaneous speech-to-speech translation (SimulS2ST) enables real-time cross-lingual communication, but existing evaluation has focused largely on short or pre-segmented speech rather than long-form, continuous input. Prior approaches are difficult to reproduce and make assumptions that do not hold for end-to-end systems. We present a practical evaluation method for long-form SimulS2ST. Given source speech, pre-segmented source transcripts, and reference translations, we run automatic speech recognition (ASR) and forced alignment on the generated target speech to recover token-level timestamps, then apply a sentence-embedding-based aligner to match the target text to its corresponding source sentences. This enables sentence-level computation of latency and quality metrics, including YAAL and xCOMET, which are then aggregated into final system-level scores. Experiments on representative SimulS2ST systems show that the method is effective in practice and reveal that current systems suffer from substantial latency accumulation on long speech.

</details>


### [30] [CoCoGEC: Counterfactual Generation for Robust Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15069)
*Qianyu Wang,Xiaoman Wang,Yuanyuan Liang,Xinyuan Li,Yunshi Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoCoGEC框架，通过生成保持错误模式但改变上下文的反事实实例来增强语法纠错模型的鲁棒性，在多个扰动基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GEC模型对上下文扰动极为敏感，当上下文发生微小变化时性能急剧下降，说明模型未能真正理解不同上下文中的错误模式，存在标签翻转（label flipping）问题。

Method: 提出CoCoGEC反事实生成框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 通过词级和句级上下文改动，生成保持错误模式和句法结构的句内及句间反事实实例；(2) 利用标签翻转检测和GEC互信息（MI）系数筛选高质量反事实实例，用于数据增强训练。

Result: 在扰动后的BEA-19*、CoNLL-14*和TEM-8*数据集上，分别取得+9.9、+11.3和+20.8的绝对F0.5提升，显著优于现有数据增强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过反事实数据增强可有效提升GEC模型对上下文变化的稳定性，CoCoGEC框架为提升GEC系统的鲁棒性和泛化能力提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Grammatical error correction (GEC) systems are usually trained and evaluated on GEC benchmarks, but their performance often drops sharply once the surrounding context is slightly perturbed or extended. This indicates that the existing GEC models usually fail to understand the error patterns in the varying contexts. In this paper, we thoroughly investigate the counterfactuals for GEC tasks, where the subtle changes to the contexts could lead to the label flipping issue. We propose CoCoGEC, a counterfactual generation framework that creates copies of training instances with error-irrelevant contexts altered. Our framework systematically generates counterfactuals by (1) generating intra- and inter-sentence counterfactuals that maintain the error patterns as well as syntax of the original instances by altering the word-level and sentence-level contexts; (2) revising the generated counterfactuals by selecting the instances with flipped labels and high GEC Mutual Information (MI) coefficient. Extensive experiments show that our method substantially improves the stability of GEC models, outperforming a set of data augmentation baselines. Particularly, it could achieve absolute F0.5 gains of +9.9, +11.3, and +20.8 points on the perturbed BEA-19*,CoNLL-14*, and TEM-8* data set.Our code is released at https://github.com/Quinnok/CoCoGEC

</details>


### [31] [Stop When Further Reasoning Won't Help: Attention-State Adaptive Generation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15070)
*Jiakai Li,Ke Qin,Rongzheng Wang,Yizhuo Ma,Qizhi Chen,Muquan Li,Shuang Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ASAG，一种基于注意力分布的训练免、即插即用早期停止方法，用于缓解大型推理模型的过度思考问题，在九个基准上实现了平均准确率提升3.2%、生成token数减少近40%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRMs）通过测试时计算扩展和思维链推理解决复杂问题，但普遍存在"过度思考"（overthinking）现象，即生成大量冗余token，不仅浪费计算资源，还会降低准确率。现有解决方案中，基于训练的方法计算开销大，训练免方法则依赖精心设计的提示词或不可靠的置信度信号，缺乏一种高效、通用且可靠的解决方案。

Method: 从注意力分布的角度研究早期停止问题，提出ASAG（Adaptive Strategy based on Attention for Generation）方法。该方法通过分析模型内部的注意力分布来推断推理状态，并据此自适应调整生成策略（即判断何时应终止推理过程）。ASAG是训练免的、即插即用的框架，可无缝集成到现有LRMs中。

Result: 在九个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，ASAG在主流LRMs（包括DeepSeek-R1-Distill和Qwen3系列等不同参数规模的模型）上均取得了一致性改进。具体而言，在Qwen3-8B上，ASAG在所有推理任务中平均准确率提升3.2%，同时生成token数量减少近40%。

Conclusion: ASAG作为一种训练免、即插即用的方法，能够有效缓解LRMs的过度思考问题，通过注意力分布自适应地控制推理终止时机，在显著减少计算开销的同时提升准确率，具有很好的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: By incorporating test-time compute scaling, large reasoning models (LRMs) can solve complex problems through explicit chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning processes. However, they often suffer from overthinking, resulting in redundant token outputs and degraded accuracy. Current methods to mitigate this issue remain limited: training-based approaches require substantial computational resources, while training-free methods rely on well-crafted prompts or unreliable confidence signals. In this work, we investigate early stopping from the perspective of attention distributions and propose a simple method, ASAG, which infers the model's reasoning state and adaptively adjusts the generation strategy. The proposed framework is training-free and plug-and-play, enabling seamless integration into existing LRMs. Extensive experiments on nine benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements across mainstream LRMs with varying parameter scales, including the DeepSeek-R1-Distill and Qwen3 series. Specifically, ASAG improves average accuracy by 3.2% while reducing the number of generated tokens by nearly 40% across all reasoning tasks on Qwen3-8B.

</details>

Wang,Ziqi Liu,Zitao Xuan,Zixuan Cheng,Zujie Wen,Zuoli Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Ling-2.6和Ring-2.6模型家族，通过架构迁移预训练和大规模后训练，结合混合线性注意力、token效率优化及KPop强化学习框架，构建高效可扩展的智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前智能体系统需要在低延迟响应、强推理能力和实际可部署性之间取得平衡，现有模型难以同时满足这些需求。作者旨在通过模型架构、优化目标、服务系统和智能体训练环境的统一协同设计来解决这一挑战。
