*Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个认知债务的形式化理论：当个体将AI作为第一性原理认知的替代品而非补充品时，未经验证的推理义务存量会不断累积。模型刻画了认知资本与认知债务两个状态变量，揭示了AI采纳如何通过延期成本、外部化与短期生产率增益掩盖系统性风险，并产生认知明斯基时刻、虚假修正循环及去中心化均衡下的过度采纳等六项命题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工具的广泛使用使得个体倾向于跳过基础性推理（第一性原理认知），直接用AI输出替代独立思考。这种行为虽带来短期效率提升，却可能累积大量未经审视的推理义务。作者旨在形式化这一"认知债务"现象，揭示其系统性风险与福利含义，为理解AI对人力资本和认知能力的长期影响提供理论框架。

Method: 构建一个包含两个状态变量（认知资本与认知债务）的代理人模型，采用乘性生产技术，其中认知资本作为抵押品决定AI采纳的回报。通过均衡分析与比较静态分析，论证六项命题，涵盖理性代理人行为、风险内生演化、危机非线性损失、危机后动态、去中心化均衡与异质性代理人等维度。

Result: （1）理性代理人会累积正认知债务，因成本被递延、部分外部化且被短期生产率增益掩盖；（2）平静期压低主观风险评估、提高AI替代强度并加剧杠杆，产生"认知明斯基时刻"——主观风险下降而真实系统性脆弱上升；（3）预期危机损失对总杠杆呈凸性；（4）危机后，产出目标压力可形成"虚假修正循环"，即用更多AI修补AI失败；（5）去中心化均衡因系统性风险、认知公共品与军备竞赛外部性而过度采纳替代性AI；（6）异质性代理人经济中，高认知资本者更密集采纳AI，可能最终使其无辅助认知资本侵蚀到低于初始低技能者水平。

Conclusion: AI作为认知替代品的过度使用将引致系统性认知脆弱性累积，其风险结构与传统金融债务危机高度相似。去中心化市场无法自发实现社会最优，需要通过政策干预（如认知资本维护激励、AI辅助而非替代的使用规范）来抑制认知债务的恶性膨胀与认知能力的不可逆退化。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [49] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了使用可验证的AI辅助推理系统（VGPT-RSI）在黎曼假设（RH）相关问题上取得可认证的形式化进展，而非宣称证明。系统构建并验证了有限RH边界证书及Lagarias路径的有限证书，同时明确指出了未解决的数学瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼假设是数学中悬而未决的核心难题。本文的动机并非宣称证明RH，而是探索AI辅助推理系统能否在严格形式化验证的框架下，对RH相关问题产生可靠的、可检查的部分进展，同时诚实地识别并明确标注剩余的数学障碍，避免过度声明。

Method: 采用"可验证生长物理Transformer与递归自改进"（VGPT-RSI）系统，应用于两项RH邻近认证任务：1）构造有限RH边界证书——在参数化安全下界曲线上建立不等式，使用外舍入区间算术和Arb/FLINT球算术进行数值审计，再于Rocq/CoqInterval中完成参数化定理的形式化检查；2）启动Lagarias路径形式化——将Lagarias判据（RH等价于全局不等式）中的有限量形式化，并生成Coq可检查的有限证书。

Result: 系统成功在两項任务上生成了经过Coq形式化验证的有限证书，证明了VGPT-RSI能够产出可认证的RH邻近形式化进展。同时，系统明确识别出三个尚未解决的数学瓶颈：Lagarias等价性的完全形式化、超出任意有限截断的全局尾部定理证明，以及可能将反例约化到colossally abundant数或相关极值整数的问题。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI系统展示了在RH相关问题上取得可认证形式化进展的能力，能够有效组织证明依赖关系，并在剩余障碍属于真正数学难题时避免过度声明。这一工作为AI辅助形式化数学推理在深层未解问题上的应用提供了范式。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [50] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了IRTS-ToolBench，一个针对不规则时间序列问答的基准测试，包含1700个问题、10种任务类型、13个领域，用于评估LLM和AI代理在不规则时间序列条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列数据绝大多数是不规则的——观测异步、缺失值具有信息性而非随机、采样频率在不同传感器和时间窗口间差异显著。然而现有TSQA基准大多假设规则采样输入，导致对LLM在不规则条件下的能力评估存在根本性空白。

Method: 构建IRTS-ToolBench基准数据集，涵盖1700个问题，横跨10种任务类型和13个领域。设计标准化的输入格式与可复现的评估协议，支持独立使用，适用于任何基于LLM的不规则时间序列分析研究。

Result: 论文提供了完整的基准数据集和评估框架，并开源了代码，但摘要中未报告具体的模型性能对比数据。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench填补了不规则时间序列问答评估领域的空白，为研究者提供了标准化的测试平台和可复现的评估流程，有望推动LLM在不规则时间序列分析方向的研究进展。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [51] [CONCORD: Asynchronous Sparse Aggregation for Device-Cloud RAG under Document Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15179)
*Xuedong Hu,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CONCORD 提出一种异步稀疏聚合框架，用于文档隔离场景下的设备-云端双端 RAG。其核心思想是将云端视为异步到达的证据源而非持续同步的协作者，通过等待债务控制机制和证书引导的最小补充机制，大幅降低通信开销，同时保持生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设备-云端协作的 RAG 方法依赖频繁的远程同步和密集证据传输，在文档隔离（私有文档在设备端、公共知识在云端）且带宽受限的现实条件下，吞吐量受到严重制约。需要一种既能保护隐私又能高效利用云端知识的新方案。

Method: 提出 CONCORD 框架，包含两个关键机制：(1) 等待债务控制（Waiting Debt Control）：根据已观察到的等待回报，动态决定每个解码步是否继续等待远程证据；(2) 证书引导的最小补充机制（Certificate-Guided Minimal Supplementation）：仅请求确定当前贪心决策所必需的远程证据。咨询云端的解码步保持与密集双端聚合相同的贪心 token，其余步骤则在本地完成，无需远程参与。

Result: 在 Natural Questions 和 WikiText-2 数据集上，CONCORD 相较基线方法分别实现了 1.66 倍和 2.15 倍的端到端吞吐量提升，每 token 通信量降低超过两个数量级，同时保持可比的答案质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: CONCORD 成功证明了在文档隔离的双端 RAG 场景下，异步稀疏聚合策略可以在不牺牲生成质量的前提下，显著提升通信效率和系统吞吐量，为隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [52] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogGuard 是一个面向边缘智能服务的主动预警框架，通过解耦离线LLM画像构建与在线SLM评分预测，结合前缀对齐KV缓存复用和长度感知分布式微调策略，在教育和运维场景中显著降低了画像构建时间和微调开销，同时提升了预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能服务需要在严格延迟和隐私约束下进行主动预警，但现有方法存在两大挑战：一是画像方法领域特定、缺乏跨场景的可复用抽象；二是在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型时，由于输入序列长度差异导致同步开销过高。

Method: CogGuard 框架将离线LLM画像构建与在线SLM评分预测解耦，通过共享的静态-动态画像到评分流水线实现；设计场景特定的画像方法并采用前缀对齐KV缓存复用以降低重复编码开销；提出带有对比正则化的长度感知分布式微调策略，缓解异构集群上的负载不均问题。

Result: 在教育和运维数据集上，CogGuard 将画像构建时间降低最多48%，分布式微调时间降低19%；在100分制预警任务上，教育场景MAE为13.4，运维场景MAE为5.9；在最大规模教育场景中，相比最强基线模型预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard 通过解耦架构、KV缓存复用和长度感知分布式微调，有效解决了边缘场景下LLM画像构建与模型对齐的效率与精度问题，展现出良好的跨场景泛化能力和实际部署可行性。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [53] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个将交互式定向广告建模为有噪属性推断谕示的框架，通过合成群体校准的基准测试评估多种攻击方法，结果表明披露策略是最强控制手段，聚合报告可根本性移除推断信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向广告中，当用户交互（如点击）可被广告主观察到并与具体用户关联时，该信道构成了一个"有噪谕示"（noisy oracle），可能被用于推断用户敏感属性。现有隐私研究多关注投放前的资格筛选（eligibility），而忽略了交互后的披露环节，作者旨在填补这一空白，系统建模并量化交互披露带来的属性推断风险。

Method: 1）将定向广告流程形式化分解为四个阶段：定向谓词（targeting predicates）、曝光（exposure）、交互（interaction）、披露（disclosure），明确区分"资格与投放"及"交互与广告主可见性"之间的边界；2）利用公开数据构建可复现的合成群体基准，每个个体带有已知敏感标签，并生成含主题变体和交互先验的广告语义层；3）模拟器生成真实标签、事件轨迹、披露观察及评估指标；4）在统一框架下比较贝叶斯攻击、监督学习攻击、正例与未标记学习（PU learning）攻击及自适应攻击的推断效果；5）评估聚合报告、类型过滤、随机化披露等防御策略的效力。

Result: 在四类主题变体、七种模拟器种子和两种交互设置下：重复广告活动中的身份级曝光可产生可测量但有界的推断信号；进行160次广告活动后，贝叶斯攻击和监督攻击在主设置下AUC约0.64，高交互设置下约0.65；披露策略是最强控制手段——聚合报告消除了与用户关联的谕示输入，类型过滤和随机化披露则降低释放的信号量。

Conclusion: 该工作构建了一个完整的模型、实验构件和防御评估方法论，系统揭示了交互式定向广告中的属性推断隐私风险。核心洞见在于：披露环节（而非仅投放环节）对隐私保护具有决定性作用，聚合报告可从根本上切断基于用户标识的推断信道。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [54] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Visual-Seeker，一个视觉原生多模态深度搜索代理，通过主动视觉推理实现动态视觉证据收集，在多模态搜索基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态大模型在开放世界场景中事实性扎根能力不足，多模态深度搜索代理主要依赖显式语义的简单图像和纯文本证据轨迹，缺乏多跳跨模态推理与搜索能力，视觉原生搜索范式未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker代理，将视觉作为动态输入而非静态输入，主动关注细粒度视觉细节并在搜索过程中动态收集视觉证据；设计了主动视觉推理数据管线，合成5K高质量多模态推理轨迹用于模型训练。

Result: 在五个具有挑战性的多模态搜索基准上取得最先进性能，超越多个闭源商用模型，验证了在真实网络环境中的鲁棒视觉原生推理与搜索能力。

Conclusion: 视觉原生主动推理范式能有效提升多模态搜索代理的事实扎根和跨模态推理能力，Visual-Seeker展现了该方法在实际网络搜索场景中的优越性。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [QPILOTS: Efficient Test-Time Q-Steering for Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14801)
*Yifan Ruan,Chenyang Cao,Andreas Burger,Ali Pesaranghader,Kaveh Kamali,Jaehong Kim,Nandita Vijaykumar,Alan Aspuru-Guzik,Igor Gilitschenski,Nicholas Rhinehart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QPILOTS 是一种在推理时引导扩散/流匹配策略的方法，通过在去噪过程中将中间噪声状态投影到干净动作估计并计算 Critic 梯度，避免了直接通过多步去噪反向传播导致的数值不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配和扩散策略虽表达力强，但用 TD 强化学习优化时，直接通过多步去噪过程反向传播 Critic 梯度会导致数值不稳定。现有方法要么丢弃梯度信息，要么蒸馏为单步 Actor，要么反复微调去噪策略，均存在局限。需要一种无需修改原策略、在推理时即可有效利用 Critic 梯度的方法。

Method: 提出 QPILOTS，在推理时引导去噪过程：在每个去噪步骤，先将噪声中间状态投影为最终干净动作的估计，再在该估计上计算 Critic 梯度（而非在不可靠的噪声中间状态上评估 Critic）。包含两个变体：QPILOTS-U 使用快速单点近似；QPILOTS-M 通过学习的辅助网络抽取可微后验样本。

Result: 在标准 offline-to-online RL 基准上，QPILOTS 在 50 个任务中达到平均 90% 成功率，获得最佳聚合性能。在预训练 VLA 基础模型上，QPILOTS 在 6 个仿真操作任务中优于或匹配先前的推理时方法。

Conclusion: QPILOTS 提供了一种无需修改原始策略的推理时引导方案，有效解决了扩散策略在 RL 中利用 Critic 梯度时的数值稳定性问题，在离线-在线 RL 和大规模 VLA 模型微调两个场景均展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Flow-matching and diffusion policies are expressive action generators, but optimizing them with temporal-difference reinforcement learning (RL) remains difficult. Effective policy extraction requires exploiting the critic's action gradient, yet directly backpropagating this signal through a multi-step denoising process can be numerically unstable. Existing methods work around this either by discarding gradient information, distilling the policy into a simpler one-step actor, or repeatedly fine-tuning the denoising policy as the critic improves. We propose QPILOTS, a method that leaves the original policy unmodified and steers the denoising process at inference time. At each denoising step, instead of evaluating the critic on the noisy intermediate action where critic predictions are unreliable, we first project that intermediate state to an estimate of the final clean action and compute the critic gradient there. We introduce two variants: QPILOTS-U uses a fast single-point approximation, while QPILOTS-M draws differentiable posterior samples via a learned auxiliary network. On a standard offline-to-online RL benchmark, QPILOTS achieves the best aggregate performance, reaching an average success rate of 90% across 50 tasks. We also apply QPILOTS to steer a large, frozen, pretrained Vision-Language Action (VLA) foundation model, outperforming or matching prior inference-time approaches across six manipulation tasks in simulation.

</details>


### [56] [GRAPE: Guided Parameter-Space Evolution for Compact Adversarial Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14865)
*Zhiyuan Ye,Xiangyu Zhou,Ji Qi,Hao Zhang,Yi Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GRAPE框架，通过渐进式参数空间演化替代传统固定结构对抗训练，在匹配计算量下显著提升紧凑模型的鲁棒精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗训练方法从训练开始就固定整个参数空间进行优化，作者质疑这一做法，并探究参数变得可优化的顺序是否会影响最终鲁棒解，即便控制最终架构和计算预算不变。

Method: 提出GRAPE（Guided Parameter-Space Evolution）框架，包含三个阶段：(1) 在当前暴露的参数空间中进行鲁棒优化的稳定化；(2) 逐步释放新的可优化维度；(3) 使用对抗谱利用率得分（adversarial spectral utilization score）引导新释放的容量流向高压模块。整体将鲁棒模型学习视为渐进式参数空间暴露与演化的过程。

Result: 在CIFAR-10的ℓ∞威胁模型下，以固定结构ResNet-18对抗训练为对照：GRAPE将PGD-20鲁棒准确率从51.70%提升至56.94%，FLOPs比率仅1.009x，参数量减少约21.4%。顺序增长变体在相同最终ResNet-18架构下达到56.52% PGD-20鲁棒准确率，表明收益不仅来自最终架构差异，更来自参数空间暴露路径。

Conclusion: 引导式参数空间演化能够在匹配计算量下产生更紧凑且鲁棒的参数配置，参数优化的顺序对最终鲁棒解具有显著影响。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) improves neural network robustness, but most methods train a fixed parameter space from the start. This paper asks whether the order in which parameters become optimizable can affect the final robust solution, even when the final architecture or computation budget is controlled.
  We propose GRAPE, Guided Parameter-Space Evolution, a training framework for compact adversarial robustness. GRAPE combines parameter-space stabilization with progressive hidden expansion: it stabilizes robust optimization in the currently exposed space, gradually releases new optimizable dimensions, and uses an adversarial spectral utilization score to guide newly released capacity toward high-pressure modules. In contrast to fixed-structure AT, GRAPE treats robust model learning as a process of progressive parameter-space exposure and evolution.
  Under the standard $\ell_\infty$ threat model on CIFAR-10, with fixed-structure ResNet-18 AT as a controlled reference, GRAPE improves PGD-20 robust accuracy from 51.70% to 56.94% at a nearly matched computation budget with a FLOPs ratio of 1.009x, while reducing parameter count by about 21.4%. A sequential grow variant with the same final ResNet-18 architecture reaches 56.52% PGD-20 robust accuracy, indicating that the gain is not only due to final architecture differences but also to the parameter-space exposure path. These results suggest that guided parameter-space evolution can yield compact and robust parameter configurations under matched computation.

</details>


### [57] [α-Fair Insurance Pricing: A Fairness Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14898)
*Tianhe Zhang,Xiguang Liu,Peng Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了α-FISP（α-公平个体偿付保费）框架，通过约束优化将精算公平与团结公平之间的权衡参数化为一个连续谱，在保证偿付能力的前提下，使决策者能够根据监管需求选择公平性折中点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保险定价中长期存在精算公平（按风险差异化定价）与团结公平（跨群体交叉补贴）之间的根本性张力。随着颗粒度数据使风险细分日益精细，以及监管对弱势群体保护压力增大，亟需一个能显式刻画这一权衡并保证偿付能力的统一框架。

Method: 将保险定价建模为约束优化问题：以精算公平保费为基准，在每个风险类别内施加交叉补贴的预算约束，通过参数α控制两类公平性的权衡，生成从纯精算定价到纯团结定价的连续解族（α-FISP框架），并提供理论保证。

Result: 数值实验表明，α-FISP框架在计算上可处理，且能与美国各州异质性公平要求的监管制度良好对齐，证明了该框架的实用性和灵活性。

Conclusion: α-FISP框架为保险定价中精算公平与团结公平的权衡提供了形式化、可操作的解决方案，使监管者和保险公司能够在公平性谱系上选择适宜的运行点，同时满足偿付能力这一保险运营的基本要求。

Abstract: Fairness in insurance pricing remains a long-standing and deeply debated puzzle. On one hand, insurers, driven by profitability considerations, set premiums that differentiate across individual risks to achieve actuarial fairness. On the other hand, insurance serves a critical societal function by pooling risks across a population, motivating cross-subsidization among groups to promote solidarity fairness. The tension between these two competing notions of fairness makes insurance pricing inherently complex, particularly in modern settings where granular data allow for increasingly fine risk differentiation and regulators face growing pressure to protect vulnerable groups. To address this challenge, we propose an $α$-\textbf{F}air \textbf{I}ndividual \textbf{S}olvent \textbf{P}remium ($α$-FISP) framework for insurance pricing that explicitly captures the trade-off between actuarial and solidarity fairness while guaranteeing solvency, a fundamental requirement in insurance operations. We formulate the pricing problem as a constrained optimization task, where actuarially fair premiums are adjusted subject to budget constraints on cross-subsidization within each risk class. This formulation naturally yields a family of solutions parameterized by $α$, tracing a continuum between purely actuarial and purely solidarity-based pricing and enabling decision-makers to select an operating point along this fairness spectrum. We derive theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework. Numerical experiments show that $α$-FISP is computationally tractable and aligns well with the U.S. regulatory regimes featuring heterogeneous state-level fairness requirements.

</details>


### [58] [GRASP: Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer for Memory-Efficient Multi-Source Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14900)
*Mary Isabelle Wisell,Nicholas Jacobs,Aayush Manandhar,Salimeh Yasaei Sekeh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRASP 提出了一种面向多源迁移学习的序列化参数融合方法，通过逐源合并、梯度对齐选择与迭代微调，在仅需 O(1) 内存的条件下实现 93.5% 的平均准确率，显著优于传统集成方法的 71.7%，解决了多源迁移中的内存瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多源迁移学习方法存在根本性的可扩展瓶颈：参数融合时需要将全部 K 个源模型同时加载到内存（O(K) 内存），或推理时需部署所有模型，这在资源受限环境中不可行。本文旨在实现 O(1) 恒定内存消耗下的高质量多源知识整合。

Method: GRASP 包含三项核心创新：(1) 序列化处理——每次仅合并一个源模型到持续演化的目标模型中；(2) 参数级梯度对齐——通过分析各参数的优化方向是否与目标域对齐，选择性迁移梯度方向一致的参数，避免负迁移；(3) 迭代微调——在整合下一个源之前先对已迁移知识进行适应性微调。

Result: 在 Yearbook、CLEAR-10、CLEAR-100 三个持续学习基准（覆盖 10 至 108 年的时间分布偏移）及四种架构（1.3M 至 25.6M 参数）上的实验表明，GRASP 在所有数据集和架构上达到 93.5% 的平均准确率，远超集成方法的 71.7%，且内存恒定为 O(1)，而标准多源融合需 K 倍模型内存。

Conclusion: GRASP 的序列化合并机制使其可扩展到任意数量的源域而内存不增长，特别适用于资源受限部署和持续演化的源域场景，为多源迁移学习的实际生产部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Multi-source transfer learning faces a fundamental scalability bottleneck: existing approaches require either loading all K source models into memory simultaneously during parameter fusion, requiring O(K) memory, or deploying all models at inference time, making production deployment infeasible. We propose GRASP (Gradient-Aligned Sequential Parameter Transfer), which achieves superior knowledge integration while maintaining O(1) memory consumption through three key innovations: (1) sequential processing that merges one source at a time into an evolving target model, (2) parameter-wise gradient alignment that selectively transfers only parameters whose optimization directions align with the target domain, avoiding negative transfer, and (3) iterative fine-tuning that adapts transferred knowledge before integrating the next source. Extensive experiments across three continual learning benchmarks (Yearbook, CLEAR-10, CLEAR-100) spanning 10 to 108-year temporal distribution shifts and four architectures (1.3M to 25.6M parameters) demonstrate that GRASP achieves 93.5% mean accuracy over all datasets and architectures compared to ensemble method's 71.7% accuracy while requiring only constant memory versus K models for standard multi-source fusion. Critically, GRASP's sequential previously merged models and scales to arbitrarily many sources without memory growth, making it uniquely suitable for resource-constrained deployment and continually evolving source domains.

</details>


### [59] [Policy Regret for Embedding Model Routing: Contextual Bandits with Low-Rank Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14929)
*Yan Dai,Negin Golrezaei,Patrick Jaillet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将嵌入模型路由建模为具有低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性赌博机问题，识别出标准遗憾概念的不足，提出对数二次策略类及Hypentropy策略梯度算法（HPG），实现$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(s\sqrt{MT})$的线性化策略遗憾，避免了维度灾难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代推荐系统需动态路由多样化查询至多个嵌入模型，但该问题在对抗性查询、赌博机反馈及模型有限可观测性等现实条件下缺乏理论理解。现有方法面临结构性误设或统计不可处理性。

Method: 将问题形式化为具有低秩专家的对抗性上下文线性赌博机：上下文为查询，动作为物品，专家为工作于低秩隐表示空间的嵌入模型。首先识别标准遗憾度量的问题，提出对数二次策略类（兼具表达力与高效在线学习能力）。进而提出Hypentropy策略梯度算法（HPG），在不完全信息下可证明地适应未知低秩结构，并给出计算高效、无参数的实现。

Result: HPG算法达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(s\sqrt{MT})$的线性化策略遗憾界，其中$s$为专家内在秩，$M$为模型数，$T$为轮数，有效避免了维度灾难。

Conclusion: 通过引入低秩结构假设和对数二次策略类，结合HPG算法，首次在对抗性上下文线性赌博机框架下为嵌入模型路由问题提供了既可证明有效、又计算可行的解决方案，为推荐系统的动态模型选择奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Modern recommendation systems increasingly rely on dynamically routing diverse queries to multiple embedding models. Despite its practical significance, this problem remains poorly understood under realistic conditions like adversarial queries, bandit feedback, and limited observability of models. We formalize embedding model routing as an adversarial contextual linear bandit with low-rank experts, where contexts are queries, actions are items, and experts are the embedding models working on low-rank latent representation spaces. We first establish that standard regret notions suffer from structural misspecification or statistical intractability, and we identify a log-quadratic policy class that is expressive enough to capture query-dependent model routing, yet structured enough to allow efficient online learning. Second, we propose a policy gradient algorithm called Hypentropy Policy Gradient (HPG). It provably adapts to the unknown low-rank structure under incomplete information and attains $\tilde{\mathcal O}(s\sqrt{M T})$ linearized policy regret -- where $s, M$, and $T$ are the intrinsic rank of the experts, the number of models, and the number of rounds -- thus avoiding a curse of dimensionality. Finally, we also provide an computationally efficient and parameter-free implementation of HPG.

</details>


### [60] [Separable Neural Architectures as Physical World Models: from Mathematical Theory to Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14934)
*Reza T Batley,Andrew Kichline,Sourav Saha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出可分离神经架构(SNA)，将神经网络逼近与张量分解相结合，利用变分框架求解高维时空-参数PDE。通过完全因子化的交替最小二乘(ALS)优化，将计算代价降至维度线性增长，在7D参数化制造仿真中实现百万次蒙特卡洛扫描仅需102秒，相对有限元基线加速15万倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时空-参数偏微分方程(PDE)面临维度灾难，传统方法计算代价随维度指数增长。需要一个既能保持变分法理论保证（适定性、拟最优性、收敛性），又具有紧凑光滑归纳偏置，且计算代价随维度代数增长的表示架构。

Method: 提出SNA表示类，将局部坐标函数(atom)与由稀疏低秩交互对象控制的全局交互解耦；在变分SNA(VSNA)框架下作为Galerkin试验空间，满足Lax-Milgram定理下的变分保证；采用完全因子化的张量原生交替最小二乘(ALS)优化框架，将计算代价降至维度线性增长。

Result: 在椭圆、双曲和抛物型系统中验证了与预测的代数及谱缩放率高度一致。7D参数化制造仿真中，百万次蒙特卡洛扫描在标准笔记本CPU上仅用102秒，相比NVIDIA A100 GPU上的全网格有限元基线实现150,000倍加速。热-属性反演管线实现100ms内的实时生成式逆向重建。

Conclusion: SNA作为连续参数流形的紧凑数学基底，能够实现实时反演、优化循环和快速不确定性传播，展示了其作为"一次求解、随处查询"物理世界模型的能力。

Abstract: This work introduces the Separable Neural Architecture (SNA), a function representational class combining neural approximation with tensor decomposition. The SNA decouples localized coordinate functions (atoms) from global interactions governed by a sparse, low-rank interaction object. This architecture possesses a compact and smooth inductive bias well-suited for solving partial differential equations (PDEs). When viewed as a Galerkin trial space under the variational SNA (VSNA) framework, the formulation satisfies classical variational guarantees under Lax-Milgram: well-posedness, quasi-optimality, convergence, and stability. In high-dimensional spatiotemporal--parametric PDEs, the VSNA mitigates the curse of dimensionality by scaling algebraically rather than exponentially. Exploiting an entirely factorized, tensor-native alternating least squares (ALS) optimization framework reduces this cost to linear in dimension. The VSNA is validated across elliptic, hyperbolic, and parabolic systems, demonstrating close alignment with predicted algebraic and spectral scaling rates. We showcase the SNA as a "solve once, query anywhere" physical world model via two engineering case studies: a 7D parametric manufacturing simulation and an experimental thermal-to-property inversion pipeline for Inconel 718. The VSNA executes a 1,000,000-query Monte Carlo sweep in 102s on a standard laptop CPU, yielding a 150,000x speedup over a full-grid finite element baseline hosted on an NVIDIA A100 GPU. It further enables real-time generative inverse-mode reconstructions under 100ms. These results demonstrate that the SNA serves as a compact mathematical substrate for continuous parameter manifolds to enable real-time inversion, optimization loops, and rapid uncertainty propagation.

</details>


### [61] [Remember, Don't Re-read: Stateful ReAct Agents for Token-Efficient Autonomous Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14945)
*Faramarz Jabbarvaziri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将无状态的autoresearch模式重构为基于LangGraph的有状态ReAct智能体，通过持久化状态携带实验历史，在超参数调优和代码优化两个基准测试上分别节省90%和52%的token消耗，同时保持相当的优化质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无状态autoresearch模式在每次迭代时从零开始重建实验上下文，导致每轮$O(n)$的token开销和总计$O(n^2)$的复杂度。作者旨在通过引入有状态设计来解决这一结构性的token浪费问题。

Method: 将autoresearch模式重新设计为基于LangGraph的有状态ReAct智能体。通过类型化的持久状态（typed persistent state），利用工具调用接口（tool-calling interface）在迭代间传递实验历史，使智能体在固定大小的对话窗口内运行，实现$O(1)$的每轮token开销。

Result: 在超参数调优（15轮迭代）中，有状态智能体消耗2,492个token，对比无状态方案的24,465个token，减少90%。在代码性能优化（40轮迭代）中，有状态智能体消耗627K token，对比1,275K token，减少52%，且两项任务的优化质量与无状态方案相当。

Conclusion: token减少具有结构性优势——有状态智能体将每轮$O(n)$的上下文重建成本降低为$O(1)$的固定窗口成本。文章提供了足够详细的架构描述，便于实践者实现自己的有状态autoresearch工作流。

Abstract: The autoresearch pattern enables autonomous experimentation by having a large language model (LLM) iteratively modify code to optimize a target metric. Its stateless design, however, reconstructs experimental context from scratch at every iteration, incurring $O(n)$ token cost per iteration and $O(n^{2})$ total. This work reformulates the pattern as a stateful ReAct agent using LangGraph, where typed persistent state carries experimental history across iterations via a tool-calling interface. Two benchmarks are evaluated: hyperparameter tuning (15 iterations, small per-iteration observations) and code performance optimization (40 iterations, large per-iteration observations containing full source code and benchmark results). On hyperparameter tuning, the stateful agent consumes 90\% fewer tokens (2{,}492 vs.\ 24{,}465). On code optimization, the stateful agent consumes 52\% fewer tokens (627K vs.\ 1{,}275K) while achieving comparable optimization quality on both tasks. The token reduction is structural: the stateless agent re-reads the full history at $O(n)$ cost per iteration, while the stateful agent operates within a fixed-size conversation window at $O(1)$ cost. This paper describes the architecture in sufficient detail for practitioners to implement a stateful autoresearch agent for their own workflows.

</details>


### [62] [A Comparative Study of Graph Neural Network Layer Selection for Interaction Modelling in Driving Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14956)
*George Daoud,Mohamed El-Darieby*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对19种图神经网络层在轨迹预测任务中的时空建模能力进行了系统对比，发现ARMA、Chebyshev和拓扑感知层表现最佳，并总结出求和聚合优于均值聚合、多头注意力增强交互、差异化跳数权重等实用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在轨迹预测中的应用日益广泛，但GNN架构设计缺乏标准化指导，鲜有研究系统对比不同图层在空间交互和时间动态建模上的有效性，导致研究者难以选择最优架构。

Method: 作者对19种图神经网络层类型进行了详细的比较研究。在统一的超参数设定下，从空间处理能力和时间处理能力两个维度评估各层组合的性能。通过系统实验对比各图层在轨迹预测基准上的表现，归纳出高性能架构的共同特征。

Result: 在探索的超参数范围内，五种层组合表现突出：ARMA、Chebyshev以及拓扑感知层持续优于其他方案。具体发现包括：基于求和的聚合方法优于基于均值的聚合；多头注意力机制能实现更丰富的交互；为不同跳数距离分配差异化权重可显著提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 该研究为轨迹预测中GNN架构的设计提供了实用指导原则，有助于构建更可解释、更有效的轨迹预测模型，推动了GNN在该领域从经验性设计向有据可循的规范化设计转变。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems rely on precise trajectory prediction to plan safe and efficient movement. Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have become a promising approach for modelling spatiotemporal interactions among road agents. However, designing GNN architectures for trajectory prediction remains non-standardized, with little guidance on which graph layers effectively capture spatial interactions and temporal dynamics. This paper offers a detailed comparative study of 19 graph layer types, focusing on their spatial and temporal processing capabilities to discover the most effective architectures for trajectory prediction. Within the explored hyperparameter setting, we highlight five standout layer combinations, with ARMA, Chebyshev, and topology-aware layers consistently performing better than others. Beyond performance metrics, our findings yield practical design principles: sum-based aggregation is more effective than mean-based methods, multi-head attention mechanisms enable richer interactions, and assigning different weights to different hop distances significantly improves prediction accuracy. These findings offer useful guidance for designing more interpretable and effective trajectory prediction models.

</details>


### [63] [Leveraging Physiological Signals to Predict Exam Outcomes with Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14960)
*Lala Yamazaki,Ramchandra Rimal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用考试期间的生理数据（皮电活动、心率、皮肤温度），对比了逻辑回归、随机森林、SVM与Transformer、LSTM、GRU等多种机器学习模型在预测考试成绩上的表现。结果表明，深度学习模型擅长捕捉复杂关系，但随机森林等简单模型在兼顾性能的同时更具计算效率和可解释性；Transformer展现出与LSTM/GRU相当的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索生理应激指标与学业表现之间的关联，利用机器学习从生理数据中预测考试结果，从而理解影响学生心理健康的压力因素，促进学生福祉与学业成效。

Method: 采集考试期间的皮电活动、心率、皮肤温度等生理应激指标。采用多种机器学习模型进行对比：传统模型包括逻辑回归、随机森林、支持向量机；深度学习模型包括Transformer、LSTM、GRU。以准确率、精确率、召回率、F1-score等标准指标评估模型性能，重点关注Transformer处理数值型数据的适应性。

Result: 深度学习模型在捕捉生理数据中的复杂关系方面整体表现优异；但随机森林等简单模型在部分场景下性能更优，且兼具计算效率与可解释性；Transformer模型表现与LSTM和GRU相当，展示了其在数值型数据上的通用性。

Conclusion: 应根据问题目标在模型精度、效率与可解释性之间权衡，尝试广泛的模型类别。该研究阐明了生理信号与学业表现之间的关系，为利用生理数据改善学生心理健康和学业成果提供了支持。

Abstract: This study investigates the application of machine learning models to predict exam outcomes using physiological data collected during examination sessions. Physiological stress indicators, including electrodermal activity, heart rate, and skin temperature, were analyzed to uncover their association with academic performance. A variety of machine learning approaches were employed, ranging from standard models like logistic regression, random forest, and support vector machines to more advanced architectures, including transformers, long short-term memory (LSTM), and gated recurrent unit (GRU) models. This diversity aimed to capture the complex interactions within the data effectively. A key focus was assessing the adaptability of transformers in processing numerical data and evaluating their performance in this novel context. Standard performance metrics, such as accuracy, precision, recall, and F1-score, were used to compare model efficacy. The experimental results demonstrate that while deep learning models generally excel at capturing complex relationships in physiological data, simpler models like random forests can sometimes achieve superior performance while offering computational efficiency and interpretability. Furthermore, transformers demonstrated notable versatility, showcasing performances comparable to those of the LSTM and GRU models. This research underscores the importance of experimenting with a broad class of models that align with the objectives of the problem at hand, balancing precision, efficiency, and interpretability. By elucidating the relationships between physiological signals and academic performance, this study contributes to understanding stressors affecting students' mental health. It further promotes leveraging physiological data to enhance student well-being and academic outcomes.

</details>


### [64] [Benchmarking Instance-Dependent Label Noise with Controlled Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14965)
*Shadman Islam,Agustinus Kristiadi,Mostafa Milani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CILN框架，通过可控的输入损坏生成实例依赖的标签噪声基准，揭示噪声结构（而非仅噪声率）对算法行为的关键影响，并暴露流行方法的失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成实例依赖标签噪声（IDN）基准依赖不完美标注者或分类器评分者生成噪声，导致模糊来源不明确，无法系统性研究不同噪声结构对算法的影响。

Method: 提出CILN框架，通过对输入施加可控损坏（如模糊、遮挡等）构建多样化投票池，由受损实例的标注投票产生标签，使模糊来源和严重程度显式可控。在CIFAR10、MNIST、Adult上构建90种基准设置，覆盖多种损坏族和严重级别。

Result: （1）生成的基准呈现真实的实例依赖噪声和多样化混淆结构；（2）在CIFAR-10上产生的标签分布比现有合成IDN基准更接近人类不确定性；（3）暴露了Co-Teaching和DivideMix等方法在可比噪声率下未被观察到的失效模式，证明噪声结构对基准难度和算法行为的重要性。

Conclusion: CILN通过显式可控的模糊生成，为研究不同实例难度来源下的噪声标签学习提供了互补的基准框架，强调噪声结构应成为算法评估的核心维度。

Abstract: Synthetic instance-dependent label noise (IDN) benchmarks are widely used to evaluate noisy-label learning methods, yet existing approaches typically generate noise through imperfect annotators or classifier raters, leaving the source of ambiguity implicit. We introduce CILN, a benchmark generation framework that creates IDN through controlled input corruptions. A diverse voter pool labels corrupted instances, producing benchmark datasets in which both the source and severity of ambiguity are explicit and controllable. Using CIFAR10, MNIST, and Adult, we construct 90 benchmark settings spanning multiple corruption families and severity levels. Our experiments show that the resulting benchmarks exhibit genuine instance-dependent noise, provide diverse confusion structures, and, on CIFAR-10, can produce label distributions that are closer to human uncertainty than an existing synthetic IDN benchmark. We further demonstrate that corruption-mediated IDN can expose failure modes of popular noisy-label learning methods, including Co-Teaching and DivideMix, that are not observed under comparable levels of rater-fallibility noise. These findings suggest that noise structure, not only noise rate, plays an important role in benchmark difficulty and algorithm behavior. By making ambiguity generation explicit and controllable, CILN provides a complementary benchmarking framework for studying noisy-label learning under diverse sources of instance difficulty.

</details>


### [65] [Zero-order Parameter-free Optimization for LMO-based Methods: Novel Approach for Efficient Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14970)
*Dmitriy Bystrov,Daniil Medyakov,Dmitry Bylinkin,Aleksandr Beznosikov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaNAGED方法，将零阶优化、无参数自适应调参和基于线性最小化预言机的非欧几何更新统一起来，用于大规模LLM微调，并在OPT-1.3B模型上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模LLM微调中，反向传播内存开销巨大；零阶优化虽节省内存，但对步长和平滑参数高度敏感，需昂贵的逐任务调参。此外，大规模微调还需考虑参数块的异质结构，利用几何感知更新。因此需要一种能自动调参、内存高效且能处理非欧几何结构的优化方法。

Method: 提出AdaNAGED方法，将无参数（PF）自适应策略与基于线性最小化预言机（LMO）的零阶（ZO）优化相结合。具体地，在LMO-based ZO框架中引入自适应步长和平滑参数机制，无需问题依赖常数的先验知识，同时通过LMO实现非欧几何空间中的参数更新。论文还建立了该方法的收敛性理论保证。

Result: 在OPT-1.3B模型的大规模LLM微调任务上验证了AdaNAGED方法，证明其能够有效进行梯度无关训练，自适应调节参数，并利用非欧更新几何实现高效微调。同时提供了收敛性理论分析。

Conclusion: AdaNAGED成功统一了梯度无关训练、自适应调参与非欧几何更新，为大规模LLM内存高效微调提供了新的解决方案，兼具理论保证和实证效果。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models (LLMs) has become a central application of modern optimization, enabling pretrained models to adapt to diverse downstream tasks and domain-specific data. A major obstacle in large-scale fine-tuning is the memory overhead of backpropagation, which requires storing activations, gradients, and optimizer states. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a memory-efficient alternative, but its performance is highly sensitive to the stepsize and smoothing parameter, often requiring costly task-specific tuning. Parameter-free (PF) optimization addresses this issue by adapting algorithmic parameters without prior knowledge of problem-dependent constants. Moreover, large-scale fine-tuning can benefit from geometry-aware updates that account for the heterogeneous structure of parameter blocks, which can be modeled through methods that exploit linear minimization oracle (LMO). In this work, we study PF adaptation for LMO-based ZO optimization and introduce $\texttt{AdaNAGED}$, a method that unifies gradient-free training, adaptive tuning, and non-Euclidean update geometry. We establish convergence guarantees and validate the method on large-scale LLM fine-tuning task with $\texttt{OPT}-1.3\mathrm{B}$ model.

</details>


### [66] [FastMix: Fast Data Mixture Optimization via Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14971)
*Haoru Tan,Sitong Wu,Yanfeng Chen,Jun Xia,Ruobing Xie,Bin Xia,Xingwu Sun,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASTMIX 提出了一种自动化数据混合比例发现的框架，通过在单一代理模型上联合优化混合系数与模型参数，将数据混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题，以极低的搜索成本在预训练和后训练阶段均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模模型的成功依赖于海量多样化数据，但如何为预训练和后训练阶段确定最优的数据混合比例仍是一个重要的开放问题。现有方法要么依赖预定义的启发式规则，要么需要耗费大量资源的模拟实验，缺乏高效、可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Method: FASTMIX 将数据混合选择重新表述为双层优化问题：内层在按当前混合比例采样的数据上更新模型参数，外层基于验证反馈更新混合比例。关键创新在于证明了优化混合比例在数学上等价于在均匀源采样下为每个数据源分配损失权重，从而将混合系数直接嵌入可微的迭代优化目标中，实现模型和混合比例的联合梯度优化。整个过程仅需训练一个代理模型。

Result: FASTMIX 在预训练和后训练任务上均优于基线方法，同时大幅降低了搜索成本。

Conclusion: FASTMIX 通过双层优化框架实现了高效、自动化的数据混合比例发现，仅需单一代理模型即可完成优化，为大规模模型训练中的数据配比问题提供了一种可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: While large and diverse datasets have driven recent advances in large models, identifying the optimal data mixture for pre-training and post-training remains a significant open problem. We address this challenge with FASTMIX, a novel framework that automates data mixture discovery while training only a single proxy model. Instead of relying on predefined heuristics or resource-intensive simulations, FASTMIX jointly optimizes mixture coefficients and model parameters, substantially improving efficiency and scalability over prior approaches. At the core of FASTMIX is a reformulation of mixture selection as a bilevel optimization problem. Under this reformulation, we show that optimizing mixture ratios is mathematically equivalent to assigning per-source loss weights under uniform source sampling. This embeds the mixture coefficients directly into the differentiable iterative optimization objective, enabling efficient, gradient-based optimization of both mixture and model. To solve the optimization problem, FASTMIX implements an approximate iterative optimization procedure, alternating between (i) updating model parameters on data sampled according to current mixture ratios (inner loop) and (ii) updating mixture ratios based on validation feedback (outer loop). Across pre- and post-training, FASTMIX outperforms baselines while drastically reducing search cost. Code (https://github.com/hrtan/fastmix)

</details>


### [67] [Rational Sparse Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14990)
*Naiyu Yin,Yue Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE)，用可训练的有理函数替代SAE中固定的编码器非线性激活（如ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），通过两阶段流程（基于Remez交换的初始化和微调）在多个语言模型上实现重建质量与下游行为指标的严格提升，且仅增加少量标量参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏自编码器（SAE）受限于固定的编码器非线性（ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），这导致两方面问题：一是将特定稀疏机制硬编码进模型，限制了灵活性；二是可能扭曲重建与稀疏性之间的权衡。作者旨在通过引入更灵活的可训练激活函数来克服这些限制。

Method: 提出Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE)，核心是将固定编码器激活替换为可训练的有理函数。具体采用两阶段流程：(1) 初始化阶段：复制预训练基线SAE权重，利用松弛Remez交换算法在合成数据上拟合有理系数，并校准尺度参数；(2) 微调阶段：在标准稀疏正则化重建目标下进行微调。有理函数在紧集上能一致逼近ReLU、JumpReLU及TopK的阈值门控等现有激活原语。

Result: 在三个开源语言模型的残差流激活上，RSAE在微调后严格优于所有三种基线激活族（ReLU、JumpReLU、TopK），在重建侧指标和下游行为指标上均有改进，且不牺牲稀疏探测下的特征级可解释性。这些收益在模型、激活族和稀疏度范围内一致，额外参数量仅为每个自编码器数个标量参数，在单张消费级GPU上数分钟即可完成。

Conclusion: RSAE提供了一种轻量且高效的SAE升级方案，通过有理函数激活的灵活性打破了固定非线性的限制，在不牺牲可解释性的前提下显著提升了重建质量与下游行为保真度，具有广泛的适用性和实用价值。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are standard tools for mechanistic interpretability, but current SAE families are constrained by fixed encoder nonlinearities such as ReLU, JumpReLU, and TopK. This hard-codes a particular sparsity mechanism into the model and can distort the reconstruction-versus-sparsity trade-off. We introduce the Rational Sparse Autoencoder (RSAE), which replaces the fixed encoder activation with a trainable rational function. Rational activations are flexible enough to uniformly approximate the activation primitives used by existing SAE families on compact domains (for TopK, the thresholded gate obtained after a separating top-k threshold is supplied), while also providing a richer function class for adapting to the observed pre-activation geometry. We realise this idea through a two-stage pipeline: an initialisation procedure that copies the pre-trained baseline SAE weights, plugs in rational coefficients obtained by the relaxed Remez exchange on synthetic data, and calibrates the scale parameters along with the rational coefficients; followed by a fine-tuning step under the standard sparsity-regularised reconstruction objective. Empirically, on residual-stream activations of three open-weight language models and across all three baseline activation families, the RSAE strictly improves on it after the fine-tuning step, both on reconstruction-side metrics and on downstream-behaviour metrics, without sacrificing feature-level interpretability under sparse probing. These gains are consistent across host language models, across baseline activation families, and across the full range of baseline sparsity we tested, while the upgrade itself adds only a handful of scalar parameters per autoencoder and runs in minutes on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [68] [Unlocking Latent Dimensions: Exploring Representations of Large-Scale X-ray Scattering Data using Variational Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14999)
*Monika Choudhary,Xiaoya Chong,Runbo Jiang,Wiebke Koepp,Petrus H. Zwart,Damon English,Gregory M. Su,Eric Schaible,Chenhui Zhu,Mostafa Nassr,Noah P. Wamble,Kelvin Kam-Yun Li,Jonathan M. Chan,Jose Carlos Diaz,Cameron McKay,Lynn Katz,Benny Freeman,Guillaume Freychet,Yevgen Matviychuk,Eliot Gann,Daniel B. Allan,Benedikt Sochor,Frank Schluenzen,Stephan V. Roth,Ethan Crumlin,Dylan McReynolds,Tanny Chavez,Alexander Hexemer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注意力机制的卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE），在150万张X射线散射图像上训练，学习低维潜在表示以捕获结构变化。该潜在空间既可组织离线数据集，也可实时分析同步辐射实验，并支持受控合成散射图像生成。与通用视觉基础模型DINOv3相比，领域专用训练产生的潜在空间更具可解释性。该方法已整合至MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学用户设施产生的X射线散射数据速度远超传统工作流的处理能力，亟需一种能同时支持离线数据集探索和在线实时分析的通用方法。

Method: 训练一个领域专用的基于注意力的卷积变分自编码器（C-VAE），在150万张X射线散射图像上学习低维潜在表示；利用潜在空间进行聚类组织、轨迹追踪及受控合成散射图像生成；无需重新训练即可部署于两个同步辐射装置的时间分辨薄膜形成实验；与DINOv3（ViT-7B）通用视觉基础模型进行基准对比；将工作流集成至MLExchange平台的Latent Space Explorer工具中。

Result: 潜在空间呈现组织良好的聚类和平滑轨迹，反映实验进程；模型无需重新训练即可在新实验数据上生成可解释的潜在结构；受控合成散射图像可覆盖多种结构状态；领域专用训练在散射数据的潜在组织可解释性上优于DINOv3通用模型。

Conclusion: 领域专用的注意力C-VAE能够有效将大规模X射线散射数据组织为可解释的低维潜在表示，支持离线探索与在线实时分析双重场景，优于通用视觉基础模型，为同步辐射实验的交互式结构探索提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Scientific user facilities generate X-ray scattering data faster than traditional workflows can process them. We address this challenge across two settings, offline dataset exploration and live on-the-fly analysis. We train a domain-specific attention-based Convolutional Variational Autoencoder (C-VAE) on 1.5 million X-ray scattering images to learn low-dimensional representations capturing structural variation across diverse experimental conditions. The learned latent space reveals well-organized clusters and smooth trajectories reflecting experimental progression. It further supports controlled synthetic scattering image generation across diverse structural states. When deployed without retraining, the model organizes time-resolved film formation experiments at two synchrotron facilities into interpretable latent structures. Benchmarking against DINOv3 (ViT-7B), a general-purpose vision foundation model, demonstrates that domain-specific training yields more interpretable latent organization for scattering data. Both workflows are integrated within Latent Space Explorer, a component of the MLExchange platform, supporting interactive structural exploration across archived datasets and live experiments.

</details>


### [69] [How Should World Models Be Evaluated? A Decision-Making-Centric Position](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15032)
*Yang Yu,Shiyuan Zhang,Yifei Sheng,Haoxiang Ren,Haoxin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了"世界模型"这一术语在当代AI中的多重含义与评估乱象，指出现有评估常与论文声称不匹配。作者提出应以具身决策为中心，构建L0-L7评估阶梯，将评估焦点从视觉逼真度转向反事实推理、策略评估与策略优化等决策效用指标，并设计了相应的基准协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: "世界模型"已成为现代AI的核心抽象之一，但该术语指代对象繁多（环境模型、潜在想象模型、未来视频预测器等），评估指标也高度碎片化。由此产生一个反复出现的核心问题：论文的声称与其评估所能证明的结论之间存在系统性错配——许多论文声称其模型对决策有用，但评估仅停留在视频逼真度层面，无法真正支撑这一论断。

Method: 作者对近年文献进行了系统综述，提出以"用途依赖"为核心的分析视角。核心方法论是构建一个L0-L7的评估阶梯：L0视觉逼真度，L1感知相似性，L2指令遵循，L3物理合理性，L4反事实动作保真度（首个真正的干预性测试），L5闭环 rollout 有效性，L6策略排序一致性，L7策略优化提升。L0-L3被解读为生成产物的诊断性评估，L4-L7则提供决策有用性的直接证据。在此基础上，作者提出了一套以决策为中心的评估框架与基准协议，涵盖反事实动作保真度、闭环rollout有效性、奖励/价值预测、策略排序一致性、优化提升、模型可被利用性及不确定性校准等维度。

Result: 通过文献梳理发现：（1）当前"世界模型"文献的评估方法高度多样化且碎片化；（2）普遍存在声称-证据不匹配问题，即模型声称的决策效用远超其评估能支撑的范围；（3）L4被识别为关键分水岭——在此之上评估才真正触及干预与因果；（4）L5-L7层次提供了决策有用性的最直接证据，但目前多数工作尚未达到这一标准。

Conclusion: 世界模型的评估应以其预期用途为中心进行组织。对于面向具身决策的世界模型，决定性标准不是视频的视觉逼真度，而是在干预、策略诱导分布偏移和长视界rollout条件下，模型是否支持可靠的反事实推理、策略评估、规划与策略优化。本文提出的L0-L7阶梯和决策中心型评估框架为此提供了系统化的诊断工具和基准设计指南。

Abstract: World models have rapidly become one of the central abstractions in modern AI. Yet the term now refers to several different objects: action-conditioned environment models, latent imagination models, future-video predictors, interactive neural simulators, latent predictive representations, and synthetic-data engines. Evaluation has broadened with the term. Recent papers measure video realism, perceptual similarity, instruction following, physical plausibility, policy ranking, executability, planning success, and downstream policy improvement. The result is not only metric diversity but also a recurring problem of claim/evidence mismatch: papers frequently make a stronger claim about what their model is useful for than their evaluation can actually establish.
  This paper surveys the recent literature and argues that the central question is use-dependent. When a model is presented as a world model for embodied decision-making, a more decisive issue is not whether it generates visually compelling videos, but whether it supports reliable counterfactual reasoning, policy evaluation, planning, and policy optimization under intervention, policy-induced distribution shift, and long-horizon rollout. We organize the literature using an L0--L7 ladder that ranges from visual plausibility to policy optimization utility. In our interpretation, L0--L3 are most naturally read as diagnostics of generated artifacts, L4 is often the first genuinely interventional test, and L5--L7 provide the most direct evidence of decision usefulness. Based on this diagnosis, we propose a decision-making-centric evaluation framework and a benchmark protocol that foreground counterfactual action fidelity, closed-loop rollout validity, reward/value prediction, policy-ranking agreement, optimization lift, model exploitability, and uncertainty calibration.

</details>


### [70] [Transformers Learn the Mestre-Nagao Heuristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15036)
*Pranav Venkata Konda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 训练一个两层Transformer编码器，仅从椭圆曲线的Frobenius迹数据中学习到与解析数论中Mestre-Nagao和公式高度一致的权重，实现了>99%的秩分类准确率，并通过机械可解释性方法揭示了模型内部稀疏的"推挽检测器"电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Transformer能否从纯数据中重新发现数学结构（椭圆曲线秩分类），并通过逆向工程理解模型学到的算法机制，从而为AI辅助数学发现的可信度提供机械层面的证据。

Method: 训练两层Transformer编码器对导子≤10000的椭圆曲线进行秩0/1二分类，输入为前128个归一化Frobenius迹。随后采用注意力分析、线性探测、激活修补、logit归因和神经元级电路分析等机械可解释性技术进行逆向工程，分析50个独立训练模型的解在函数空间和权重空间的一致性。

Result: 模型对两类均达到>99%准确率，且对训练集外无同源或二次扭曲关联的测试曲线准确率不变。发现仅需20/512个第一层MLP神经元构成的稀疏电路即可在AUROC 0.992下完成秩预测，形成"秩-0检测器"与"秩-1检测器"的推挽架构。最关键的是，顶级判别神经元的输入权重与Mestre-Nagao和公式权重log(p)/(p·log B)的Spearman相关系数达0.997、Pearson相关系数达0.952。CLS嵌入编码了log L(E,1)（控制导子后R²=0.962±0.011）。50个独立训练的模型在函数空间几乎一致（成对一致性>98.8%），但权重空间存在较大差异。

Conclusion: Transformer能够从纯数据中学习到与已知解析数论公式高度一致的数学结构，且该能力具有可复现性（50个模型收敛到相同函数解）。机械可解释性分析揭示了模型内部的稀疏推挽检测电路，但也暴露出读出机制的次优性问题。这一工作为AI辅助数学发现提供了机械层面的可信度验证。

Abstract: We train a two-layer transformer encoder to classify rational elliptic curves $E/\mathbb{Q}$ of conductor $\leq 10000$ as either rank 0 or rank 1 from the first 128 normalized Frobenius traces. We achieve >99% accuracy on both classes, and accuracy is essentially unchanged on test curves with no isogeny or quadratic-twist relative in the training set. We then apply techniques from mechanistic interpretability such as attention analysis, linear probing, activation patching, logit attribution, and neuron-level circuit analysis to reverse-engineer the algorithm the (centroid in function space) model learned. We find that a sparse circuit of 20 out of 512 layer-1 MLP neurons is sufficient for rank prediction under a linear probe with an AUROC of 0.992 at plateau, implementing a push-pull detector architecture of rank-0 and rank-1 detectors with a one-sided readout. However, we notice that the model has sub-optimal readout problems indicating a mismatch in rank-order between the readout pathway and the discriminative circuit. Critically, the learned input weights of the top discriminating neuron match the Mestre-Nagao sum heuristic weights $\log(p)/(p\cdot \log{B})$ with a Spearman coefficient $r = 0.997$ and Pearson coefficient $r = 0.952$: the model has learnt a result from analytic number theory from the Frobenius trace data alone. We additionally find that all 50 independently trained models concentrate CLS attention on prime positions at 2-50$\times$ the rate of composite positions. The CLS embedding encodes $\log{L(E,1)}$ with $R^2 = 0.962\pm 0.011$ across the 50 models (after controlling for the conductor). Activation patching analysis reveals that attention weights are dissociated from causal information flow. Additionally, the 50 solutions from training are near-identical in function space (with pairwise agreement $>$98.8%) despite large weight space barriers.

</details>


### [71] [Temporal Difference Learning for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15048)
*Qizhen Ying,Yangchen Pan,Victor Adrian Prisacariu,Junfeng Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种时域差分（TD）目标函数，用于增强扩散模型沿去噪轨迹的跨时间一致性。通过将扩散过程建模为马尔可夫奖励过程，并将去噪视为策略评估问题，作者推导出适用于离散和连续时间扩散的统一TD方法，并引入基于样本的重新加权策略以稳定训练。实验表明，该方法显著提升FID，尤其在少步采样场景下优势明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型的训练目标仅关注单个时间步（或相邻时间步对）的局部去噪目标，缺乏对去噪轨迹上不同时间步预测之间一致性的约束。这种跨时间不一致性会降低生成质量，尤其在少步采样器场景下问题更为突出。

Method: 作者将扩散过程重新表述为马尔可夫奖励过程，将去噪问题转化为强化学习中的策略评估问题。基于此框架，推导出统一的时域差分（TD）目标，适用于离散时间和连续时间扩散模型。此外，提出一种有原则的基于样本的重新加权方法以稳定训练，并对成对损失重新加权、正则化权重和单步步幅等设计选择进行了消融研究。

Result: 采用TD训练后，生成样本的FID指标显著改善；在采样步数较少时优势更为明显，凸显了该方法在低计算预算场景下的实用价值。消融实验验证了各项设计选择的合理性。

Conclusion: TD方法可作为一种通用的即插即用组件，强制跨时间一致性，从而提升不同扩散生成模型的生成质量，尤其在少步采样场景中具有重要实际意义。

Abstract: Diffusion models are typically trained with objectives that focus on local denoising targets at individual time steps (or adjacent pairs), which do not enforce consistency between predictions along the denoising trajectory. This lack of cross-time consistency can degrade performance, especially for few-step samplers. We introduce a temporal difference (TD) objective that penalizes inconsistency of the model's multi-step progress along the denoising path. By reformulating the diffusion process as a Markov reward process and casting denoising as a policy evaluation problem in reinforcement learning, we derive a unified TD approach that applies to both discrete- and continuous-time diffusion formulations. We further propose a principled sample-based reweighting method that stabilizes training. Empirically, we show that using our TD training can significantly improve sample quality measured by FID, with stronger advantages when the number of sampling steps is small, highlighting its practical utility under low-computation-budget scenarios. We provide ablation studies to justify our design choices, including pairwise loss reweighting, regularization weight, and one-step stride. Overall, our TD approach can be a general drop-in that enforces cross-time consistency and improves generation quality across different diffusion generative models.

</details>


### [72] [Physics-conforming Latent Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15053)
*Matthias Chung,Yutong Bu,Deepanshu Verma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"物理一致潜在孪生"（Physics-conforming Latent Twins）框架，通过在潜在空间中设计保持或耗散物理结构的潜在流映射，使学习到的代理解算子严格遵循守恒律、不变量、容许条件和耗散结构，从而在保持预测精度同时提升长时间行为的结构保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对时变物理系统的代理建模，现有方法仅关注训练轨迹的插值精度，却忽视了对物理意义至关重要的守恒律、不变量、容许条件和耗散结构。这导致代理模型在长期预测中出现物理不一致甚至违背基本定律。因此需要一种能从根本上保证潜在动力学遵循指定物理原理的框架。

Method: 方法核心：（1）在潜在孪生（Latent Twin）框架基础上，联合学习编码器、解码器和任意时间索引状态间的潜在流映射；（2）引入"约束迁移"视角，将原始状态空间的物理结构映射到潜在空间中的相容约束；（3）推导保证线性/二次不变量守恒或耗散不等式的潜在流映射的代数条件；（4）通过结构保持界（structure-preservation bounds）证明潜在约束的施加如何改善解码后物理缺陷的控制。

Result: 在代表性ODE和PDE基准上的数值实验表明：该方法在保持准确代理预测的同时，显著改善了约束满足程度、结构保真度以及长时间定性行为。

Conclusion: 物理一致潜在孪生框架成功实现了物理原理与数据驱动代理建模的深度融合，为时变系统的科学机器学习提供了一条在潜在空间内天然保证物理结构的新路径。

Abstract: Surrogate models are central to scientific machine learning, where they enable fast prediction, simulation, inference, and control for complex physical systems. For time-dependent problems, however, accurate interpolation of training trajectories is not sufficient: reliable surrogates should also respect the conservation laws, invariants, admissibility conditions, and dissipative structures that give those trajectories physical meaning. We introduce Physics-conforming Latent Twins, a framework for learning latent surrogate solution operators whose dynamics satisfy selected physical principles by design. The method builds on the Latent Twin formulation by jointly learning an encoder, a decoder, and a latent flow map between arbitrary time-indexed states, while constraining the latent dynamics to preserve or dissipate prescribed structural quantities. We develop a constraint-transfer viewpoint that connects physical structure in the original state space with compatible constraints in latent space, and prove structure-preservation bounds showing how latent enforcement improves control of physical defects after decoding. We also derive algebraic conditions for latent flow maps that preserve linear and quadratic invariants or enforce dissipative inequalities. Numerical experiments on representative ODE and PDE benchmarks demonstrate improved constraint satisfaction, structural fidelity, and qualitative long-time behavior while maintaining accurate surrogate prediction.

</details>


### [73] [Can Neural Networks Achieve Optimal Computational-statistical Tradeoff? An Analysis on Single-Index Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15219)
*Siyu Chen,Beining Wu,Miao Lu,Zhuoran Yang,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的梯度下降算法，用于训练两层神经网络以学习高斯单指数模型，证明了该方法在样本复杂度上达到了统计查询（SQ）下界（相差多对数因子），并进一步通过权重扰动技术扩展至稀疏设置，同样匹配SQ下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已知，在SQ框架下，学习高斯单指数模型的多项式时间算法至少需要Ω(d^{s*/2} ∨ d)样本，但尚不清楚基于梯度训练的神经网络能否达到这一最优计算-统计权衡。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 作者受标签变换和景观平滑等先前技术启发，提出统一的基于梯度的两层神经网络训练算法，兼容多种损失函数和激活函数。核心创新包括：(1) 通用的梯度下降框架，实现特征表示与未知信号θ*的强对齐；(2) 针对k-稀疏θ*（k=o(√d)）的权重扰动技术，利用稀疏结构提升效率。

Result: 算法在生成指数s*≥1的所有情况下，样本复杂度为Õ(d^{s*/2} ∨ d)，匹配SQ下界（仅相差多对数因子）。在稀疏设定下，导出对应SQ下界~Ω(k^{s*})，方法同样达到匹配。权重扰动技术具有独立研究价值，可启发稀疏张量PCA等问题的梯度求解。

Conclusion: 神经网络通过梯度训练确实可以达到学习高斯单指数模型的最优计算-统计权衡，结束了此前关于这一能力的悬而未决状态。统一框架和权重扰动技术为相关领域提供了新的方法论工具。

Abstract: In this work, we tackle the following question: Can neural networks trained with gradient-based methods achieve the optimal computational-statistical tradeoff in learning Gaussian single-index models? Prior research has shown that any polynomial-time algorithm under the statistical query (SQ) framework requires $Ω(d^{s^\star/2}\lor d)$ samples, where $s^\star$ is the generative exponent representing the intrinsic difficulty of learning the underlying model. However, it remains unknown whether neural networks can achieve this sample complexity. Inspired by prior techniques such as label transformation and landscape smoothing for learning single-index models, we propose a unified gradient-based algorithm for training a two-layer neural network in polynomial time. Our method is adaptable to a variety of loss and activation functions, covering a broad class of existing approaches. We show that our algorithm learns a feature representation that strongly aligns with the unknown signal $θ^\star$, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O} (d^{s^\star/2} \lor d)$, matching the SQ lower bound up to a polylogarithmic factor for all generative exponents $s^\star\geq 1$. Furthermore, we extend our approach to the setting where $θ^\star$ is $k$-sparse for $k = o(\sqrt{d})$ by introducing a novel weight perturbation technique that leverages the sparsity structure. We derive a corresponding SQ lower bound of order $\widetildeΩ(k^{s^\star})$, matched by our method up to a polylogarithmic factor. Our framework, especially the weight perturbation technique, is of independent interest, and suggests potential gradient-based solutions to other problems such as sparse tensor PCA.

</details>


### [74] [Size Doesn't Matter: Cosine-Scored Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15054)
*Silen Naihin,Lev Stambler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）通过内积检测特征，导致高范数token会"劫持"字典容量。本文提出用余弦相似度与输入范数的可学习混合来替代内积评分，使优化器自行决定范数的使用程度。结果表明，优化器从不完全恢复内积，余弦编码器学到的特征更符合人类可识别概念，在匹配重构水平下显著优于标准内积方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SAE使用内积作为特征检测评分，但子层归一化（LayerNorm/RMSNorm）已经丢弃了内积所测量的范数信息，这意味着编码器检测的量是模型实际上并不读取的量。高范数token通过BatchTopK机制同时膨胀所有预激活值，占据字典槽位而不考虑内容对齐，导致字典容量被浪费在"范数检测器"上。

Method: 提出两种替代评分方案：(1) 全局混合：将内积替换为余弦相似度与输入范数的可学习凸组合，由优化器决定范数的权重；(2) 逐特征扩展：每个特征独立学习自己的范数依赖程度。同时使用损失重加权（loss reweighting）来均衡梯度，以排除梯度差异的干扰，确认前向传播评分几何是真正的操控杠杆。

Result: 在两种方案下，训练完全自由恢复内积，但从未发生——没有特征选择超过半范数依赖。在匹配重构水平下，余弦编码器学到的特征与人类可识别概念的吻合度远高于标准SAE，且填充了原本被内积浪费在范数检测器上的字典槽位。损失重加权仅能勉强缩小差距，证实前向评分几何是关键因素。该优势并非在所有任务或深度上普遍成立。

Conclusion: 余弦评分应成为对归一化表示进行字典学习的默认选择，因为它有效避免高范数token劫持字典容量的问题，学到的特征更具语义可解释性，且这种改进根源于前向传播中的评分几何，而非梯度效应。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) detect features via inner product, so a feature's activation scales with both its directional alignment and the input's norm. Under BatchTopK, high-norm tokens inflate all pre-activations simultaneously, claiming dictionary slots regardless of content alignment. This matters because sublayer normalization has already discarded the magnitude the score measures, so the encoder detects a quantity the model does not read. We replace the score with a learned blend of cosine similarity and input magnitude, letting the optimizer choose how much norm to use; a per-feature extension lets each feature decide independently. In both regimes, training is free to recover inner product but never does, with no feature ever choosing more than half-magnitude dependence. At matched reconstruction, the cosine encoder learns features that align with human-recognizable concepts far more often than standard, filling dictionary slots that inner product wastes on norm detectors. Loss reweighting that equalizes gradients barely closes the gap, confirming forward-pass score geometry as the lever. The advantage is not universal across tasks or depths, but we believe cosine scoring should be the default for dictionary learning on normalized representations.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Bridging data-driven priors via the score function for posterior sampling -- Comparative review and experimental study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14800)
*Elhadji Cisse Faye,Mame Diarra Fall,Sylvain Delchini,Nicolas Dobigeon*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多种数据驱动先验可通过得分函数统一，并将它们集成到一种新采样算法中，在图像修复和超分辨率任务上验证了该统一框架的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在贝叶斯逆问题中，各类数据驱动先验（如正则化去噪、归一化流、得分生成模型、凸脊正则化）形式迥异，缺乏统一视角，导致难以将它们系统性地融入高效的后验采样方法中。

Method: 通过得分函数（score function）这一共同视角统一四类数据驱动先验，将其整合进一种近期提出的采样算法框架中，从而实现对后验分布的高效采样。

Result: 在图像修复和单图像超分辨率任务上，四种先验均表现出良好性能；在地质背景的真实图像复原中也验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于得分函数的统一框架具有高度通用性，能够处理由广泛得分函数类先验定义的任意后验分布，不限于本文讨论的具体情况。

Abstract: This paper reviews how a diverse set of popular data-driven priors commonly used in Bayesian inverse problems can be unified through their respective score functions. By framing these priors under this common perspective, we show that they can benefit from their straightfoward and effective integration into a recently proposed sampling algorithm. The applicability of this common framework is illustrated by considering several data-driven priors, namely regularization-by-denoising, normalizing flow-based priors, score-based generative models, and convex-ridge regularizers. For these four particular priors, the performance of the method is evaluated when conducting image inpainting and single image super-resolution. These results, as well as those obtained when restoring real images acquired in a geological context, demonstrate the efficiency of the method. This unified framework proves versatile enough to handle any posterior distribution defined by a broad class of score function-based priors, beyond the specific cases considered in this paper.

</details>


### [76] [Bartlett adjustment for Gaussian random effects meta-analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14837)
*Haben Michael*

Main category: stat.ME

Motivation: 元分析通常基于的研究数量过少，不足以支撑所使用统计程序的渐近方法。高阶渐近理论可作为解决这一问题的补救手段，但现有文献中的Bartlett校正公式存在错误，需要加以纠正。

Method: 采用高阶渐近理论（higher-order asymptotics），针对理想化高斯情形（idealized Gaussian case）推导Bartlett校正（Bartlett correction）。

Result: 成功推导出理想化高斯情形下正确的Bartlett校正公式，并纠正了当前文献中存在的错误公式。

Conclusion: 文献中已有的Bartlett校正公式有误，本文提供的修正公式可为小样本元分析提供更可靠的统计推断基础。

Abstract: Meta-analyses are often based on too few studies to justify the asymptotic methods underlying the statistical procedures applied to them. We consider higher-order asymptotics as a remedy. We derive the Bartlett correction for the idealized Gaussian case, correcting the formula currently appearing in the literature.

</details>

*Thomas W. Hsiao,Howard H. Chang,Razieh Nabi*

Main category: stat.ME
