<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在环境流行病学中，研究者关注化学品和污染物混合物的健康效应，但多变量连续暴露的因果暴露-响应曲面呈高维特性，导致联合暴露效应的估计和解释极为困难。现有基于边际结构模型的方法在实现上较为复杂，亟需一种更简洁有效的框架。

Method: 提出因果充分降维（CSDR），一种半参数框架。目标参数为因果中心均值子空间（causal central mean subspace）。构建模块化两阶段估计器：第一阶段估计干扰函数（nuisance functions），第二阶段估计子空间，两者解耦以简化实现。提出子空间恢复的收敛速率（考虑第一阶段干扰估计误差），证明结构维度可被一致估计，并引入子空间重要性评分（subspace importance score）量化各暴露对降维的贡献。

Result: 模拟研究中，CSDR 在暴露-响应曲面的估计精度和不确定性量化方面均优于使用非因果降维方法或原始暴露的方法。在亚特兰大非裔美国母婴队列的实证应用中，CSDR 被用于研究孕期 PFAS 化学混合物暴露对婴儿出生体重的影响。

Conclusion: CSDR 为多变量连续暴露的因果效应估计提供了一种有效的降维框架，降维后的暴露保留了表征联合因果效应所需的信息，同时支持高效的下游估计，在环境流行病学的混合物暴露研究中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects with multivariate continuous exposures is challenging because causal exposure-response surfaces can be high-dimensional, complicating estimation and interpretation of joint exposure effects. Such settings arise in environmental epidemiology, where interest centers on the health effects of chemical and pollutant mixtures. We develop causal sufficient dimension reduction (CSDR), a semiparametric framework for representing causal exposure-response surfaces through low-dimensional exposure summaries. We formalize the reduction target as the causal central mean subspace and propose a modular two-stage estimator that decouples nuisance-function estimation from subspace estimation, simplifying implementation relative to existing marginal structural model-based approaches. The reduced exposure preserves the information needed to characterize joint causal effects while enabling efficient downstream estimation. We establish a convergence rate for causal subspace recovery accounting for first-stage nuisance estimation error, show that the structural dimension can be estimated consistently, and introduce a subspace importance score that quantifies the contribution of each exposure to the reduction. In simulations, CSDR yielded more accurate estimation and uncertainty quantification of the exposure-response surface than methods using noncausal dimension reduction or the original exposure. We apply CSDR to study the effect of maternal exposure to PFAS chemical mixtures on infant birth weight in the Atlanta African American Maternal-Child Cohort.

</details>


### [78] [Heterogeneous behavioral mechanisms in epidemiological models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14902)
*Jessica Pavani,Rob Deardon,Alexandra M. Schmidt*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 传统传染病模型忽略人群行为异质性，本文提出贝叶斯混合模型，将人群分为风险中性（维持基线接触率）和风险规避（根据疫情严重程度调整行为）两类，整合异质行为动力学到统一传播框架中。仿真和实证表明，该方法在参数恢复、疫情轨迹估计和预测精度上显著优于传统模型，忽略行为多样性会导致峰值估计偏差和疫情曲线人为拉伸。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统SIR等模型假设行为同质性，行为改变模型虽引入了个体对风险的响应，但两者均未考虑人群内在异质性——即不同个体在风险容忍度和行为倾向上存在本质差异。这种忽略会掩盖个体感知和应对疾病威胁的重要差异，导致疫情预测偏差。

Method: 提出贝叶斯混合模型，将人群划分为两种行为模式：(1) 风险中性个体——保持基线接触率不变；(2) 风险规避个体——根据疫情严重程度动态调节自身行为。通过贝叶斯框架将这两种异质动力学统一整合到传播模型中，实现对群体行为差异的显式建模。

Result: 仿真研究和实证数据应用均表明，所提模型在参数恢复（parameter recovery）、疫情轨迹估计（epidemic trajectory estimation）和预测精度（forecasting precision）三个维度上显著优于传统聚合模型。忽略行为多样性会导致峰值估计偏差和疫情曲线人为拉伸。

Conclusion: 该研究为在社会分化的环境中预测疫情轨迹提供了更加精细化的计算工具，确保公共卫生干预策略建立在行为现实主义基础之上，强调在疫情建模中纳入行为异质性的必要性。

Abstract: Traditional epidemic models frequently assume behavioral homogeneity. The susceptible-infected-recovered model provides a robust foundation for characterizing disease transmission, but it does so without accounting for how people actually respond to risk. In contrast, behavioral change models incorporate mechanisms that capture how individuals adjust their actions during an outbreak, recognizing that rising infection risk typically motivates protective behaviors. Yet both approaches share a key limitation: they overlook the inherent heterogeneity of a population. In reality, communities are a complex mixture of risk tolerances and behavioral tendencies. Ignoring this inherent heterogeneity can obscure important differences in how individuals perceive and respond to disease threats. This paper introduces a novel Bayesian mixture model designed to address this limitation by partitioning the population into two distinct behavioral patterns: risk-neutral individuals, who maintain baseline contact rates, and risk-averse individuals, who modulate their behavior in response to epidemic severity. By integrating these disparate dynamics into a unified transmission framework, the proposed model explicitly accounts for varying population behaviors often overlooked by aggregate approaches. Through simulation studies and empirical data applications, we demonstrate that this approach significantly outperforms traditional models in parameter recovery, epidemic trajectory estimation, and forecasting precision. The findings suggest that failing to account for behavioral diversity leads to biased peak estimates and artificially stretched epidemic curves. Consequently, this research provides a more nuanced computational toolkit for predicting outbreak trajectories in socially fragmented environments, ensuring that public health intervention strategies are informed by a foundation of behavioral realism.

</details>


### [79] [Flexible Method Comparison with the Probability of Agreement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14921)
*Nathaniel T. Stevens*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文对方法比较中的一致性概率（PoA）方法进行了全面改进和扩展，建立了更灵活的推断框架，放宽了以往实现中的限制性假设，使其适用于更广泛的应用场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中新测量方法的开发需要验证其与现有方法的一致性。现有PoA方法虽直观有效，但受限于多个严格假设，限制了其应用范围。因此需要建立一个更灵活、更通用的推断框架。

Method: 作者对PoA方法进行了系统性的改进与扩展，构建了一个放宽多重限制性假设的推断框架，通过总前列腺特异性抗原（tPSA）测量方法比较的实例进行说明，并利用模拟研究全面检验其性能。

Result: 通过实例分析和模拟研究，验证了该灵活方法的有效性。新框架在保持PoA直观性和信息量的同时，显著提升了方法的灵活性和可用性。

Conclusion: 该工作极大地增强了PoA方法在方法比较中的灵活性、可用性及影响力，为临床测量方法的一致性评估提供了更通用、更实用的工具。

Abstract: The comparison of methods of measurement is a common problem in clinical practice; as novel methods are developed, establishing their agreement with existing methods is crucial. The probability of agreement (PoA) has previously been proposed as an intuitive and informative means of assessing agreement between two methods of measurement. It straightforwardly quantifies the likelihood that two measurements by different methods on the same subject are clinically indistinguishable. In this paper, we overhaul and extend the PoA methodology by developing an inference framework that relaxes several restrictive assumptions made in previous implementations, ultimately increasing its utility in a wider range of applications. We illustrate this more flexible methodology in an example that compares methods of measuring total Prostatic Specific Antigen (tPSA). And we thoroughly investigate its performance via simulation. This work dramatically increases the flexibility, availability, and hence impact of the PoA approach for method comparison.

</details>


### [80] [A Kuramoto-von Mises Time Series Model for Probabilistic Modeling of Coupled Oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15012)
*Yun Hwang,Todd P. Coleman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种不依赖热力学平衡假设的耦合振子概率分布估计方法，基于Langevin动力学构建，在高采样率下有闭式解，在非平衡场景下优于现有方法，并展示了其在脑电刺激旅行波和胃电记录中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有耦合振子系统相位关系建模通常依赖平衡态热力学假设，采用Boltzmann形式的成对指数族分布，但真实系统（如脑、胃肠）本质上是动态的，常在平衡与非平衡态间切换，上述假设经常被违反。

Method: 基于Langevin动力学构建耦合振子概率分布模型，无需热力学平衡假设；推导出在高采样率条件下最大似然估计具有闭式代数解，便于实际应用。

Result: 在模拟数据的非平衡场景中，该方法优于现有方法；成功应用于脑刺激诱发的动态脑旅行波表征，以及人体胃电生理记录中的假设检验。

Conclusion: 该方法为耦合振子系统提供了一种无需平衡假设的高效概率建模途径，兼具理论优雅性与实用便利性，适用于神经科学和胃肠电生理等多种动态振荡系统分析。

Abstract: A system of coupled oscillators provides a fundamental framework for modeling a wide range of physical and biological phenomena. In neuroscience, the central nervous system exhibits synchronized oscillatory activity with adjacent brain regions, giving rise to traveling wave dynamics for instance during sleep. Similarly, in the gastrointestinal system, neuromuscular cells coordinate their oscillations to generate propagating waves of slow wave activity. To estimate probability distributions of multivariate phase relationships, existing approaches typically rely on equilibrium thermodynamics, expressing the system in a Boltzmann form through a pairwise exponential family distribution. However, these assumptions are often violated in real-world systems, which are inherently dynamic and frequently transition between equilibrium and non-equilibrium regimes. To address this, we propose an efficient method for estimating the probability distribution of coupled oscillators that does not assume thermodynamic equilibrium. Using a Langevin dynamics-based construction, the approach enables accurate modeling even in non-equilibrium regimes. The maximum likelihood estimation method is shown to have a closed form algebraic solution in the high sampling rate regime, a condition commonly satisfied by modern data acquisition systems, which makes it readily applicable in practice. We demonstrate its robustness on simulated data, where it outperforms existing approaches in non-equilibrium settings, and further illustrate its utility for characterizing dynamic brain traveling waves in response to brain stimulation and in hypothesis testing within the context of electrophysiologic recordings of the human stomach.

</details>


### [81] [Separate versus pooled winsorization for group mean contrasts: a finite-sample theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15097)
*Chao Cheng,Chenshan Hu,Yukai Huang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文比较了合并缩尾（pooled winsorization）与分组缩尾（separate winsorization）两种策略在重尾分布下估计组均值对比的表现，证明合并缩尾在确定性规则下无法达到次高斯收敛速率，而分组缩尾可以达到，并推荐在实践中采用分组缩尾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重尾分布下，传统的均值对比估计量（如两组研究、随机试验、双重差分设计）表现不稳定，缩尾是一种常用补救手段。然而主流方法——合并缩尾——的理论性质尚不明确，需要系统比较两种缩尾策略的优劣。

Method: 作者推导了两种缩尾策略的有限样本偏差界（finite-sample deviation bounds），并证明了关于合并缩尾的不可能性结果：任何确定性规则选择合并缩尾水平都无法达到次高斯速率。同时证明分组缩尾可达次高斯速率，并可推广至一般线性对比。

Result: 理论上证明了合并缩尾的确定性规则无法达到次高斯速率，而分组缩尾可以。模拟研究证实合并缩尾可能存在显著偏差，分组缩尾则保持近似无偏且围绕真值集中良好。

Conclusion: 推荐在每个组内分别进行缩尾处理，而非合并所有样本后再缩尾。

Abstract: Comparing group means is foundational to many statistical areas, including two-sample studies, randomized trials, and difference-in-differences designs, yet heavy-tailed outcomes can make conventional estimators unstable. A common remedy is to winsorize the data before estimating the target mean contrast. The dominant approach, pooled winsorization, computes winsorization thresholds from the combined sample across all groups, while the rarely used alternative, separate winsorization, computes them within each group. We study finite-sample deviation bounds for these two winsorization strategies, and we prove an impossibility result: no deterministic rule for selecting the pooled winsorization level can attain the sub-Gaussian rate. In contrast, separate winsorization attains this rate, and the guarantee extends to general linear contrasts of group means. Simulation studies confirm that pooled winsorization can have substantial bias, while separate winsorization remains nearly unbiased and concentrates well around the truth. These results support a simple recommendation: winsorize within each group rather than after pooling.

</details>


### [82] [On the estimation of the median odds ratio for measuring contextual effects in multilevel binary data from complex survey designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15145)
*Shafayet Khan Shafee,M. Shafiqur Rahman*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文讨论了在两层和三层层次结构二元数据中，中位比值比（MOR）的点估计和区间估计方法。通过Delta法构建基于方差分量的置信区间，模拟研究表明估计量偏差小、覆盖率良好，并应用于剖腹产率的背景效应分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOR研究多集中于两层模型的点估计，缺乏对更复杂多层结构下统计性质及区间估计的探讨。然而，许多实际数据（如多阶段调查）涉及两层以上的层次结构，且区间估计对统计推断至关重要。

Method: 基于随机效应logistic回归模型的方差分量，将log转换后的MOR视为渐近正态，采用Delta法推导其置信区间。方法在两层和三层模型的不同设定下进行演示，并通过广泛的模拟研究评估不同场景下估计量的表现。

Result: 模拟结果表明，在中等至大样本下，MOR估计量偏差可忽略不计，95%置信区间的覆盖率令人满意；小样本偏倚主要源于方差分量估计的偏差。应用于剖腹产背景效应的实例表明，该框架增强了可解释性，有助于更深入的统计与政策分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的MOR区间估计框架适用于两层和三层层次结构数据，在大样本下表现优良，能够为多层次背景效应的推断提供有效工具，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In studies with clustered or hierarchical data structures, quantifying between-cluster heterogeneity, referred to as contextual effects, is crucial for valid cluster-level inference. The median odds ratio (MOR), derived from random effects (RE) logistic regression models for clustered binary data, provides an intuitive assessment of contextual effects. Most existing research focuses on point estimation of the MOR for two-level models, with limited exploration of its statistical properties under complex multilevel structures. However, the development of corresponding interval estimators is essential for statistical inference. Moreover, many real-world datasets, particularly those from multistage surveys, involve hierarchical structures beyond two levels, where contextual effects at each level are of interest. This paper discusses the estimation of MOR for both the two-and three-level binary data, with particular emphasis on interval estimation. Since the MOR is a post-estimation measure based on variance components of the RE logit model, its confidence interval is derived using the Delta method, treating the log-transformed MOR as asymptotically normal. The approach is demonstrated across different model specifications in two-and three-level settings. An extensive simulation study evaluated the performance of the MOR estimators across diverse scenarios in hierarchical data settings. The results showed that the estimators exhibited negligible bias and satisfactory coverage probability of a 95% confidence interval for moderate to large samples, with small-sample bias mainly due to variance component estimation. An application of the methods for estimating the contextual effect on C-section delivery demonstrated that the proposed framework enhances interpretability and supports more informed statistical and policy-oriented analyses.

</details>


### [83] [Optimized Sequential Testing for Binary Ensemble Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15237)
*Joseph Kalman,Amit Moscovich*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究如何通过序贯测试（sequential testing）方法降低集成分类器在二分类任务中的计算成本。核心思想是不必评估所有基模型再投票，而是逐个评估基模型，一旦多数票结果明确即提前停止。作者针对三种最优性定义构建线性规划来求解最优停止策略，在UCI和Grinsztajn等基准数据集上实现了4倍以上加速，同时将分歧率控制在0.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集成模型（如随机森林）虽然准确率高，但基模型数量越多，训练和推理的计算成本也越高。现有方法通常是评估所有基模型后投票，存在大量冗余计算。作者希望借鉴序贯测试领域的思路，通过提前停止机制在不牺牲分类精度的前提下显著降低计算开销。

Method: 将集成分类的推理过程建模为序贯决策问题：逐个评估基模型，实时监控当前投票结果，当某一类别的票数已形成不可逆转的多数时立即停止。作者提出三种不同的最优性定义（notions of optimality），在给定可容忍分歧率（disagreement rate）约束下，将最优停止策略的求解转化为线性规划（Linear Program）问题，并可高效求解。

Result: 在UCI机器学习仓库的真实数据集以及Grinsztajn等人提出的基准数据集上进行实验。结果表明，在大多数数据集上，该方法在控制与完整集成模型分歧率仅为0.1%的条件下，实现了4倍以上的推理加速。

Conclusion: 通过序贯测试和线性规划求解最优提前停止策略，可以在几乎不损失集成模型精度的前提下，大幅降低二分类推理的计算成本，具有重要的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Ensemble classifiers are predictive models that combine the results of simpler base models, often by majority vote. A classic example is random forests, which combine the predictions of decision trees. Ensembles that use more base models can be more accurate but also more costly to train and run. In this paper, we consider strategies for reducing the computational cost of binary classification using an approach from the field of sequential testing. Rather than evaluating all the base models and taking a majority vote, we evaluate the base models sequentially and stop execution when a clear majority emerges. We consider three different notions of optimality for early-stopping strategies that minimize the number of base models executed while controlling the rate of disagreement with the full ensemble. For each notion of optimality and allowable disagreement rate, we show that a linear program can be constructed and solved efficiently to find the optimal stopping strategy. We tested these methods on real-world datasets taken from the UC Irvine Machine Learning repository, and on the benchmark datasets proposed by Grinsztajn et al. We found that on most datasets, these methods provide speed-ups of 4x or more while controlling disagreement at 0.1%

</details>


### [84] [Generating-Element Maximum Entropy for Non-Gaussian Uncertainty Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15360)
*Serhii Zabolotnii*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文研究矩约束最大熵（MaxEnt）密度重建中约束函数（生成元）的选择问题。作者证明经典单项式基仅是Kunchenko分解空间中的一种选择，并提出三种替代生成元：分数幂元素（PATP）、三角（特征函数）元素和对数-有理元素，后者可精确表示代数尾部。还证明了奇函数元素无法表示非均匀对称密度的奇偶容许性定理。核心教训是需将生成元与目标分布的尾部类别相匹配。实验表明，分数幂元素在双峰混合上比六矩单项式基线降低MSE 8.5倍，而对数-有理元素可从单约束恢复Cauchy尾部指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典矩约束最大熵方法使用单项式约束x^i，但该选择——而非求解器本身——决定了可表示的密度类型及对偶问题的条件数。现有方法在重尾分布上常不可行，且单项式基对尾部表征能力有限。作者旨在系统研究约束生成元的选择空间，为不同尾部类别的目标分布匹配合适的生成元。

Method: 在统一的对偶求解器框架下，研究三类Kunchenko分解空间生成元：(1) 分数幂元素PATP，将分数矩指数选择约化为有符号支撑上的一维扫描；(2) 三角元素，利用特征函数约束，其Hessian始终有界；(3) 对数-有理元素log(1+(x/s)^2)，单约束即可恢复Student/Cauchy族。提出奇偶容许性定理：奇函数生成元无法表示非均匀对称密度。引入方差最优规则(oPMM-alpha)为特定报告泛函选择生成元，以及解析乘积矩评估器使优化确定性且快于MC。

Result: 双峰高斯混合上，扫描选择的分数元素在全部20个种子点上比六矩单项式基线降低重建MSE 8.5倍；三角元素条件数最优。重尾分布上，分数元素在单项式MaxEnt不可行的19/20种子上恢复可行性，重建主体（KS=0.068），但尾部不佳；匹配的对数-有理元素从单约束恢复Cauchy尾部指数。解析乘积矩评估器消除了蒙特卡洛的噪声违规。

Conclusion: MaxEnt密度重建的质量取决于约束生成元与目标分布尾部类别的匹配。单项式基并非通用最优，应根据目标尾部行为选择分数幂、三角或对数-有理元素。设计映射"生成元↔尾部类别"是统一的方法论原则。

Abstract: Moment-constrained maximum entropy (MaxEnt) reconstructs probability densities from a few moments in uncertainty evaluation (GUM) and reliability analysis. The classical method uses monomial constraints x^i. We show that monomials are merely one choice of generating element of the underlying Kunchenko decomposition space, and that this choice -- more than the solver -- governs which densities are representable and how well-conditioned the dual problem is. We study three elements under one dual solver: a fractional-power element (PATP) that reduces fractional-moment exponent selection to a one-dimensional scan on signed supports; a trigonometric (characteristic-function) element whose constraints exist for every distribution and keep the dual Hessian bounded; and a logarithmic-rational element log(1+(x/s)^2) whose single constraint yields the Student/Cauchy family (1+(x/s)^2)^lambda, representing algebraic tails the first two do not produce. A parity-admissibility theorem shows that an element of odd functions cannot represent any non-uniform symmetric density; the unifying lesson is a design map matching the element to the target's tail class. Empirically, on a bimodal Gaussian mixture the scan-selected fractional member cuts reconstruction MSE by 8.5x over the six-moment monomial baseline (all 20 seeds), while the trigonometric element is best-conditioned. On heavy tails the fractional element restores feasibility where monomial MaxEnt is infeasible (19/20 seeds) and reconstructs the body (KS 0.068) but not the tail, whereas the matched logarithmic element recovers the Cauchy tail index from one constraint. A variance-optimal rule (oPMM-alpha) selects the element for the reported functional. An analytical product-moment evaluator makes a measurement-and-verification optimization fitness exactly deterministic and faster than Monte Carlo, removing its noise-induced violations.

</details>


### [85] [Interim Monitoring as an Information-Time Alignment Problem: The WCR Framework for Time-to-Event Trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15445)
*Haitao Pan,Zhongheng Cai*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: WCR框架通过锁定队列规模和校准最低随访时间，将中期分析时机与随访成熟度直接对齐，解决了事件驱动设计中不可预测的日历延迟和入组驱动设计中随访不成熟的问题，并在罕见儿科肿瘤试验模拟中展示了稳定的操作特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统事件驱动设计的中期分析时机受事件累积速度影响，日历时间不可预测；入组驱动设计虽然时间可预测，但随访成熟度不足。两者在稀疏事件、缓慢入组和长时程终点的单臂试验中均存在局限，需要一种能同时兼顾信息成熟度与操作可行性的新框架。

Method: 提出WCR（Window-Cohort with Calibrated Follow-Up Requirement）框架：预设一个锁定队列规模，并在该队列入组完成后设定一个校准的最低随访时间要求。中期分析在锁定队列满足该随访要求后进行，后续入组患者仅用于最终分析。框架区分两种随访类型——针对地标生存估计量的限制性随访和针对比例风险估计量的非限制性随访。设计参数（队列规模、随访时长）与决策阈值通过约束优化联合校准，以控制I类错误和把握度，同时平衡日历时间、中期成熟度和决策延迟。方法已实现为R包WCRBayesDesign。

Result: 以罕见儿科肿瘤试验为背景的模拟研究表明，WCR在校准模型下达到目标操作特性，且相比传统事件驱动和入组驱动方法，其中期分析时机更稳定、可解释性更强。

Conclusion: WCR将中期监测重新定义为信息-时间对齐问题，为稀疏事件、缓慢入组和长时程终点的单臂试验提供了实用的设计策略。

Abstract: Interim monitoring in time-to-event trials must balance inferential maturity with operationally meaningful timing. Event-driven designs align analyses with event accumulation but can produce substantial and unpredictable calendar delays, whereas enrollment-driven designs provide predictable timing but may rely on immature follow-up. We propose the Window-Cohort with Calibrated Follow-Up Requirement (WCR) framework, which directly parameterizes follow-up maturity through a locked cohort size and a post-lock follow-up requirement. The interim analysis is conducted after the prespecified cohort has accrued the calibrated minimum follow-up, while enrollment may continue and later patients are reserved for the final analysis. The framework distinguishes restricted follow-up for landmark survival estimands from unrestricted follow-up for proportional hazards estimands, thereby linking the effective information horizon to the estimand. Design parameters and decision thresholds are jointly calibrated through constrained optimization to control type I error and power while balancing calendar time, interim maturity, and decision-lag burden. Simulation studies motivated by a rare pediatric oncology trial show that WCR attains target operating characteristics under the calibration model and offers more stable and interpretable interim timing than conventional event-driven and enrollment-driven approaches. The methodology is implemented in the open-source R package WCRBayesDesign, available on CRAN. WCR reframes interim monitoring as an information-time alignment problem and provides a practical design strategy for single-arm trials with sparse events, slow accrual, and long-horizon endpoints.

</details>


### [86] [Kernel Density Estimation by Spectral Decomposition: Data-Driven Tapering and Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15450)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在特征函数域中处理核密度估计的带宽选择和密度估计问题，将带宽视为谱截断点，提出了自动带宽选择器、自适应Wiener估计器，以及结合高斯混合与带限残差的叠加方法，并用数据驱动的底噪估计提升对堆积和舍入数据的鲁棒性。在Marron-Wand基准测试和大样本下，谱方法以低偏差优势超越交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统核密度估计高度依赖带宽选择，而固定带宽在尖锐峰值、梳状等多模态密度上表现不佳。作者希望从频率域视角统一带宽选择与密度估计，利用经验特征函数的谱结构，实现自适应、最优的密度估计，并处理带测量误差的反卷积问题。

Method: 核心框架：在特征函数域中，分组数据的循环群平均协方差以平方经验特征函数为谱，真实特征函数位于采样噪声底限1/n之上，带宽即两者交汇的谱截断频率。方法包括：(1) 自动带宽选择器：剥离噪声底限，最小化频域误差准则；(2) 自适应Wiener估计器：将固定核推广为逐频率最优Wiener锥削，并在同一框架下处理已知测量误差的反卷积；(3) 两种混合策略：逐段划分与平滑基+带限残差的叠加（默认方案）；(4) 数据驱动的底限估计：从谱中读取底限替代假设的1/n，增强对堆积和舍入数据的鲁棒性。评估采用精确积分平方误差在Marron-Wand基准上进行。

Result: 自动选择器在平滑密度上匹配经验法则，在多模态密度上接近最优固定带宽。自适应Wiener估计器在大多数标准密度（包括尖锐峰值和梳状密度）上匹配或超越最优固定带宽。数据驱动底限在堆积和舍入数据上保持鲁棒。Marron-Wand基准测试显示：n=100时交叉验证领先，n=5000时Wiener滤波和叠加方法分别位列前两名，体现了偏差-方差权衡——谱估计器偏差低但方差较高，大样本下优势显现。方法在六组真实数据集和合成数据质量检查中得到验证，所有实验可复现。

Conclusion: 在特征函数域中进行密度估计和带宽选择是一种有效范式：自动选择器提供可靠的固定带宽参考，Wiener自适应估计器能解析尖锐结构，与高斯混合的叠加组合在灵活性和经济性之间取得平衡；数据驱动底限使方法对数据质量问题具有鲁棒性；谱方法在大样本下因低偏差特性而优于交叉验证方法。

Abstract: Kernel density estimation depends largely on one choice, the smoothing bandwidth. We treat bandwidth selection and density estimation in the characteristic-function domain, where the cyclic group-averaged covariance of the binned data has the squared empirical characteristic function as its spectrum: the true characteristic function sits over a sampling-noise floor of $1/n$, and the bandwidth is the spectral cutoff where the two meet. Several methods follow. An automatic selector strips the floor and minimizes a frequency-domain error criterion, matching the rule of thumb on smooth densities and approaching the best fixed bandwidth on multimodal ones. An adaptive estimator generalizes the fixed kernel to the per-frequency optimal Wiener taper, matching or surpassing the best fixed bandwidth on most standard densities, including sharply peaked and comb-like cases where fixed bandwidths fail; deconvolution under known measurement error follows in the same domain. Because the Wiener estimator resolves sharp structure but does not fit smooth bases as economically as a mixture, a Gaussian mixture is combined with it two ways, a piecewise partition and a superposition of a smooth base and a band-limited residual, the default. A data-driven floor read from the spectrum replaces the assumed $1/n$ floor and stays robust on heaped and rounded data. On the Marron-Wand benchmark scored by exact integrated squared error, the advantage emerges with sample size, a bias-variance tradeoff: the spectral estimators carry low bias but pay in variance, so cross-validation leads at $n=100$ while the Wiener filter and superposition take the top two ranks at $n=5000$. The methods are validated on six real datasets (CRSP returns, NHANES self-reports, CMS dimuon and SDSS spectra, a random-beacon stream, and UNSW-NB15 traffic) and on a synthetic-data quality check. All experiments are reproducible.

</details>


### [87] [A Bayesian Functional Accelerated Failure-Time Model with Varying Effects Correcting for Measurement Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15478)
*Joseph Yang,Roger Zoh,Carmen Tekwe,Lan Xue*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种贝叶斯函数型加速失效时间（AFT）模型，通过高斯过程单指标结构对函数系数随时间和标量协变量变化的非线性联合效应进行建模，并利用工具变量处理函数型协变量的测量误差。方法通过模拟验证并在REGARDS研究中应用于步数体力活动与缺血性卒中死亡时间的关联分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有函数型AFT的频率学派方法难以灵活捕捉标量协变量与时间对函数效应的联合非线性影响，且未能妥善处理函数型暴露中常见的测量误差。可穿戴设备采集的体力活动数据正是典型的同时存在测量误差和异质性健康效应的函数型数据。

Method: 提出贝叶斯函数型AFT模型：函数系数被建模为依赖于时间和标量协变量的变系数函数（varying coefficient function），采用高斯过程单指标（single-index）结构实现灵活的非线性亚组修饰；测量误差通过将代理观测与工具变量配对来处理，该工具变量能适应与潜在函数型暴露的非线性关联。

Result: 模拟研究表明，所提方法能恢复真实的变系数函数效应，且相比忽略测量误差的朴素模型显著降低偏倚。在REGARDS研究应用中，该方法揭示了步数体力活动与缺血性卒中死亡时间之间的关联在不同种族和地区亚组中呈现异质性。

Conclusion: 所提出的贝叶斯函数型AFT模型能够同时处理函数型协变量的测量误差及其效应在不同亚组中的异质性，为可穿戴设备等生物医学函数型数据的生存分析提供了更为灵活和可靠的框架。

Abstract: Functional data collected as continuously observed trajectories arise naturally in many biomedical settings, and a key inferential goal is understanding how such functional covariates relate to time-to-event outcomes while allowing that relationship to vary across subgroups defined by scalar characteristics. Existing frequentist approaches to functional accelerated failure-time (AFT) models struggle to flexibly capture the joint, nonlinear influence of scalar covariates and time on the functional effect, and none adequately address the measurement error that frequently contaminates functionally observed exposures. We propose a Bayesian functional AFT model in which the functional coefficient is a varying effect function of both time and a set of scalar covariates, modeled through a Gaussian process single-index structure that provides a flexible, nonlinear framework for subgroup modification. Measurement error in the functional covariate is handled by pairing a proxy observation with an instrumental variable that accommodates non-linear associations with the latent functional exposure. A prominent source of such functional data is wearable devices, which can continuously monitor physical activity (PA) behavioral patterns over time, yet whose outputs are well known to be prone to measurement error and to exhibit heterogeneous associations with health outcomes across demographic subgroups. Through simulations, we show that our approach recovers the true varying functional effects and reduces bias relative tonaïve models that ignore measurement error. We apply our methods to the Reasons for Geographical and Racial Differences in Stroke (REGARDS) study to investigate how step-count physical activity relates to time-to-death from ischemic stroke across racial and regional groups.

</details>


### [88] [Latent Variable Models for Distributional Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15526)
*Luna Fazio,Paul-Christian Bürkner*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布特征潜变量模型（DFLVM），一个将个体间分布特征差异建模为随机截距的统一框架，可在单步估计中同时估计分布特征及其与下游结果的关联，避免了两步法中因忽略估计误差导致的偏差和错误率上升问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理学研究中常见的两步法——先估计分布特征（如变异性、偏度），再将估计值作为预测变量——忽略了第一步的估计误差，导致有偏估计和错误率膨胀。需要一个能够一步完成估计的统计框架来解决此问题。

Method: 提出分布特征潜变量模型（DFLVM），将个体间的分布特征差异（如均值、方差、偏度等）表示为随机截距，并将这些潜变量同时作为下游结果的预测变量，在单一估计步骤中完成所有参数估计。通过模拟研究和真实数据再分析验证方法性能。

Result: 模拟研究和真实数据再分析表明，DFLVM相比两步法能够有效纠正因忽略估计误差导致的偏差，降低错误率，提供更准确的参数估计。

Conclusion: DFLVM为研究者提供了一个统一、灵活且统计上更严谨的框架，用于分析分布特征与其他结果变量之间的关联，优于传统两步法。

Abstract: Analyzing the mean response of study subjects in psychological research is a standard, well-justified practice. However, theoretical arguments and empirical evidence also suggest that there is value in investigating other aspects of the distribution of such responses, such as their variability or skewness.
  A particular challenge that practitioners face is statistical modeling of associations between distributional features and other outcomes of interest. The most common approach is to perform estimation in two steps: distributional features are estimated first, and then those estimates are used as predictors for the relevant outcomes. Such an approach is most amenable to implementation in standard statistical software, but it ignores estimation error and can therefore lead to biased estimates and increased error rates.
  We introduce Distributional Feature Latent Variable Models (DFLVM), a general framework that represents between-person difference in distributional features as random intercepts. These intercepts can be simultaneously used as predictors for downstream outcomes and their associations estimated in a single estimation step. We compare the performance of our approach against two-step procedures in a simulation study and through a re-analysis of a real dataset.

</details>


### [89] [Bias-Aware External-Model-Assisted Inference in High-Dimensional Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15602)
*Hongzhe Zhang,Hanxuan Ye,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: DEAL是一种用于高维半监督线性回归的新方法，通过将外部预测器路由到去偏估计量的方差中，并结合跨拟合收缩步骤，在多种数据场景下实现了比去偏Lasso、PPI和PPI++更短的置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在PPI框架中，线性模型下的校正项会抵消外部预测器，导致PPI和PPI++退化为普通最小二乘，且当预测器接近Oracle时反而膨胀方差。需要一种能自适应目标-only、近Oracle和有偏但有用三种场景的方法。

Method: 提出DEAL方法：(1)将外部估计量和未标记协变量路由到去偏估计量的方差中；(2)采用偏差感知的交叉拟合收缩步骤，自适应跨越三种数据场景；(3)扩展到误设定和非线性标注器下的投影参数；(4)提出偏移感知变体以应对协变量偏移。

Result: 理论上证明了坐标渐近正态性及自适应方差；仿真中DEAL区间长度为去偏Lasso的0.49-0.87倍；六个真实数据集（天文、化学、蛋白质组学、肿瘤学）上均缩短了置信区间，中位长度比为0.23-0.53。

Conclusion: DEAL在统一的未标记数据预算下，始终产生比去偏Lasso、PPI和PPI++更短的置信区间，在理论和实践上均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: In high-dimensional semi-supervised linear regression, prediction-powered inference (PPI) corrects an external predictor with a rectifier estimated from the labeled data. In a linear model, however, this rectifier cancels the predictor: PPI and PPI++ reduce to ordinary least squares and can inflate variance when the predictor is close to the oracle. We propose the Debiased External-model-Assisted Lasso (DEAL), which routes the external estimator and the unlabeled covariates into the variance of a debiased estimator, with a bias-aware, cross-fitted shrinkage step that adapts across target-only, near-oracle, and biased-but-informative regimes. We prove coordinate-wise asymptotic normality with an adaptive variance, extend validity to the projection parameter under misspecification and nonlinear labelers, and show that, at a common unlabeled budget, DEAL intervals are shorter than those of debiased Lasso, PPI, and PPI++; a shift-aware variant preserves coverage under covariate shift. In simulations, DEAL intervals are 0.49-0.87 of the debiased-Lasso length, and across six real-data applications spanning astronomy, chemistry, proteomics, and oncology, the last using a large-language-model oracle, they tighten in every case, with median length ratios of 0.23-0.53.

</details>


### [90] [Paired Sample Tests for High-dimensional Uncorrelatedness via Random Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15636)
*Shiyao Huang,Xiaojun Song*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的非参数检验方法，用于检验两个高维随机向量之间的不相关性。该检验通过推广Jiang等人(2023, 2024)提出的随机积分方法，构建了一个估计协方差矩阵加权平方L2范数的检验统计量。在零假设下，该统计量渐近服从标准正态分布，且无需对n与p的相对大小做任何假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高维独立性检验在检测"弱但普遍"的相依结构时功效不足，且许多方法对样本量n与维度p的相对增长速度有严格限制。本文旨在提出一种更灵活、功效更高的非参数检验方法。

Method: 将Jiang等人(2023, 2024)的随机积分思想推广至两样本情形，构造检验统计量以估计协方差矩阵的加权平方L2范数；在n和p同时发散至无穷的框架下推导统计量的渐近性质，证明其在零假设下的渐近正态性（均值为0，方差为1），且该结果对n与p的相对量级无约束。

Result: Monte Carlo模拟表明，该方法在有限样本下表现良好。与现有检验相比，在保持相近经验水平的同时，对"弱但普遍"的相依结构具有更高的检验功效。DNA甲基化与基因表达相关性分析的实际数据进一步验证了所提方法的优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的检验方法有效克服了现有方法在检测微弱但普遍的相依关系时的不足，且对维度与样本量的相对关系无限制，在高维数据分析中具有广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel nonparametric test to assess the uncorrelatedness between two high-dimensional random vectors. We develop our test by generalizing the random integration proposed by Jiang et al. (2023, 2024), and the resulting test statistic estimates a weighted squared $\mathscr{L}_2$ norm of the covariance matrix. Asymptotic properties of the test statistic are derived by letting both the sample size $n$ and the dimension $p$ diverge to infinity. Under the null hypothesis of uncorrelatedness, our proposed test statistic is asymptotically normal with zero mean and unit variance, without requiring any specification of the relative magnitude regarding $n$ and $p$. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate the good finite-sample performance of our proposed methods. Compared with many existing tests, our test statistic is more powerful at detecting ``weak but pervasive'' dependence while maintaining a comparable empirical size. The advantages of the proposed methods are further illustrated by an empirical analysis that assesses the correlation between DNA methylation and gene expression.

</details>


### [91] [Bounding Causal Effects for Ordinal Outcomes Under Positive Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15754)
*Micha Mandel,Daniel Rodan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对序数数据的因果效应界定与估计，已有基于独立性工作假设的界虽然更紧但缺乏理论保证。本文提出"对角尾部占优"（DTD）这一新的相依性条件，证明在该条件下独立性界是有效的，同时指出该条件较强，进而引入局部DTD概念以推导改进的界，扩展了独立性假设在序数因果推断中的合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准平均处理效应对序数尺度不适用，而替代估计量（如处理结果优于或未恶化对照结果的概率）通常不可识别。尽管基于独立性工作假设得到的界比仅用边际分布得到的界更紧，但其有效性条件尚不明确，现有正相依性条件不足以提供理论保证。

Method: 提出对角尾部占优（DTD）条件，证明在此条件下独立性界成立；分析该条件的强度与局限性；引入局部DTD概念，在概率表的选定部分利用独立性工作假设推导改进的界；通过理论结果、数值示例及急性缺血性脑卒中临床试验数据加以验证。

Result: 常用正相依性概念（正象限相依、正回归相依）不足以支撑独立性界；DTD条件能保障独立性界成立，但该条件较强，在多数场景下可能不适用；局部DTD在应用中更可行，据此可导出改进的界。

Conclusion: DTD条件为独立性界提供了理论保证，但其强度限制了适用范围；局部DTD提供了一种更灵活的替代方案，在保持界的有效性的同时扩展了实际应用的可行性。

Abstract: Defining and estimating causal effects for ordinal data is challenging. Standard average treatment effects are not appropriate for ordinal scales, and alternative estimands, such as the probabilities that the treatment outcome exceeds or does not worsen the control outcome, are generally not identifiable. Existing work provides sharp bounds for these quantities based only on marginal distributions. Motivated by a previous observation showing that bounds obtained under an independence working assumption can be substantially tighter, we investigate conditions under which such bounds are valid. We show that commonly used notions of positive dependence, including positive quadrant dependence and positive regression dependence, are not sufficient to justify these bounds. We then propose a new dependence condition, diagonal tail dominance (DTD), under which the independence-based bounds are guaranteed to hold. We explain why this condition is quite strong and may not be appropriate in many settings, limiting the justification for using the independence-based bounds. However, local DTD may be plausible in many applications, and we derive improved bounds that exploit an independence working assumption on selected parts of the probability table. Through theoretical results, numerical examples, and an analysis of data from a clinical trial of a new treatment for acute ischemic stroke, we illustrate the properties of the bounds and the role of the proposed conditions.

</details>


### [92] [Archetypal analysis of European 10-year government bond yields with multidimensional scaling of two-mode three-way asymmetric dissimilarities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15876)
*Aleix Alcacer,Rafael Benitez,Vicente J. Bolos,Irene Epifanio*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文应用一种从三向非对称邻近数据中提取原型轮廓的新方法，分析了23个欧洲国家在三个时间段内10年期国债收益率的小波相干性非对称相异度矩阵，通过h图和原型分析识别核心国家并量化非对称程度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何从三向、双模式、非对称的相异度数据中提取有意义的原型轮廓，以揭示欧洲国家间国债收益率联动关系的潜在结构和非对称性特征。

Method: 首先计算无条件双模式三向数据的h图，将其表示在统一欧氏空间中实现可视化；随后进行原型分析（archetypoid analysis），识别原型国家并将其他所有国家表达为这些原型实例的混合；同时计算每个国家的非对称度以补充结构信息。

Result: 成功将23×23×3的非对称相异度矩阵映射到可解释的欧氏空间，识别出关键的原型国家，并量化了各国在国债收益率联动中的非对称程度。

Conclusion: 该方法学框架能够有效处理三向非对称邻近数据，为理解欧洲主权债券市场的互联性和非对称依赖关系提供了直观且可解释的分析工具，数据与代码的公开也支持了可重复研究。

Abstract: A recent methodology for extracting archetypal profiles from three-way asymmetric proximity data is applied to a dataset comprising 23 x 23 x 3 two-mode, three-way asymmetric dissimilarity matrices. The asymmetric dissimilarities are based on the oriented wavelet squared coherence of 10-year government bond yields among 23 European countries across three time intervals from 2001 to the present. First, the h-plot is computed for the unconditional two-mode three-way data, which is then represented in a unified Euclidean space, providing an intuitive and interpretable visualization. Subsequently, archetypoid analysis is performed. This unsupervised methodology identifies the archetypal countries and expresses all remaining countries as mixtures of these archetypal instances. Additionally, the degree of asymmetry for each country is calculated, offering further insight into the structure of the dissimilarities. The dataset and code are provided to support reproducible research.

</details>

### [93] [Audited Conformal Prediction for Classification under Unknown Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14909)
*Yanfei Zhou,Rizal Fathony,Nam H. Nguyen,Matteo Sesia*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出审计共形预测（ACP），利用目标域少量标注数据训练审计模型来识别预训练分类器可能失败的输入，将其融入共形预测框架，在保证边际覆盖率的同时显著提升条件覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练分类模型在部署时面临未知分布漂移，现有共形预测方法虽能保证边际覆盖率，但条件覆盖率（特别是在模型易失败的子群体上）往往不足。需一种利用少量目标域标注数据即能同时保证边际覆盖并提升条件覆盖的方法。

Method: 提出审计共形预测（ACP）：（1）从目标分布采集少量标注数据训练审计模型，用于识别预训练模型可能失败的输入；（2）设计两种审计分数整合策略——第一种以改进条件覆盖为目标的边际覆盖方案，第二种提供显式的组条件覆盖保证；（3）为两种策略分别建立理论保证，分析预测集大小与条件覆盖的权衡。

Result: 在合成数据和真实世界数据集上的实验表明，ACP在保持边际覆盖率达标的同时，条件覆盖率显著优于现有方法，并揭示了预测集大小与条件覆盖率之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: ACP通过审计模型与共形预测的有效整合，在分布漂移场景下实现了对预训练模型预测可靠性的精细量化，兼具理论保证和实用价值。

Abstract: We consider the problem of uncertainty quantification for a pretrained classification model deployed under unknown distribution shift. We propose Audited Conformal Prediction (ACP), a method that leverages a small labeled dataset from the target population to train an auxiliary audit model identifying inputs where the legacy model is likely to fail. By integrating the audit model's outputs into the conformal prediction framework, ACP produces prediction sets that guarantee marginal coverage while achieving substantially higher conditional coverage in practice than existing approaches. We develop and analyze two complementary integration strategies -- one targeting marginal coverage with improved conditional performance, the other providing explicit group-conditional coverage guarantees -- and establish theoretical guarantees for both. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate the method and illustrate trade-offs between prediction set size and conditional coverage.

</details>


### [94] [Conformal Candidate Certification for Offline Model-Based Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15217)
*Seungjin Choi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: CCC 是一种事后包装方法，利用重要性加权共形预测为离线 MBO 的候选设计提供校准的单侧下界，仅推进边界超过目标阈值的候选，在分布偏移下仍能维持名义覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线 MBO 方法在分布外区域进行激进优化时，代理模型的排序可靠性最差，且缺乏对每个候选是否满足目标阈值的统计认证（即置信下界）。

Method: 提出 CCC（Conformal Candidate Certification）：利用熵正则化代理最大化诱导 Gibbs 倾斜分布，将同一代理模型用于构建重要性权重，结合加权共形预测为每个候选输出校准的单侧下界，仅推进下界超过目标的候选。无需额外的密度比估计步骤。

Result: 在受控合成实验中，CCC 以名义覆盖率 0.90 认证了激进候选池中 16.7% 的候选，经验覆盖率达 0.990；而忽略协变量偏移的标准共形预测覆盖率崩溃至 0.416。

Conclusion: CCC 作为一种事后方法，能够在不增加密度比估计负担的情况下，为离线 MBO 提供可靠的逐候选统计认证，有效缓解分布偏移带来的代理不可靠问题。

Abstract: Offline model-based optimization (MBO) proposes candidates by optimizing a surrogate trained on a fixed historical dataset. Because candidates are deliberately out-of-distribution, surrogate rankings are least reliable exactly where the optimizer is most aggressive, yet existing methods provide no per-candidate statistical certificate that a design meets a target threshold. We propose \emph{Conformal Candidate Certification} (CCC), a post-hoc wrapper that attaches a calibrated one-sided lower bound to each candidate and advances only those whose bound exceeds the target. We show that entropy-regularized surrogate maximization induces a Gibbs-tilted proposal, so the same surrogate supplies importance weights for weighted conformal prediction without a separate density-ratio estimation step. In a controlled synthetic study, CCC certifies $16.7\%$ of an aggressive proposal pool with empirical coverage 0.990 at nominal 0.90, while standard conformal prediction ignoring the covariate shift collapses to 0.416 coverage.

</details>


### [95] [Finite Resources False Discovery Rate Control in Structured Hypothesis Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15393)
*Binyamin Perets,Shie Mannor*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）的FDR控制框架，解决两个核心挑战：p值因有限零分布抽样而不确定，以及假设空间存在任意结构。框架包含两种决策规则——一种保 exact FDR control，另一种通过放宽镜像对称性提升统计功效，并基于解析框架给出零分布样本高效分配策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模假设检验中，获取零分布参考数据成本高昂，导致p值存在有限样本不确定性；同时假设空间常具有内在结构（如层次、图结构），现有方法难以在控制FDR的同时有效利用该结构。作者旨在填补这一双重空白。

Method: 将假设空间结构通过再生核嵌入RKHS；提出两种决策规则：(1) 保证精确FDR控制的鲁棒规则；(2) 将mirror-statistic推广至count space的最大功效规则，在放松精确镜像对称条件下用解析框架评估FDR；利用RKHS的可处理性分析有限数据不确定性，进而优化零分布抽样分配策略。

Result: 两种规则均对结构误指定具有鲁棒性：规则一严格保证FDR控制；规则二在放松镜像对称后仍可通过解析框架评估FDR，并获得更高的统计功效。RKHS框架使有限数据不确定性可直接分析，由此导出高效的零分布样本分配策略。

Conclusion: 该框架成功统一了有限零分布样本下的不确定性与结构化假设空间中的FDR控制，提供了精确控制与高功效之间的灵活权衡，并为实验资源分配提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Scientific discovery relies on large-scale hypothesis testing. However, the capacity to identify true discoveries while controlling false discovery faces major challenges: obtaining relevant reference data (the null distribution) is resource-intensive, leaving finite-data uncertainty, and the procedure should account for the inherent structure in the hypothesis space, when such structure exists. Here, we present a framework for controlling the false discovery rate both when each hypothesis is evidenced only by a finite count of null draws, leaving its p-value uncertain, and when the hypothesis space carries arbitrary structure, requiring only that the structure be represented through a suitable reproducing kernel. We present two decision rules that are both robust to structural mis-specification, yet offer a distinct trade-off between exact FDR control and statistical power. The first rule guarantees exact FDR control; the second maximizes power by adapting mirror-statistic control into count space, utilizing an analytical framework to assess FDR control when exact mirror symmetry is relaxed. Furthermore, the tractability gained by the RKHS framework allows us to directly investigate finite-data uncertainties, which we leverage to suggest a policy for the efficient allocation of null distribution samples.

</details>


### [96] [The Reverse Telescoping Coordinate System for Positive Definite Matrices: Geometry, Computation, and Generative Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15442)
*Anindya Bhadra*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的对称正定矩阵无约束坐标系统——逆向伸缩映射(RT)，将SPD矩阵分解为体积(对数行列式)、形状(对数相对对角尺度和偏协方差)三部分，实现了$O(p^2)$的矩阵与逆矩阵运算，并基于此设计了体积-形状分离的流模型，在生成建模和SPD流形上的扩散任务中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SPD矩阵参数化方法(如矩阵对数)存在局限：雅可比行列式依赖于完整的矩阵结构而非仅行列式，且无法将矩阵与其逆在变换域中进行无损符号表示。此外，SPD约束在生成建模中难以处理，缺乏将行列式(体积)与形状自然解耦的框架。

Method: 作者设计了逆向伸缩映射$\Theta(x)=\rm{RT}(x)$，其中$x=(v,d,r)$分别表示对数体积、对数相对对角尺度、偏协方差。该映射具有以下性质：(1)雅可比仅依赖于对数行列式；(2)$x$同时无损表示矩阵及其逆；(3)变换域中单位行列式矩阵可由直线连接且路径上保持单位行列式。基于此，提出分离的体积-形状流模型：用条件流匹配在单位行列式路径上传输形状，用单独的一维流传输体积。

Result: 该方法在高达$p=200$的SPD矩阵生成建模中得到验证，包括困难的合成双峰目标分布和基于fMRI数据的脑连接网络生成任务，以及SPD流形上的内蕴扩散任务。

Conclusion: 逆向伸缩映射为SPD矩阵提供了一种新的无约束参数化，将SPD约束转化为结构化优势，使得在某种意义上对SPD矩阵设计体积归一化的形状流比在无约束空间中更容易。该框架在生成建模和扩散模型中展现了良好的可扩展性和有效性。

Abstract: We design a new unconstrained coordinate system where a $p\times p$ symmetric positive definite (SPD) matrix $Θ$ is represented by a reverse telescoping map $Θ(x)=\rm{RT}(x)$, with $x=(v,d,r)\in\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{(p-1)}\times\mathbb{R}^{p(p-1)/2}$, representing respectively the log volume or log determinant; and the shape, as encoded by log relative diagonal scales and partial covariances among the nodes. This construction results in important properties not available in other charts, e.g., matrix logarithm, such as Jacobian depending on only the log-determinant. A useful feature of our construction is $x$ contains a lossless symbolic representation of both the matrix and its inverse. Many important computations involving a matrix and its inverse can be performed in $O(p^2)$ in the transformed domain, while it is the rendering of results in matrix forms (on demand) that must incur an $O(p^3)$ cost. Moreover, two unit-determinant matrices in the transformed domain can be joined by a straight line with pathwise unit determinant. For generative modeling, this allows designing a split volume-shape flow model trained by conditional flow matching for transporting the shape over the unit-determinant path, with a separate one-dimensional flow for transporting the volume or the determinant. The forbidding SPD constraint, tamed thus into a powerful guiding force, leads to the surprising insight that it is in some sense easier to design a volume-normalized shape flow for SPD compared to the unconstrained $\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}$, with no intrinsic notion of volume to aid normalization, unlike the determinant of SPD matrices. We apply our construction for up to $p=200$ in generative modeling of SPD matrices on a difficult synthetic bimodal target, and in generating brain connectivity networks by models trained on fMRI data; as well as in intrinsic diffusion on the SPD manifold.

</details>


### [97] [Structured Nonparametric Variational Inference for Dependent Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15458)
*Yuda Shao,Zhiling Gu,Shan Yu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SN-VI 是一种利用多元样条技术建模潜在变量间复杂依赖关系的结构化非参数变分推断框架，突破了传统平均场假设，在理论上保证了下界和渐近一致性，并在计算机视觉和空间转录组学等高维结构化数据上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分推断普遍依赖平均场假设，忽略了潜在变量之间的复杂依赖结构，导致后验近似精度不足。本文旨在提出一种能够自动捕捉并保留潜在变量间复杂依赖关系的灵活后验近似框架。

Method: 提出结构化非参数变分推断（SN-VI），核心是利用多元样条（multivariate spline）技术对潜在变量间的依赖结构进行建模。此外，开发了一种无需人工指定即可自动识别依赖变量及其底层依赖结构的算法。理论上推导了变分目标的下界，并证明了后验估计的渐近一致性。

Result: 模拟研究表明，SN-VI 在近似具有有界支撑和复杂依赖关系的后验分布方面表现有效。在计算机视觉和空间转录组学等应用场景中，SN-VI 提升了生成模型性能，并通过学习到的依赖结构有效揭示了耦合的生物信号。

Conclusion: SN-VI 突破了平均场变分推断的局限，通过多元样条灵活建模潜在变量依赖，兼顾理论严谨性和实践可行性，为大规模概率模型和生成式模型的贝叶斯学习提供了更精确的近似推断方案。

Abstract: Variational inference (VI) is a core engine of modern AI, enabling scalable approximate Bayesian learning and uncertainty-aware training of large probabilistic and generative models. In this paper, we propose Structured Nonparametric Variational Inference (SN-VI), a novel framework for modeling complex dependencies among latent variables in posterior approximation, leveraging multivariate spline techniques. Unlike traditional methods that rely on the mean-field assumption, SN-VI preserves intricate latent variable dependencies, providing a flexible and accurate approximation of posteriors with arbitrary shapes. We establish rigorous theoretical guarantees, including the derivation of the lower bound for the variational objective and proof of asymptotic consistency in posterior estimation. To facilitate practical implementation, we develop an algorithm that automatically identifies dependent latent variables and their underlying dependence structure, without requiring manual specification. Simulation studies validate the effectiveness of SN-VI in approximating posterior distributions with bounded support and complex dependencies. The proposed method has been successfully applied to high-dimensional structured data, including computer vision datasets and spatial transcriptomics. In these applications, SN-VI demonstrates improved generative model performance and effectively uncovers coupled biological signals through the learned dependency structure.

</details>


### [98] [Ricci-Filtration: Boosting Retrieval-Augmented Generation Reranker to Query-Answer Tasks by Discrete Ricci Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15482)
*Tian Qin,Wei-Min Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出了Ricci-Filtration框架，将离散Ricci流应用于RAG系统的重排序阶段。通过将查询和检索块建模为图网络，利用离散曲率和Ricci流评估每个块相对于查询的结构重要性，过滤掉具有负Ricci曲率和大权重的噪声块，再由重排序器处理剩余块。理论和实验证明该方法在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在检索阶段常引入噪声文档块，影响生成质量。受Ricci流在连续空间和离散图上通过曲率驱动扩散实现聚类分离的启发，本文旨在利用几何方法识别并过滤与查询不相关的噪声块，从而提升重排序和生成性能。

Method: 提出Ricci-Filtration框架：(1)将查询和初始检索块建模为加权图，节点为查询和块，边由嵌入相似度定义；(2)计算离散Ricci曲率（Ollivier-Ricci曲率），评估每个块与查询的几何关系；(3)应用归一化离散Ricci流演化边权，理论证明其渐近行为可检测社区结构；(4)过滤掉具有负Ricci曲率和大权重的噪声块；(5)重排序器处理剩余块以增强生成性能。

Result: 大量实验表明Ricci-Filtration在准确率、精确率、召回率和F1分数上均优于多个基线重排序方法。消融研究进一步证明该框架在不同设置和架构下均表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: Ricci-Filtration作为一种基于几何的重排序增强方法，能有效利用离散曲率信息过滤噪声文档块，提升RAG系统的检索和生成质量，展现出跨架构的鲁棒性和通用性。

Abstract: Ricci flow is a curvature-guided diffusion process that deforms space by shrinking regions of high positive curvature and expanding those with negative curvature. Similarly, discrete Ricci flow on weighted graphs modifies edge weights by shrinking edges with positive Ricci curvature and stretching those with negative Ricci curvature, effectively increasing the separation between clusters. Inspired by these two cornerstone works, we propose a geometry-based RAG reranker enhancement procedure called Ricci-Filtration. By modeling the input query and initial retrieved chunks as a network, where the input query and chunks serve as nodes and embedding-based pairwise relations define an initial graph, Ricci-Filtration leverages discrete curvature and Ricci flow to evaluate the structural importance of each chunk with respect to the user query. The system first filters the initial chunks based on their geometric curvature relative to the query; then, a reranker processes the remaining chunks to enhance generative performance. We theoretically prove that normalized discrete Ricci flow can detect community structures by identifying distinct asymptotic behaviors in edge weights. This supports the removal of ``noisy'' document chunks characterized by large weights and negative Ricci curvature relative to the query node. Extensive experiments confirm that Ricci-Filtration outperforms several baseline reranking methods in accuracy, precision, recall, and F1 scores. Furthermore, ablation studies demonstrate that the Ricci-Filtration generally outperforms the baseline under various settings, highlighting the framework's robustness across different architectures.

</details>


### [99] [Phase Transition in Convex Relaxations for Graph Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15581)
*Laurent Massoulié,Sushil Mahavir Varma,Louis Vassaux,Irène Waldspurger*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 研究了相关高斯正交系综矩阵的图对齐问题，分析了基于最小化 $\|AX - XB\|_F$ 的凸松弛方法。证明了当相关参数 $\sigma = o(n^{-1/2}/\log^4 n)$ 时，解集中在真实置换矩阵周围，并精确刻画了从 $o(n)$ 到 $\Omega(n)$ 的相变边界，显著收紧并扩展了先前结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大似然估计在图对齐问题中信息论最优，但归结为二次指派问题而不可计算。作者旨在分析计算可行的凸松弛方法，并精确刻画其在何种相关参数范围内能够恢复隐藏的顶点置换。

Method: 考虑两个相关对称高斯矩阵 $(A, B)$，相关性为 $1/\sqrt{1+\sigma^2}$。分析基于最小化 Frobenius 范数 $\|AX - XB\|_F$ 的凸松弛，约束域分别为双随机矩阵集合和单位超立方体。通过对解 $X^\star$ 与真实置换矩阵 $\Pi^\star$ 的 Frobenius 距离进行非渐近分析，建立相变边界。

Result: 当 $\sigma = o(n^{-1/2}/\log^4 n)$ 时，凸松弛的解 $X^\star$ 满足 $\|X^\star - \Pi^\star\|_F^2 = o(n)$，即通过简单后处理可恢复除消失比例外的所有顶点。与已有下界结合，精确刻画了相变：$\|X^\star - \Pi^\star\|_F^2$ 在 $\sigma = \tilde{o}(n^{-1/2})$ 时为 $o(n)$，在 $\sigma = \tilde{\Omega}(n^{-1/2})$ 时为 $\Omega(n)$。

Conclusion: 凸松弛方法在图对齐问题中存在尖锐的相变现象，阈值在 $\sigma \sim n^{-1/2}$ 附近。该分析显著收紧了先前结果，并将结论从双随机松弛拓展至更一般的单位超立方体松弛。

Abstract: We study the graph alignment problem for correlated Gaussian Orthogonal Ensemble (GOE) matrices, where the goal is to recover a hidden vertex permutation given two correlated symmetric Gaussian matrices $(A, B)$ with correlation $1/\sqrt{1+σ^2}$. While the maximum likelihood estimator is information-theoretically optimal, its computation, which reduces to a quadratic assignment problem, is intractable. Motivated by this, we analyze convex relaxations based on minimizing $\|AX - XB\|_F$ over the set of doubly stochastic matrices and the unit hypercube. We show that when the correlation parameter satisfies $σ= o(n^{-1/2}/\log^4 n)$, the solution of either relaxation $(X^\star)$ concentrates around the ground-truth permutation matrix $(Π^\star)$, i.e., $\|X^\star-Π^\star\|_F^2 = o(n)$, implying recovery of all but a vanishing fraction of vertices after simple post-processing. Combined with existing lower bounds, our results precisely characterize that $\|X^\star-Π^\star\|_F^2$ transitions from $o(n)$ for $σ= \tilde{o}(n^{-1/2})$ to $Ω(n)$ for $σ= \tildeΩ(n^{-1/2})$. In doing so, our analysis significantly tightens prior results and extends them beyond doubly stochastic relaxations.

</details>


### [100] [Information Gap and Feasibility-Aware Inference in Binomial Logistic Mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15665)
*Yuta Hayashida,Shonosuke Sugasawa*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 二项 Logistic 混合模型中，BIC 能检测到双组分结构，但标签恢复可能依然不可行。本文揭示了这一信息鸿沟的数学根源，并提出两种可行性感知的推断方法来避免误导性模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准似然准则（如 BIC）可以检测混合模型中的组分存在，但组分可检测并不保证个体标签可恢复。这一信息鸿沟在实践中可能导致统计推断的误导，作者旨在从理论上刻画并解决这一问题。

Method: 作者在固定试验次数（fixed number of trials）的二项 Logistic 混合模型框架下，分析了观测数据证据与逐观测标签信息在组分分离度下的局部阶数差异。理论推导表明，前者随样本量累积而增强，后者则不然，从而产生“可检测但不可恢复”区间。基于此，提出两种方法：(1) 引入后验熵惩罚的恢复感知 BIC（recoverability-aware BIC）；(2) 熵正则化估计量，抑制 MLE 过度分离组分和过度集中后验归属概率的倾向。

Result: 数值实验证实了理论预测的检测-恢复鸿沟，表明所提方法能有效避免误导性的组分选择，并改善了后验标签概率的校准度。

Conclusion: 在二项 Logistic 混合模型中，组分检测与标签恢复之间存在内在的信息鸿沟，存在“可检测但不可恢复”的参数区间。通过引入可行性感知的推断程序，可以避免仅依赖 BIC 可能导致的虚假发现，并获得更可靠的后验推断。

Abstract: This paper studies the information gap between mixture detection and label recovery in binomial logistic mixtures. Standard likelihood-based criteria such as the Bayesian information criterion (BIC) can detect the presence of two components, but this does not guarantee that the corresponding labels are recoverable. We show that this gap is intrinsic to binomial logistic mixtures with a fixed number of trials: observed-data evidence for mixture structure and per-observation information for label recovery have different local orders in the component separation, and only the former accumulates with the sample size. As a result, there exists a detectable-but-unrecoverable regime in which BIC selects two components while the posterior labels remain essentially uninformative. To address this issue, we propose two feasibility-aware inference procedures: a recoverability-aware BIC with a posterior-entropy penalty and an entropy-regularized estimator that mitigates the tendency of the maximum likelihood estimator to produce overly separated components and overly concentrated posterior responsibilities. Numerical experiments confirm the predicted gap and demonstrate that the proposed methods avoid misleading component selections and improve the calibration of posterior label probabilities.

</details>


### [101] [Stochastic trace estimation with tensor train random vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15679)
*Zvonimir Bujanović,Daniel Kressner,Hrvoje Olić*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯随机张量列车（Tensor Train）向量作为随机迹估计中非结构化高斯向量的结构化替代方案。结果表明，在合适的TT秩选择下（r ≥ d-1），可恢复与经典Girard-Hutchinson估计器相同的维度无关保证，包括中位数均值变体的误差-失败概率依赖关系、遗忘子空间注入性质，以及在Nyström++框架下达到O(ε⁻¹)样本复杂度的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在张量结构化场景中，非结构化的高斯或Rademacher测试向量存储和计算成本过高，而廉价的秩一张量积向量则需要随张量阶数指数增长的样本量。因此需要一种兼顾计算效率与统计效率的结构化随机向量。

Method: 采用高斯随机张量列车（TT）向量作为测试向量，分析其在Girard-Hutchinson迹估计器中的表现。具体包括：(1) 中位数均值（median-of-means）变体下的理论分析；(2) 遗忘子空间注入（oblivious subspace injection）性质证明；(3) 在Nyström++框架下结合谱尾部条件进行应用分析。

Result: （1）TT秩 r ≥ d-1 时，中位数均值估计器对精度ε和失败概率δ的依赖性与经典非结构化高斯估计器一致；（2）相同秩条件下，O(ε⁻²(k+log(1/δ)))个样本可实现对k维目标子空间的遗忘子空间注入；（3）在额外谱尾部条件下，Nyström++估计器可达到O(ε⁻¹)样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 随机张量列车向量在迹估计中具有实用潜力，TT秩 r ≥ d-1 是恢复维度无关理论保证的临界阈值。该工作厘清了随机TT向量在随机迹估计中的能力边界与局限性。

Abstract: Stochastic trace estimation is a standard tool for approximating the trace of a large-scale matrix available only through matrix-vector products. However, in tensor-structured settings, unstructured Gaussian or Rademacher test vectors may be prohibitively expensive to store and compute with, while cheaper rank-one tensor-product vectors can require sample complexities that grow exponentially with the tensor order. This work studies Gaussian random tensor train vectors as a structured alternative for stochastic trace estimation. We show that, with a suitable choice of the tensor train rank, random tensor train vectors recover dimension-independent guarantees for the Girard--Hutchinson estimator. In particular, a median-of-means variant with tensor train rank $r \geq d-1$ achieves the same dependence on the accuracy $\varepsilon$ and failure probability $δ$ as the classical estimator based on unstructured Gaussian vectors. We further prove an oblivious subspace injection result for sketches formed from independent Gaussian random tensor train vectors: tensor train rank $r\geq d-1$ and $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-2}(k+\log(1/δ)))$ samples suffice for a $k$-dimensional target subspace. Finally, we investigate the use of such sketches within the Nyström++ framework. We show that the resulting estimator can achieve the desired $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-1})$ sample complexity under an additional spectral-tail condition. These results provide clarififcation on both the potential and the limitations of random tensor train vectors in stochastic trace estimation.

</details>


### [102] [Spectral Adaptive Conformal Prediction for Structured Non-Exchangeable Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15950)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出谱自适应共形预测(SACP)，结合局部谱相似性加权与在线误覆盖率自适应更新，在非可交换的时间序列数据上实现有限样本覆盖保证，并通过模拟和三个美国真实数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测要求数据可交换，但许多时间序列数据具有季节性、周期性机制切换、频率变化等结构化依赖，破坏了可交换性。现有方法未能有效利用这类谱结构信息来改进预测区间的覆盖性能。

Method: 提出谱自适应共形预测(SACP)，包含两个核心组件：(1) 基于局部谱相似性的加权共形分位数——利用时间序列的谱特征选择与当前测试点相关的校准残差；(2) 在线自适应误覆盖率更新——当不确定性随时间变化时动态校正长期误覆盖率。同时给出固定谱加权分位数的近似覆盖结果和自适应更新的确定性长期校准结果，并提出有效样本量诊断来监控谱加权的可靠性。

Result: 在具有周期性机制切换和缓慢变化频率的模拟实验中，SACP相对于固定谱加权方法表现更优。三个美国真实数据实例（可能涉及经济、能源或环境等领域）进一步验证了混合方法的改进效果，同时表明谱加权需通过有效样本量诊断进行监控。

Conclusion: 谱自适应共形预测能够有效利用时间序列的谱结构信息，在非可交换设定下提供更好的预测区间覆盖。混合方法优于纯固定谱加权，但必须配合有效样本量诊断以确保谱权重在局部确实有足够信息支撑。

Abstract: Conformal prediction gives prediction intervals with finite-sample coverage when the data are exchangeable. Many time-indexed datasets are not exchangeable. They have seasons, recurring regimes, changing frequencies, or other forms of structured dependence. This paper studies a simple way to use that structure. We propose spectral adaptive conformal prediction, a method that forms weighted conformal quantiles using local spectral similarity and then updates the target miscoverage level online. The spectral weights choose calibration residuals that look relevant to the current test point. The adaptive update corrects the long-run miss rate when uncertainty changes over time. We give an approximate coverage result for the fixed spectral weighted quantile and a deterministic long-run calibration result for the adaptive update. Simulations with recurring regimes and slowly changing frequencies, together with three U.S. real-data examples, show that the hybrid method can improve on fixed spectral weighting, while also showing that spectral weighting must be monitored through effective sample size diagnostics.

</details>


### [103] [PromptShift-CRC: Drift-Aware Conformal Risk Control for Foundation Models Under Prompt and Domain Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15964)
*Jeffery Opoku,David Banahene*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出 PromptShift CRC，一种针对基础模型在提示词分布漂移下的漂移感知共形风险控制方法。通过嵌入提示与响应、度量当前提示流与校准池的距离、加权校准样本并在线更新风险水平，结合三种诊断指标，在合成与真实基准上均优于静态共形方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型部署后，提示词会因用户、话题、策略变化而快速漂移，导致固定校准集失效。传统共形预测与共形风险控制依赖校准数据与未来数据同分布假设，在分布漂移下无法保证风险控制。需一种能感知漂移、自适应调整的校准方法。

Method: （1）嵌入：对提示词和响应进行向量嵌入；（2）漂移度量：计算当前提示流与校准池之间的分布距离；（3）加权重校准：对相关或近期的校准样本赋予更高权重；（4）在线更新：观察到违规后在线调整风险水平；（5）输出三种诊断指标：已实现风险误差、提示漂移量、有效校准规模。理论分析给出方法在分布不匹配和加权分位数不确定性下控制风险的条件。

Result: 在合成提示漂移基准上，静态共形风险控制在漂移后急剧失效，而 PromptShift-CRC 在所有自适应基线中给出最佳覆盖率。在问答、毒性检测、摘要事实性、长上下文幻觉风险四个公开基准衍生流上的评估进一步验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: PromptShift CRC 通过嵌入提示度量漂移、加权校准样本和在线风险更新，有效应对基础模型部署中的提示与领域漂移问题，在理论与实验上均证明其优于静态共形方法，为动态环境下的风险控制提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Foundation models are now used in settings where the prompts they receive can change quickly. Users change, topics change, policies change, and the model may suddenly face a kind of request that was rare in the calibration data. This makes fixed calibration risky. Conformal prediction and conformal risk control give model-agnostic ways to control error, but they work best when the calibration data still look like the future data. This paper develops PromptShift CRC, a drift-aware conformal risk control method for foundation-model outputs under prompt and domain shift. The method embeds prompts and responses, measures how far the current prompt stream has moved from the calibration pool, gives more weight to relevant or recent calibration examples, and updates the risk level online after observed violations. It reports three practical diagnostics: realized risk error, prompt drift, and effective calibration size. We give conditions under which the method controls risk up to terms for distribution mismatch and weighted quantile uncertainty. In a synthetic prompt-shift benchmark, static conformal risk control fails sharply after drift, while PromptShift-CRC gives the best coverage among the adaptive baselines considered. We then evaluate the same calibration layer on public benchmark derived streams for question answering, toxicity, summarization factuality, and long-context hallucination risk

</details>


### [104] [Closing the Approximation Gap in Simulation-free Latent SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16138)
*Henry D. Smith,Brian L. Trippe,Scott W. Linderman*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: Helmholtz-SDE 是一种无模拟变分推断算法，通过优化与给定边缘分布集合相容的路径测度，克服了现有无模拟方法因参数化受限而导致的近似后验退化问题，在保持计算高效的同时，恢复动态系统的精度可媲美基于模拟的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无模拟变分推断算法虽然规避了数值模拟的计算开销，但其参数化方式将近似后验限制在可用SDE的子集内，损害了后验推断和参数学习的质量。本文旨在弥合这一缺口，在保持无模拟高效性的前提下提升推断精度。

Method: 提出 Helmholtz-SDE 算法，核心是优化与一组预设边缘分布相容的路径测度（path laws）。该算法不直接参数化漂移项，而是通过瞬时边缘分布来表征后验，并在满足边缘约束的所有SDE路径测度上进行优化，从而扩展了无模拟VI的表达能力。

Result: Helmholtz-SDE 在动态系统恢复上优于先前的无模拟方法，尤其在高后验不确定性场景下增益最大；同时达到与基于模拟的变分推断方法相当的性能，但运行时间仅为后者的一小部分。

Conclusion: Helmholtz-SDE 成功弥合了无模拟VI与基于模拟VI在近似后验表达能力上的差距，实现了精度与效率的双赢，为从噪声观测中恢复潜在动态系统提供了一种兼具理论优雅性和实用价值的新方案。

Abstract: Recovering dynamical systems from noisy observations is a recurring challenge across scientific domains, including neuroscience and physics. Latent stochastic differential equations (SDEs) address this by modeling the system as an unobserved state that evolves according to a learnable SDE and generates the observations. Variational inference (VI) provides a tractable objective for fitting latent SDEs. Traditional VI algorithms evaluate this objective by numerical simulation over a time discretization, trading fidelity for computational cost. A recent class of algorithms, simulation-free VI, sidesteps this tradeoff by parameterizing the posterior through its instantaneous marginals rather than its drift. In this work, we show that the efficiency of existing simulation-free VI algorithms comes at a price: their parameterizations restrict the approximate posterior to a subset of the SDEs available to simulation-based methods, degrading posterior inference and parameter learning. We propose Helmholtz-SDE, a simulation-free VI algorithm that closes this gap by optimizing over path laws compatible with a prescribed collection of marginals. Helmholtz-SDE recovers dynamics more faithfully than prior simulation-free methods, with the largest gains under high posterior uncertainty. It further matches the performance of simulation-based VI at a fraction of the runtime.

</details>


### [105] [Generative Modeling on Metric Graphs via Neural Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16273)
*Alessandro Micheli,Yueqi Cao,Anthea Monod,Samir Bhatt*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出首个在紧致度量图上支持连续概率分布的深度生成建模框架。该方法将图嵌入光滑环绕空间，通过神经半对偶参数化求解熵正则Kantorovich问题，再将生成的样本投影回原图。作者研究了两种嵌入几何：外在欧几里得实现和内蕴热带Abel–Jacobi嵌入。理论证明在增大神经表达力的联合极限下，学习到的生成器弱收敛到有效的传输耦合。实验表明该方法在多种几何结构不同的图上匹配或优于基于离散图OT的启发式基线，并在曼哈顿百万级Uber数据的城市场景中验证了可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统最优传输方法在图结构数据上通常依赖离散化，难以处理连续概率测度，且面临图上的测地线距离计算复杂、可扩展性差等问题。作者旨在填补“紧致度量图上连续分布深度生成建模”的空白，利用嵌入技巧将图上的传输问题转化为连续环绕空间中的可微问题，从而借助现代神经网络进行端到端学习。

Method: 核心技术路线包含三步：(1) 将度量图通过外源欧几里得坐标或内蕴热带Abel–Jacobi映射嵌入到一个光滑环绕空间（如欧几里得空间或Jacobian环面）；(2) 在嵌入空间中利用神经半对偶（neural semidual）参数化求解熵正则的Kantorovich最优传输问题；(3) 将生成样本通过最近点投影映射回原始图结构。整个生成器天然保持图支撑性。理论部分给出了该框架在神经网络表达力增强时弱收敛到真实传输耦合的证明。

Result: 在多种几何特征各异的图（如树、环、网格等合成图）上，所提方法与基于离散图OT的Sinkhorn基线、朴素的欧几里得嵌入方法相比，传输质量（如Wasserstein距离近似）相等或更优，且在大规模图上计算效率更高。在真实场景中，该方法成功训练于曼哈顿100万Uber接送点数据上，展示了处理城市级空间流数据的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了度量图上连续分布深度生成建模从无到有的突破，通过巧妙的嵌入策略将图结构最优传输问题转化为可微学习任务。两种嵌入几何各有优势：欧几里得嵌入简单直接，热带嵌入更具内蕴几何保真度。理论保证和实证可扩展性使得该方法在城市计算、网络流量建模等现实应用中具有广阔前景。

Abstract: We introduce, to our knowledge, the first deep generative modeling framework for probability distributions continuously supported on compact metric graphs. Given source and target measures on a metric graph, our method embeds the graph into a smooth ambient space, solves an entropic Kantorovich problem via a neural semidual parameterization, and projects generated samples back onto the original graph. We study two embedded geometries: an extrinsic Euclidean realization and the intrinsic tropical Abel--Jacobi embedding into the Jacobian torus. In both cases, the resulting generator is graph-supported by construction. We prove that, in the joint limit of increasing neural expressivity, the learned generator converges weakly to a valid transport coupling between the original graph measures. Empirically, across a range of geometrically distinct graphs, our method matches or improves upon heuristic transport baselines based on discrete graph OT, while scaling more favorably. Finally, we demonstrate scalability on real-world urban mobility data by training our model on one million Uber pickup locations in Manhattan, New York City.

</details>


### [106] [Diffusion Flow Matching: Dimension-Improved KL Bounds and Wasserstein Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16610)
*Marta Gentiloni Silveri,Giovanni Conforti,Alain Durmus*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文对基于布朗运动的扩散流匹配(DFM)的离散化误差提供了改进的收敛性分析，在KL散度和2-Wasserstein距离下推导出具有改进维度依赖的收敛界，在最小假设条件下达到了当前最优的标度律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFM作为一种通用生成建模框架近期受到广泛关注，但其理论收敛性质尚不完全清楚，尤其是离散化误差的分析仍有改进空间。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 在有限矩条件和温和的得分可积性假设下，针对KL散度推导收敛界；进一步在一阶得分可积性假设和弱对数凹性条件下，将分析扩展到2-Wasserstein距离，两者均获得一致的维度依赖性。

Result: 在KL散度下获得了比先前工作更优的维度依赖标度律；在2-Wasserstein距离下也获得了与KL情形一致的维度依赖收敛保证。

Conclusion: 在最小假设条件下，本文为布朗运动DFM建立了当前最优的收敛性理论，显著推进了对DFM离散化误差的理论理解。

Abstract: Diffusion Flow Matching (DFM) has recently emerged as a versatile framework for generative modeling, yet its theoretical convergence properties remain only partially understood. In this work, we provide refined and novel convergence guarantees for Brownian motion based DFMs, focusing on the discretization error. Our analysis is conducted under the Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence and the 2-Wasserstein distance. Under finite-moment conditions and a mild score integrability assumption, we derive KL convergence bounds with improved dimensional dependence compared to prior work, achieving, up to our knowledge, state-of-the-art scaling under minimal conditions. We further extend the analysis to the 2-Wasserstein distance: under an additional first-order score integrability assumption and a weak log-concavity condition, we obtain convergence guarantees with dimensional dependence consistent with the KL case.

</details>


### [107] [Attention is Just Another Name for Coupling?: A Fast-Slow ODE Perspective on Hierarchical Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16730)
*Zhengyuan Gao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将因果自注意力视为一种快变量耦合机制，并引入一个时间尺度更慢的子系统——通过对序列进行时间下采样并经由零初始化门控反馈至快速路径——形成快-慢耦合架构。该问题被框架化为奇异摄动常微分方程（ODE），其中快变量以 token 速率演化，慢变量每 P 个 token 更新一次，时间尺度比 ε=1/P 通过因果块均值池化结构性地强制实现。作者将该形式具体化为神经网络：快速路径为标准因果注意力，慢速路径为对 T/P 个池化 token 的全注意力（每层成本降低 P² 倍），以及一个零初始化加性门控。在快速动力学满足线性生成元假设下，证明了平衡流形 x=φ(y) 恰好是主方程稳态分布；训练得到的 MLP φ_θ(y) 是其变分近似。实验显示在 50 万 token 规模下，门保持关闭，耦合与冻结消融间的差异在运行间噪声范围内，壁钟成本与稠密基线相当。本文的核心贡献在于精确的、有差距标记的映射本身，而非性能增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果自注意力仅在单一时间尺度上运作，作者探索是否可以通过引入一个时间上更慢的第二耦合子系统来补充快速路径——慢速路径在时间下采样的序列视图上运行，并通过零初始化门控反馈回主干网络，从而为序列建模提供不同时间尺度的归纳偏置。

Method: 本文采用奇异摄动 ODE 框架形式化快-慢耦合：快变量 x 按 token 步进，慢变量 y 每 P 步更新一次（ε=1/P），通过因果块均值池化实现。具体网络结构中，快速路径为标准因果自注意力（T 个 token），慢速路径对 T/P 个池化 token 做全注意力（成本为 O((T/P)²)，每层便宜 P² 倍），二者通过零初始化加性门控融合。理论部分在快速动力学为线性生成元的假设下，分析平衡流形与主方程稳态分布的等价性。

Result: 理论上证明了在线性生成元假设下，平衡流形 x=φ(y) 精确等于主方程稳态分布 p_st(y)，训练的 MLP φ_θ(y) 为该分布的变分近似（但训练后的实际网络并非生成元，该恒等式仅为结构化极限）。实验上，在 50 万 token 的训练规模下，耦合表现为中性——门控保持关闭，耦合模型与冻结消融的性能差异在运行间噪声范围内，且壁钟计算成本与稠密基线相当。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献不在于性能提升，而在于提供了从奇异摄动 ODE 到快-慢耦合注意力架构的精确、有差距标记的形式化映射。该映射为理解多时间尺度注意力机制提供了理论框架，尽管在当前实验规模下耦合效应呈中性，但该理论连接本身具有独立价值。

Abstract: Causal self-attention is a coupling mechanism: each token's hidden state is updated by a learned mixture of preceding tokens at the same timescale. This paper asks whether a second, temporally slower coupling-a slow sub-system operating on a temporally-downsampled view of the sequence and fed back into the fast path through a zero-initialised gate-complements it. The question is framed in the language of singularly perturbed ordinary differential equations (ODEs), where the fast variable $x$ evolves at the token rate, the slow variable $y$ evolves at one update per $P$ tokens, and the timescale ratio $\varepsilon = 1/P$ is enforced structurally by causal block-mean pooling.
  The paper instantiates the fast-slow ODE formalism as a concrete neural network: a fast path of standard causal attention over $T$ tokens, a slow path of full attention over $T/P$ pooled tokens ($P^2 \times$ cheaper per layer), and a zero-initialised additive gate. In addition, under a linear-generator assumption on the fast dynamics, we prove that the equilibrium manifold $x = φ(y)$ is exactly the master-equation (ME) stationary distribution $p_{\mathrm{st}}(y)$; in that regime a learned MLP $φ_θ(y)$ is a variational approximation of it (the trained block is not a generator, so this identity is the structured limit, not a claim about the network as trained). Empirically, at $500$k tokens the coupling is neutral -- the gate stays closed and the coupled and frozen ablations are within run-to-run noise -- at a wall-clock cost comparable to a dense baseline. The contribution is the precise, gap-marked mapping itself, not a performance gain.

</details>


### [108] [A nonparametric two-sample test using a parametric integral probability metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16941)
*Yuha Park,Yongdai Kim*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的非参数双样本检验统计量 PReLU-IPM，基于单节点神经网络参数化判别器类的积分概率度量，并建立了检验过程 PReLU-TST 的理论保证，在模拟和真实数据上展现出优越的检验功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双样本检验是统计和机器学习中的基础问题，现有非参数方法在有限样本下的检验功效有待提升。作者希望通过设计一种结构简单但表达能力强的参数化判别器类（单节点神经网络），构建兼具理论保证和实际功效的新型检验统计量。

Method: 提出基于积分概率度量（IPM）的新统计量 PReLU-IPM，其判别器类由带参数化线性整流单元（PReLU）激活的单节点神经网络构成，参数通过优化确定。基于此构建双样本检验流程 PReLU-TST，并证明该检验在正则条件下具有一致性，且渐近等价于基于 IPM 的非参数检验。

Result: 在多个模拟和真实基准数据集上，PReLU-TST 在多种备择假设下比竞争方法表现出更高或相当的检验功效，验证了其在有限样本下的优势。

Conclusion: PReLU-TST 是一种理论上有保障、实践上高效的非参数双样本检验方法，单节点神经网络判别器的设计在简洁性与检验功效之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Detecting distributional differences between two independent samples is a fundamental problem in statistics and machine learning. Nonparametric two-sample testing provides a principled framework for determining whether two samples are drawn from the same underlying distribution, without assuming any specific parametric form for the distribution. In this study, we propose a new two-sample test statistic based on a newly introduced integral probability metric (IPM), using a specially designed parametric discriminator class with a single node of a neural network. We show that the resulting test statistic, called PReLU-IPM, is nonparametric and establish theoretical guarantees for the associated two-sample testing procedure, PReLU-TST, including its consistency and asymptotical equivalence to nonparametric IPM-based tests under regularity conditions. By analyzing multiple simulated and real benchmark datasets, we demonstrate that PReLU-TST achieves higher power across a range of alternatives or performs comparably to its competitors, for finite samples.

</details>


### [109] [Sobolev Approximation by Fixed-Size Neural Networks with Arbitrary Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16975)
*Baicheng Li,Haizhao Yang,Shijun Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用固定规模神经网络实现任意精度Sobolev逼近的新型激活函数。作者提出了初等通用激活函数（EUAF）和可微通用激活函数族（DUAFₙ），并证明固定规模的网络可在W^{s-1,∞}范数下以任意精度逼近W^{s,∞}中的函数，同时给出了显式的宽度与深度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络通用逼近理论大多关注的是扩大网络规模（宽度或深度）来实现任意精度。本文的研究动机在于探索：能否在固定网络规模的条件下，通过设计特定的激活函数来实现任意精度的Sobolev空间逼近？这为资源受限场景下的网络设计提供了理论依据。

Method: 1. 构造初等通用激活函数（EUAF），证明固定规模的EUAF网络可在W^{1,∞}范数下任意精度逼近W^{2,∞}((a,b)ᵈ)中的任意函数；2. 引入可微通用激活函数族DUAFₙ及其光滑版本DUAF∞，将逼近结果推广至高阶Sobolev空间W^{s,∞}，逼近误差在W^{s-1,∞}范数下度量；3. 构造Sigmoid型变体DUAF̃ₙ，证明对任意1≤s≤n，固定规模的该类网络仍能实现对W^{s,∞}函数的任意精度逼近；4. 所有结果均显式计算网络的宽度与深度界，且所提激活函数均为初等函数。

Result: 1. EUAF激活的固定规模网络可在W^{1,∞}范数下任意精度逼近W^{2,∞}函数；2. DUAF∞激活的固定规模网络可将逼近推广至W^{s,∞}空间，逼近精度在W^{s-1,∞}范数下度量；3. Sigmoid型变体DUAF̃ₙ同样具备上述逼近能力，且适用于1≤s≤n的范围；4. 所有构造中网络的宽度与深度均有显式表达式，激活函数均为初等函数。

Conclusion: 本文证明了通过精心设计激活函数（EUAF、DUAF∞及其Sigmoid变体），固定规模的神经网络即可实现对Sobolev空间中光滑函数的任意精度逼近。这一结果突破了传统依赖扩展网络规模的逼近范式，为固定资源下的深度网络设计提供了新的理论工具。所提激活函数均为初等函数且结构显式，具有实际可部署性。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate new activation functions for achieving arbitrary-accuracy Sobolev approximation by fixed-size neural networks. We first show that any function in $W^{2,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ can be approximated with arbitrary accuracy, measured in the $W^{1,\infty}$-norm, by a fixed-size neural network using the Elementary Universal Activation Function ($\mathrm{EUAF}$). To extend this result to $W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ for $s\in\mathbb{N}$, we introduce a smooth activation $\mathrm{DUAF}_{\infty}$ from the family of Differentiable Universal Activation Functions ($\mathrm{DUAF}_n$). We prove that any function in $W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ can be approximated with arbitrary accuracy in the $W^{s-1,\infty}$-norm by a fixed-size $\mathrm{DUAF}_{\infty}$-activated network. We further construct sigmoidal variants $\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$ and show that, for every $1\leq s\leq n$, fixed-size $\widetilde{\mathrm{DUAF}}_n$-activated networks still approximate any $f\in W^{s,\infty}((a,b)^d)$ with arbitrary accuracy in the $W^{s-1,\infty}$-norm. In all these results, the width and depth bounds are computed explicitly, and the proposed activations are elementary.

</details>


### [110] [Dynestyx: A Probabilistic Programming Library for Dynamical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16985)
*Daniel Waxman,Dmitry Batenkov,John Feser,Andy Zane,Eli Bingham,Youssef Marzouk,Matthew E. Levine*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: dynestyx 是一个概率编程库，为状态空间模型提供一等支持，通过统一接口实现离散/连续时间动态系统的先验指定、混合效应数据推断以及具有原则性不确定性量化的状态与参数估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管状态空间模型在统计、信号处理和机器学习中具有重要理论与应用价值，但现代概率编程语言一直难以有效整合动态系统，导致前沿方法对从业者不够易用，并阻碍了"贝叶斯工作流"的顺畅实施。

Method: 作者开发了dynestyx库，提供对状态空间模型的一等支持，将状态估计与参数估计的最先进方法整合进统一接口，支持离散时间和连续时间动态系统的任意先验指定，并能处理混合效应数据。

Result: dynestyx使得用户能够通过单一统一接口，为离散或连续时间动态系统指定任意先验，对混合效应数据执行推断，并获得具有原则性不确定性量化的状态和参数估计。

Conclusion: dynestyx成功填补了概率编程语言在动态系统建模方面的空白，大幅降低了SSM贝叶斯分析的门槛，使前沿方法更易为从业者所用，有望推动贝叶斯工作流在动态系统领域的广泛应用。

Abstract: State-space models (SSMs) are the standard formalism for Bayesian treatment of dynamical systems, with natural applications in statistics, signal processing, and machine learning. Despite their importance in both theory and application, dynamical systems have proven difficult to incorporate in modern probabilistic programming languages (PPLs), making state-of-the-art methods less accessible to practitioners and introducing friction in following the "Bayesian workflow." We introduce dynestyx, a probabilistic programming library with first-class support for SSMs, including state-of-the-art methods in the estimation of both states and parameters. Through a single, unified interface, users may specify arbitrary priors for discrete-time or continuous-time dynamical systems, perform inference over mixed-effect data, and make state and parameter estimates with principled uncertainty quantification.

</details>
